# Agent storage and integration research

Snapshot: 2026-10-06. Purpose: facts needed to build `recall` adapters, i.e. (1) detect which AI coding/chat agents are installed, (2) import their local conversation history into one SQLite index, (3) integrate back into each agent (MCP server registration, memory auto-loaded at session start). 27 agent surfaces were researched against primary sources (vendor docs, vendor source on GitHub, changelogs) plus read-only, structure-only checks of one macOS workstation.

## Summary table

| Agent | History path (macOS) | Format | Readable locally? | MCP config path | Global instructions file | Skills (SKILL.md) | Hooks incl. session-start? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Claude Code | `~/.claude/projects/<slug>/<session-uuid>.jsonl` | JSONL, append-only; internal schema; deleted after 30 days by default | yes (plaintext) | `~/.claude.json` (user/local), `<project>/.mcp.json` | `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/rules/*.md`; AGENTS.md fallback | yes: `~/.claude/skills`, `.claude/skills`, plugins | yes: SessionStart and UserPromptSubmit inject (stdout or `additionalContext`); SessionEnd cannot |
| Claude desktop app | claude.ai chat: server-side only. Code tab and Cowork: Claude Code JSONL (`~/.claude/projects`; Cowork under `local-agent-mode-sessions/.../local_<uuid>/.claude/`) plus sidecars in `~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude-code-sessions/` | JSONL + small JSON sidecars | Code tab and local Cowork yes; chat no | `~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude_desktop_config.json` (+ `~/.claude.json` for Code tab) | Code tab `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`; chat/Cowork instructions are account-side | yes: Code tab `~/.claude/skills`; Cowork account skills | Code tab: Claude Code hooks inject; Cowork: plugin hooks only; chat: none |

Legend: `3p` = documented only by third-party parsers; `n/a` = not applicable. "Readable locally?" is about encryption and server-side storage; it was checked on a live install only where one was present, and is otherwise taken from vendor source or docs. Sources, tags and caveats are in "How to read this document" below the key findings; per-agent evidence is in the sections.

## Key findings for adapter building

1. **Easy to import today (plaintext, local, incremental-friendly):** Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Pi, Copilot CLI, Factory Droid, Kiro CLI, opencode, Goose, Crush, Cline, Kilo Code, Continue. Medium effort: Cursor (SQLite KV plus JSONL), VS Code Copilot (mutation-log replay), Zed (zstd JSON in SQLite), Warp (protobuf, macOS TCC), Aider (per-repo scan), Devin Local and current Antigravity (SQLite, third-party-documented), Amp (server-side, login). Impossible or export-only: ChatGPT Classic `.data` (encrypted), Windsurf Cascade and Antigravity v1 `.pb` (encrypted; the Cascade agent no longer exists), claude.ai and ChatGPT web chats (use the official export ZIPs, whose shapes are documented below).
2. **Hooks that can inject memory at session start:** Claude Code, Codex (needs one-time trust), Gemini CLI (JSON only), Copilot CLI, VS Code Local harness, Kiro, Factory Droid, Devin Local, and, via plugins/extensions, opencode, Kilo Code, Pi, Amp (per prompt) and Cline (VS Code extension). Prompt-submit injection: Claude Code, Codex, Gemini (`BeforeAgent`), VS Code, Kiro, Droid, Devin Local, Amp, Pi, Cline (VS Code only); NOT Cursor (`beforeSubmitPrompt`), Copilot CLI (output dropped) or Goose. No usable hook at all: Zed, Warp, Aider, Crush (tool-level only), Continue, Roo Code, Windsurf Cascade, T3 Code, ChatGPT/Claude chat modes. For those, fall back to an always-loaded instructions file plus the MCP server (see section 2 of the cross-cutting findings).
3. **One skill covers most agents:** a single `~/.agents/skills/recall/SKILL.md` is read natively by Codex, Cursor, Copilot (VS Code and CLI), Gemini CLI, opencode, Pi, Goose, Amp, Droid, Zed, Warp, Crush, Cline, Roo, Kilo and Devin Local; Claude Code, Kiro, Antigravity and Continue need their own directories (symlinks, as the `skills` installer does).
4. **MCP registration is four different shapes**: JSON `mcpServers` (Claude Code, Cursor, Gemini, Antigravity, Devin, Copilot, Cline, Roo, Pi, Kiro, Droid, Warp, Claude desktop), TOML `[mcp_servers.x]` (Codex), `mcp` with `command: [array]` (opencode, Kilo, Crush variant), and YAML/other (Goose `extensions:`, Continue, Zed `context_servers`, Amp `amp.mcpServers`, VS Code `servers`). Aider has no MCP client.
5. **Dedupe is mandatory:** the Codex desktop app imports Claude Code and Cursor sessions as Codex threads (ledger `~/.codex/external_agent_session_imports.json`), Claude desktop sessions are Claude Code transcripts (`entrypoint: claude-desktop`), Cline imports other agents' sessions, and several agents keep legacy plus new stores side by side. Key on `(agent, native session id)` and honour the import ledgers.
6. **Retention can delete history before import:** Claude Code and Gemini CLI default to 30 days; Warp keeps 200 conversations; Amp keeps nothing locally any more. Run imports often and never mirror deletions.
7. **Do not index recall's own injected context:** hosts persist hook output in transcripts (for example Claude Code `attachment` lines, Codex developer messages). Read only user/assistant text and drop host-tagged synthetic turns.
8. **The landscape moved in 2026:** Codex merged into the ChatGPT desktop app (2026-07-09); Windsurf became Devin Desktop (2026-06-02, Cascade removed 2026-09-08); Gemini CLI is being replaced by the Antigravity CLI `agy` (2026-06-18); Roo Code was shut down (2026-05-15) and Continue froze; Kilo Code was rebuilt on OpenCode; Amp stopped local thread storage; Cline moved to an SDK store; opencode has two live schema families. Format detection must be version-aware and tolerant of unknown record types.
9. **Reading live stores:** use `mode=ro` (not `immutable=1`) for WAL databases, never open read-write, tolerate truncated JSONL tails, and re-parse files that shrink (details in the cross-cutting findings).

## How to read this document

- **Citation tags** end every claim: `[doc: URL]` vendor documentation; `[src: owner/repo@<sha>:path#Lstart-Lend]` the agent's own source (commit-pinned); `[observed]` confirmed by inspecting directory names, file types, SQLite schemas and JSON key names only; `[3p: ...]` rests only on a third-party parser or write-up (cass / franken_agent_detection, SpecStory, agent-sessions, ccusage, community posts); `[inferred]` reasoning without a direct source. "Unknown" / "not found" is stated explicitly rather than guessed.
- The research machine ran macOS on Apple silicon; Linux and Windows paths are given where documented.
- **Privacy**: no conversation text, prompts, titles, project names, account ids or secrets were read into this document. Paths use `~`; ids are placeholders. Recorded structure is limited to file names, directory layout, SQLite DDL, JSON key names and value types.
- The agent ecosystem moves fast: several vendors renamed repos/domains in 2026 (sst/opencode is anomalyco/opencode; badlogic/pi-mono is earendil-works/pi; block/goose is aaif-goose/goose; docs.windsurf.com redirects to docs.devin.ai/desktop; docs.factory.ai moved to docs.factory.com). Re-verify before relying on any path older than a few months.

# Cross-cutting findings for adapter design

Everything below is derived from the per-agent sections that follow (cited there); new external facts carry their own citation. "Snippets" in this document use `recall serve` as the stdio MCP entrypoint of the recall CLI (as in `recall --help`); substitute the real command if it changes.

## 1. Import difficulty tiers

- **Easy (plaintext, local, documented or stable enough, incremental-friendly):** Claude Code (JSONL; internal schema, 30-day default retention), Codex (JSONL rollouts plus SQLite index), Gemini CLI (JSONL log with `$set`/`$rewindTo` records since v0.39.0; 30-day default retention), Pi (JSONL v3 entry tree), GitHub Copilot CLI (`events.jsonl` + `workspace.yaml`), Factory Droid (JSONL; only line 1 is vendor-confirmed), Kiro CLI (JSONL; layout is third-party-only and changed about four times in a year), opencode (one SQLite file with two live table families, v1 `session/message/part` and v2 `session_v2/session_message`), Goose (SQLite), Crush (per-project SQLite, discovered through `~/.local/share/crush/projects.json`), Cline (SDK sessions under `~/.cline/data`, legacy task dirs), Kilo Code (now an OpenCode fork: `~/.local/share/kilo/kilo.db`), Continue (JSON per session; upstream frozen), Roo Code (legacy JSON task dirs; project discontinued 2026-05-15).
- **Medium (needs decoding, indirection, scanning, or a login):** Cursor (JSONL transcripts plus a ~12 GB SQLite KV store; `agentKv` blobs are opaque; cloud-agent chats exist only as cached title/body in `conversation-search.db`), VS Code Copilot Chat (append-log JSONL to replay, workspace-hash to folder mapping), Zed (SQLite with zstd-compressed JSON; ACP agents keep their own transcripts), Warp (SQLite plus protobuf blobs; on macOS the App Group container is TCC-protected; only the newest 200 conversations are kept), Aider (Markdown per repo, no ids, no per-message timestamps, no global index so it needs a project scan), Antigravity current builds (per-conversation SQLite with protobuf plus a plaintext JSONL transcript, both third-party-documented), Devin Local / Windsurf successor (SQLite message forest, third-party-documented, 17 migrations in 2026), T3 Code (read the wrapped agents' stores instead; its own DB is metadata), Amp (current threads are server-side only since 2026-03-31: needs `amp threads export` with a login), Claude desktop Code tab and Cowork (their transcripts are Claude Code JSONL; see the Claude desktop section).
- **Hard or impossible locally:** legacy Antigravity v1 `conversations/*.pb` and Windsurf Cascade `cascade/*.pb` (measured here at 8.0 bits/byte entropy, i.e. encrypted; Cascade itself was removed 2026-09-08), claude.ai and ChatGPT web chats (server-side; official exports only), ChatGPT Classic native `conversations-v3-*/*.data` (high-entropy encrypted; key reportedly held in an OpenAI-team-signed Keychain access group; no official or community decryptor found), Cursor and Warp cloud agents (server-side).
- **Export-file importers:** ChatGPT data export and Claude.ai data export (see their sections for the JSON shapes).

## 2. Hooks that can load memory at session start or prompt submit

"Injects" means the hook's output is added to the model's context (verified in each agent's section). Plain-stdout agents accept `print(text)`; JSON-only agents reject or ignore plain text.

| Agent | Session start | Prompt submit | Session end | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Claude Code | `SessionStart` injects (plain stdout or `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext`, 10,000-char cap; also fires on `compact`) | `UserPromptSubmit` injects | `SessionEnd` no injection, 1.5 s default budget | `~/.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings(.local).json`, plugin `hooks/hooks.json`; also `mcp_tool` handler type |
| Codex | `SessionStart` injects (plain stdout or `additionalContext` as developer context; about 2,500-token cap per message) | `UserPromptSubmit` injects | `SessionEnd` advisory | `~/.codex/hooks.json` or `[hooks]` in `config.toml`; each non-managed hook needs a one-time `/hooks` trust review |
| Cursor | `sessionStart` returns `additional_context`, but documented fire-and-forget and reported dropped (race) | `beforeSubmitPrompt` cannot inject (only `continue`/`user_message`) | `sessionEnd` observe-only | `~/.cursor/hooks.json`; JSON only; also imports Claude Code hooks when its third-party toggle is on  |
| Gemini CLI | `SessionStart` injects (JSON `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext`) | `BeforeAgent` injects; `AfterTool` injects | `SessionEnd` advisory | `~/.gemini/settings.json` `hooks`; stdout must be only JSON |
| VS Code (Local harness, preview) | `SessionStart` injects `additionalContext` | `UserPromptSubmit` injects | none | `.github/hooks/*.json`, `~/.copilot/hooks/*.json`; Claude-format files only with `chat.useClaudeHooks` (default off); matcher ignored |
| GitHub Copilot CLI | `sessionStart` injects `additionalContext` | `userPromptSubmitted` output dropped for config hooks (docs and changelog disagree) | `sessionEnd` no effect | `.github/hooks/*.json`, `~/.copilot/hooks/*.json`, inline `hooks` in settings files |
| Kiro (IDE 1.x, CLI V3) | `SessionStart` stdout becomes context | `UserPromptSubmit` stdout becomes context | `SessionEnd` (CLI V3) | `~/.kiro/hooks/<id>.json`; CLI 2.x used `agentSpawn`/`userPromptSubmit` inside custom agents |
| Factory Droid | `SessionStart` injects (stdout or `additionalContext`) | `UserPromptSubmit` injects | `SessionEnd` no injection | `~/.factory/hooks.json`; Claude-compatible event names and payloads |
| Devin Local (Windsurf successor) | `SessionStart` injects `additionalContext` | `UserPromptSubmit` injects | `SessionEnd` | `~/.config/devin/config.json` `hooks`, `.devin/hooks.v1.json`; reads Claude-format files by default. Legacy Cascade hooks: none inject |
| Antigravity | none (no session-start event) | `PreInvocation` `injectSteps` runs before every model call (gate on `invocationNum == 1` for once-per-session) | none | `~/.gemini/config/hooks.json`, `.agents/hooks.json` |
| Cline | task-start hook injects only in the VS Code extension; CLI/Desktop need a plugin `beforeRun` | `UserPromptSubmit` injects only in the VS Code extension | `SessionShutdown` | file hooks in `~/.cline/hooks/`, `~/Documents/Cline/Hooks/`; `Pre/PostToolUse` inject everywhere |
| opencode | plugin only: v1 `experimental.chat.system.transform` / `chat.message`; v2 `ctx.session.hook("context")` | v2 `ctx.session.hook("prompt")` | none | no shell hooks; v1 and v2 plugins are incompatible |
| Kilo Code | plugin `experimental.chat.system.transform` (OpenCode lineage) | plugin `chat.message` | none | `~/.config/kilo/plugin/` |
| Pi | extension `session_start` + `pi.sendMessage` | `before_agent_start` / `input` / `context` | `session_shutdown` | TypeScript extensions in `~/.pi/agent/extensions/` |
| Amp | `session.start` cannot inject | plugin `agent.start` return `{message}` injects | none | `~/.config/amp/plugins/*.ts` |
| Crush | none | none | none | only `PreToolUse` (its `context` is appended to a tool result) |
| Goose | none (SessionStart banner is shown to the user only) | none | `SessionEnd` | plugin hooks cannot inject; per-turn text via `GOOSE_MOIM_MESSAGE_FILE` env |
| Continue | none (CLI hook engine exists but is never invoked) | none | none | |
| Zed, Warp, Aider, Roo Code, T3 Code | none | none | none | use instructions files / MCP / skills instead |
| Claude desktop app | Code tab: runs Claude Code hooks from `~/.claude/settings.json` (SessionStart injects); Cowork: plugin `hooks/hooks.json` only; chat: none | Code tab: `UserPromptSubmit` injects | Code tab: `SessionEnd` | Code-tab transcripts are Claude Code JSONL |
| ChatGPT desktop app (unified with Codex) | Codex/Work view: Codex `SessionStart` hook injects; Chat view and ChatGPT Classic: none | Codex/Work view: `UserPromptSubmit` injects | Codex/Work view: `SessionEnd` advisory | `~/.codex/hooks.json`; Chat mode is server-side |

## 3. Hook portability and double-firing

- Claude-Code-shaped hook files are read by more than Claude Code: Cursor (maps `SessionStart/SessionEnd/UserPromptSubmit/Stop/SubagentStop/PreCompact/PreToolUse/PostToolUse`; setting on by default) [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/reference/third-party-hooks.md], VS Code (opt-in `chat.useClaudeHooks`), Copilot CLI (inline hooks in `.claude/settings(.local).json`), Devin Local (default), Continue CLI (dormant). Codex, Factory, Kiro and Gemini use their own files but near-identical event names and payloads. A recall hook registered for Claude Code can therefore also fire in Cursor/Devin, and its output contract may differ there (Cursor `UserPromptSubmit` cannot inject; Cursor and Gemini want JSON only).
- Output contract matrix for one hook script: plain stdout is accepted by Claude Code, Codex, Kiro, Factory; JSON `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` by Claude Code, Codex, Factory, Gemini, VS Code, Devin; top-level `additionalContext` by Copilot CLI; `additional_context` by Cursor; `injectSteps` by Antigravity. Safest design: one thin per-agent shim (`recall hook --agent <name>`), detecting the host from stdin fields or env (Cursor `cursor_version`/`CURSOR_VERSION`; Gemini `GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR`; Codex `CODEX_THREAD_ID`/`turn_id`; Factory `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR`; Claude `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`), rather than one dual-format blob.
- Some hosts gate hooks behind trust: Codex requires a one-time `/hooks` review (hash-based, any edit re-flags it), Gemini requires a trusted folder for project hooks, Cursor/Claude require workspace trust for project hooks. User-level files avoid project trust but not Codex's review.

## 4. MCP registration

| Agent | Config file(s) | Shape | Register via |
|---|---|---|---|
| Claude Code | `~/.claude.json` (user/local), `.mcp.json` (project) | `mcpServers`, `type` optional for stdio | `claude mcp add --scope user` / `add-json` |
| Codex | `~/.codex/config.toml`, `.codex/config.toml` | TOML `[mcp_servers.<name>]` | `codex mcp add <name> -- <cmd>` |
| Cursor | `~/.cursor/mcp.json`, `.cursor/mcp.json` | `mcpServers`, `${env:VAR}` | edit the file (no `agent mcp add`) |
| Gemini CLI | `~/.gemini/settings.json`, `.gemini/settings.json` | `mcpServers` | `gemini mcp add --scope user` |
| Antigravity | `~/.gemini/config/mcp_config.json` (legacy `~/.gemini/antigravity/mcp_config.json`), `.agents/mcp_config.json` | `mcpServers` (remote `serverUrl`) | edit / UI |
| Devin Local / Windsurf | `~/.config/devin/mcp_config.json` (legacy `~/.codeium/windsurf/mcp_config.json`) | `mcpServers` | `devin mcp add -s user` |
| VS Code Copilot | `~/.copilot/mcp-config.json`, `.mcp.json` (portable); `.vscode/mcp.json`, User `mcp.json` (`servers`) | `mcpServers` or `servers` | `code --add-mcp` |
| Copilot CLI | `~/.copilot/mcp-config.json`, `.mcp.json`, `.github/mcp.json` | `mcpServers`, `type:"local"` | `copilot mcp add` |
| opencode | `~/.config/opencode/opencode.json(c)` | `mcp`: `{type:"local", command:[...]}` (v2 native `mcp.servers`) | edit; v2 `opencode mcp add` |
| Kilo Code | `~/.config/kilo/kilo.json(c)` | `mcp` (OpenCode shape) | `kilo mcp add` |
| Crush | `~/.config/crush/crush.json` | `mcp`: `{type:"stdio"}` | edit (crushrc DSL `mcp add`) |
| Pi | `~/.pi/agent/mcp.json` | `mcpServers` | `pi mcp add` |
| Cline | `~/.cline/data/settings/cline_mcp_settings.json` (legacy VS Code globalStorage) | `mcpServers` | `cline mcp` wizard / edit |
| Roo Code | `<globalStorage>/rooveterinaryinc.roo-cline/settings/mcp_settings.json`, `.roo/mcp.json` | `mcpServers` | edit |
| Continue | `~/.continue/mcpServers/*.yaml`, `config.yaml` | YAML `mcpServers:` list | edit |
| Zed | `~/.config/zed/settings.json`, `.zed/settings.json` | `context_servers` | edit (JSON with comments) |
| Goose | `~/.config/goose/config.yaml` | `extensions:` map (`type: stdio`, `cmd`, `args`, `envs`) | edit / `goose configure` |
| Warp | `~/.warp/.mcp.json` (also reads `~/.claude.json`, `~/.codex/config.toml`, `~/.agents/.mcp.json`) | `mcpServers` | edit / UI |
| Kiro | `~/.kiro/settings/mcp.json` | `mcpServers` | `kiro-cli mcp add --scope global` |
| Amp | `~/.config/amp/settings.json` | `amp.mcpServers` | `amp mcp add` |
| Factory Droid | `~/.factory/mcp.json` | `mcpServers` | `droid mcp add` |
| Aider | none (no MCP client upstream) | | |
| T3 Code | wrapped agent's own config | | |
| Claude desktop | `~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude_desktop_config.json` (chat + Code tab); Code tab also reads `~/.claude.json`, `.mcp.json`; `.mcpb` extensions in `Claude Extensions/` | `mcpServers` | edit the file or ship a `.mcpb` |
| ChatGPT desktop | Codex/Work view: `~/.codex/config.toml`; Chat view accepts remote MCP only (Settings > Apps, developer mode) | TOML `[mcp_servers.<name>]` | `codex mcp add` |

## 5. Instructions, skills and plugin packaging

- **Skills.** The Agent Skills format (`SKILL.md` with `name` and `description`) is the common denominator; `.agents/skills/` (project) and `~/.agents/skills/` (user) are the documented cross-client convention [doc: https://agentskills.io/client-implementation/adding-skills-support]. Vendor-documented native readers of `~/.agents/skills`: Codex, Cursor, VS Code Copilot, Copilot CLI, Gemini CLI, opencode, Pi, Goose, Amp, Factory Droid, Zed (Zed Agent only), Warp, Crush, Cline, Roo Code, Kilo Code, Devin Local. Not reading it: Claude Code (reads `~/.claude/skills`), Kiro (`~/.kiro/skills` only), Antigravity (documents `~/.gemini/config/skills`), Continue (`~/.continue/skills`), Aider (no skills). A common setup makes `~/.agents/skills` the canonical store, with symlinks from agents' skill dirs (see Appendix B), so one `~/.agents/skills/recall/SKILL.md` reaches most agents with no per-agent work.
- **AGENTS.md** is the closest thing to a universal global/project instructions file: read by Codex, opencode, Pi, Goose, Amp, Droid, Warp, Zed, Kilo, Cline, Crush, Kiro (as steering), Copilot (VS Code and CLI), Cursor (project), Antigravity (project and `~/.gemini/AGENTS.md`), Devin Local, Continue, and since v2.1.277 Claude Code (only when no CLAUDE.md exists) [doc: https://agents.md lists adopters; per-agent sections cite each]. Gemini CLI needs `context.fileName` to add it; Aider needs `read:`. Global files differ per agent (see the summary table).
- **Agent Plugins** (open standard 1.0.0: `plugin.json` + `skills/` + `mcp.json`, reverse-domain client extension namespaces; steering committee from Amazon, Cursor, Microsoft, OpenAI and Vercel) [doc: https://agent-plugins.org] is implemented in whole or part by VS Code (opt-in `chat.plugins.enabled`), Copilot CLI, Cursor (the root-`plugin.json` form), Kiro Powers and Cline (`~/.agents/plugins`) per their sections. Codex (`.codex-plugin/plugin.json`, also reads `.claude-plugin`), Factory (`.factory-plugin`, translates Claude layouts), Antigravity, Devin Local and Goose ship overlapping formats (skills + MCP + hooks directories), while Claude Code (`.claude-plugin/plugin.json` + marketplaces) and Gemini CLI (`gemini-extension.json`) keep their own. A recall plugin bundling MCP + skill + hook is feasible for Claude Code, Codex, Copilot CLI, Factory, Cursor, Antigravity, Gemini (extension) and Devin; the hook part stays agent-specific.

## 6. Duplication and feedback-loop hazards (affects dedupe design)

- The same conversation can live in several stores: Claude desktop Code-tab and Cowork sessions are written as Claude Code transcripts (`entrypoint: claude-desktop`) [observed]; the Codex desktop app imports Claude Code and Cursor sessions as ordinary Codex threads and records them in `~/.codex/external_agent_session_imports.json` (76 records here) [observed]; Cursor can import Claude Code conversations into its own store (setting off by default) [3p]; Cline imports Claude Code/Codex/OpenCode sessions (`metadata.importedFrom`) [src]; Kilo Code and opencode keep legacy JSON `storage/` next to the SQLite DB, Kilo may also hold Roo-style `tasks/` dirs; Gemini CLI can leave a legacy `.json` beside a resumed `.jsonl`; opencode v2 copies v1 rows into new tables inside the same DB; the unified ChatGPT desktop app's local Work threads are Codex rollouts (`originator = codex_work_desktop`); T3 Code, VS Code Agent Host (Copilot/Claude/Codex harnesses), Zed (ACP) and Warp host other agents whose transcripts stay in those agents' own stores.
- Suggested identity: `(source_agent, native_session_id)` plus an `origin` flag; skip import-ledger entries (Codex ledger, Cline `importedFrom`), and prefer the native store over a host wrapper's copy.
- **recall's own injected context is persisted by hosts**: Claude Code stores hook output as `attachment` lines (`hook_additional_context`, `hook_success` with `stdout`) [observed]; Cline tags injected hook context `metadata.displayRole:"system"`; Codex injects it as developer context; VS Code writes a `hook` response part; Copilot CLI logs `hook.start/end`. An importer that indexed those would re-ingest recall's own memory digest. Keep adapters on user/assistant text only and drop host-tagged synthetic turns (Codex role-user messages that start with `# AGENTS.md instructions`, `<environment_context>`, `<skill>`, etc.).

## 7. Retention, rewrites and locking

- **Deleted by the agent:** Claude Code transcripts after `cleanupPeriodDays` (default 30; `history.jsonl` and auto-memory kept) [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/claude-directory#cleaned-up-automatically]; Gemini CLI sessions after 30 days by default [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/session-management/#session-retention]; Warp keeps only the newest 200 conversations; VS Code's session index keeps 400 non-external entries; Aider users delete `.aider.*` files freely; Amp current threads live only on the server. Import early and never mirror deletions.
- **Rewritten in place (not append-only):** Zed (whole row per save), Warp (full task snapshot per write), Goose (edit/fork deletes later messages; compaction rewrites), Cline `*.messages.json`, Continue session JSON, Roo/Cline legacy task JSON (atomic writes), Pi on migration and some branch operations, VS Code `.jsonl` (snapshot rewrite when more than 512 entries accumulate), Copilot CLI (recovery of torn tails), Gemini CLI (rare tmp+rename), Claude Code (`.superseded-*` / `.orphaned-*` copies), opencode v2 (rewrites the assistant row while streaming), Crush (updates `parts` in place while streaming). Track `(size, mtime, hash of head)` and re-parse on shrink or prefix change.
- **Append-only:** Claude Code, Codex rollouts, Aider markdown, Droid, Kiro CLI JSONL, Antigravity transcripts, Pi (except above). Keep a byte offset or last `ordinal`/id.
- **SQLite:** prefer `file:<path>?mode=ro` for live WAL databases; `immutable=1` skips locking and WAL, so it can miss the newest rows or return inconsistent results if the file changes [doc: https://www.sqlite.org/uri.html#uriimmutable]. A read-only connection can read a WAL database when the `-wal`/`-shm` files exist and are readable or the directory is writable [doc: https://www.sqlite.org/wal.html#readonly]. opencode's DB here had a 511 KB `-wal` that `immutable=1` would hide. Some apps migrate or write on open (Goose), so never open read-write. Very large stores (Cursor `state.vscdb` about 12 GB; Codex `thread_history_1.sqlite` 418 MB, a derived projection) should be read by key prefix or index table (`composerHeaders`, `state_5.sqlite` `threads`), never copied.

## Claude Code (Anthropic CLI)


Observed layout: a CLI on PATH from an npm global package or the native installer (`~/.local/share/claude/versions/`); state lives in `~/.claude/`, `~/.claude.json` and `~/.mcp.json` [observed].
**Version verified:** docs fetched 2026-10-06 from code.claude.com; CLI 2.1.287 [observed].

### 1. Detection
- Binary `claude`. Install methods: native installer symlink `~/.local/bin/claude` -> `~/.local/share/claude/versions/<ver>`; Homebrew casks `claude-code` / `claude-code@latest`; npm `@anthropic-ai/claude-code` (ships the same native binary); WinGet `Anthropic.ClaudeCode` [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/setup]. With an npm global install, the PATH entry can point at `@anthropic-ai/claude-code/bin/claude.exe` [observed]. Probe order for an importer: `command -v claude`, `~/.local/bin/claude`, existence of `~/.claude/projects`.
- Data dir `~/.claude` (Windows `%USERPROFILE%\.claude`) plus global state file `~/.claude.json` [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings]. Env overrides: `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` (relocates "all settings, session history, and plugins"; ignored in project/local settings) [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/env-vars]; `CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME` (with CONFIG_DIR, fixes the `projects/<name>` dir; v2.1.234+); `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_CACHE_DIR` (plugins root); `CLAUDE_CODE_TMPDIR` (temp/scratchpad root) [doc: env-vars]. Whether `.claude.json` moves into `$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` is not stated in the docs [inferred: historically it does; verify before relying on it].
- `claude auth status` prints JSON that includes a `configDirectory` field naming the effective config dir [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/cli-reference].
- Claude Desktop's Code tab and Cowork also write Claude Code transcripts that Claude Code treats specially (kept at any age unless `desktopSessionCleanupPeriodDays`) [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/claude-directory#cleaned-up-automatically]; see the Claude desktop section for where they live.

### 2. History location
- `~/.claude/projects/<project>/<session-id>.jsonl`; `<project>` = working dir with every non-alphanumeric char replaced by `-`; names over 200 chars are truncated to 200 plus a hash of the full path [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/sessions#where-transcripts-are-stored]. Same layout on Linux; Windows uses `%USERPROFILE%\.claude\projects` (docs write `~/.claude` for all platforms) [doc: settings].
- Siblings: `projects/<project>/<session>/subagents/` (subagent transcripts), `<session>/tool-results/` (spilled large tool output), `<session>.jsonl.superseded-<ts>` and `<session>.orphaned-<ts>-<suffix>.jsonl` (set-aside older copies, not in the session picker) [doc: claude-directory#cleaned-up-automatically].
- Prompt history `~/.claude/history.jsonl`: per line `display`, `pastedContents`, `timestamp`, `project`, `sessionId` [observed]; "every prompt you've typed", kept until deleted [doc: claude-directory#kept-until-you-delete-them].
- Live-session registry `~/.claude/sessions/*` (one small file per running session; keys `peerToken`, `procStart`, `pidDomain` [observed]); used to detect concurrent sessions and crashes, removed on exit [doc: claude-directory].
- Auto memory (Claude's own notes) `~/.claude/projects/<project>/memory/MEMORY.md` + topic files with frontmatter `type: user|feedback|project|reference` and `modified` (v2.1.214+); shared across worktrees of one git repo; `autoMemoryDirectory` setting relocates it [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/memory#auto-memory]. Treat as a second import source and as overlapping functionality with recall.
- RETENTION: transcripts are deleted after `cleanupPeriodDays` (default 30, minimum 1); `history.jsonl` and `memory/` are exempt [doc: claude-directory#cleaned-up-automatically]. An importer should run early and may want to advise raising `cleanupPeriodDays`.

### 3. Storage format
History formats already verified by the lead (not re-researched): JSONL line types `user` / `assistant` / `custom-title` / `summary`; fields `cwd`, `gitBranch`, `timestamp`, `isSidechain`, `isMeta`, `message.content[]` blocks.
| Concept | Field / path |
|---|---|
| session id | file name `<session-id>.jsonl` (UUID); `sessionId` in `history.jsonl` and hook payloads (`session_id`) [doc: hooks#common-input-fields] |
| project / cwd | `cwd` on entries; `<project>` slug in the directory name (lossy) |
| title | latest `custom-title` line; `session_title` is also passed to SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit hooks when set [doc: hooks#sessionstart-input] |
| sub-agent sidechains | `isSidechain`, plus `subagents/` dir |
- Line types observed in recent transcripts (CLI 2.1.28x, key/type names only) [observed]: `user`, `assistant`, `attachment`, `system`, `queue-operation`, `last-prompt`, `custom-title`, `agent-name`, `file-history-snapshot`, `file-history-delta`, `cost-state`, `mode`, `bridge-session`, `atis-latch`, `relocated`, `pr-link`, `history-suppression`, `frame-link`, `artifact-comment-monitor`, `artifact-autoreact-ledger`; no `summary` lines appeared in these recent files (older/compacted sessions still have them per the lead). An importer should whitelist `user`/`assistant` (+ `custom-title.customTitle`, `last-prompt.lastPrompt`, `agent-name.agentName`) and skip the rest. `system.subtype` seen: `stop_hook_summary`, `compact_boundary`, `informational`, `model_refusal_fallback`.
  - Content blocks seen: assistant `text` / `thinking` / `tool_use`; user `text` / `tool_result` / `image`, or a plain string. 21 of ~1,740 `user` lines carry `isMeta: true` (synthetic/system-reminder turns; skip them); sidechain (subagent) lines carry `isSidechain`. Other top-level keys useful for provenance: `uuid`, `parentUuid` (tree), `sessionId`, `timestamp`, `cwd`, `gitBranch`, `version`, `entrypoint`, `slug`, `requestId`, `promptId`, `permissionMode`, `userType` [observed].
  - The `entrypoint` key identifies the launcher: observed values include `claude-desktop` (Claude desktop app Code tab), `sdk-py` and `sdk-cli` [observed]. So `~/.claude/projects` also holds Desktop-app and Agent-SDK sessions, and an importer can label provenance with `entrypoint` (a plain terminal run would show a CLI value; not present in this sample). `turnOrigin` values seen: `human`, `task_notification`, `sdk`, `system`, `peer`, `scheduled`; `promptSource`: `sdk`, `system` [observed]; filter on `turnOrigin == "human"` to separate typed prompts from scheduled/SDK/notification turns.
  - Hook-injected context is NOT stored as user text: it is recorded as `attachment` lines with `attachment.type` = `hook_additional_context` (keys `content`, `hookEvent`, `hookName`, `toolUseID`) or `hook_success` (keys `command`, `content`, `durationMs`, `exitCode`, `hookEvent`, `hookName`, `stderr`, `stdout`, `toolUseID`); other `attachment.type` values seen include `instructions`, `session_context`, `skill_listing`, `queued_command`, `environment`, `date`, `deferred_tools_delta`, `mcp_instructions_delta`. So recall's own SessionStart output will not be re-indexed as chat text as long as only `user`/`assistant` lines are read, but it IS persisted verbatim in the transcript attachments [observed].
- Format stability: the docs say the entry format "is internal ... and changes between versions"; stable alternatives are `/export` (rendered text), `claude -p --output-format json|stream-json`, and the `transcript_path` hook field [doc: sessions#where-transcripts-are-stored].
- Incremental import: append-only per-session files; the transcript is written asynchronously and "may lag the in-memory conversation", so do not read it from a hook for the latest turn; use `last_assistant_message` on Stop/SubagentStop [doc: hooks#common-input-fields]. Use size/mtime plus byte-offset checkpoints; treat `.superseded-*` / `.orphaned-*` files as non-canonical.

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
Plaintext at rest, no encryption; OS file permissions only [doc: claude-directory#plaintext-storage]. Writes can be suppressed (`CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_PROMPT_HISTORY=1`, or `--no-session-persistence` with `-p`), so some sessions will never exist on disk. `claude purge [path]` deletes a project's state. Official export: `/export` (clipboard or plain-text file) [doc: sessions#export-and-locate-session-data].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP**: user and local scope live in `~/.claude.json` (user = top-level `mcpServers`; local = `projects["<abs path>"].mcpServers`); project scope = `<project>/.mcp.json` (needs interactive approval, loaded without a prompt under `-p`); plugins ship `.mcp.json`. Precedence local > project > user > plugin > claude.ai connectors [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/mcp#mcp-installation-scopes]. Minimal stdio entry (entries without `type` are read as stdio; `url` entries need `"type": "http"|"sse"|"ws"`):
  `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{}}}}` [doc: mcp#from-an-mcpservers-json-block]. CLI: `claude mcp add --scope user --transport stdio recall -- recall serve`, `claude mcp add-json <name> '<json>'`, `claude mcp list|get|remove`. `${VAR}` / `${VAR:-default}` expand in command/args/env/url/headers; `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` is set in the server's env [doc: mcp#environment-variable-expansion-in-mcpjson]. Locally the user scope has 2 stdio-style entries with keys `type,command,args,env`, and `~/.mcp.json` has one remote entry [observed].
- **Global instructions**: `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`; project `./CLAUDE.md` or `./.claude/CLAUDE.md`; `./CLAUDE.local.md`; `.claude/rules/*.md` (frontmatter `paths`); managed `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/CLAUDE.md` (macOS); `@path` imports up to 4 hops; files up to 4 MiB load in full. `AGENTS.md` is read only when no CLAUDE.md/CLAUDE.local.md exists in or above cwd (v2.1.277+) [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/memory#claude-md-files, #agents-md].
- **Skills**: `~/.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md`, `.claude/skills/`, nested dirs, `--add-dir` dirs, plugin `skills/`; follows the Agent Skills standard (agentskills.io) with extra frontmatter (`allowed-tools`, `paths`, `hooks`, `context`, `model`, ...); `~/.claude/skills/synced/` is reserved for claude.ai-synced skills; Cowork/cloud sessions do not read `~/.claude/skills` [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills#where-skills-live]. 88 entries locally in `~/.claude/skills` [observed].
- **Slash commands**: `~/.claude/commands/*.md` and `.claude/commands/*.md` still work but "have been merged into skills" (same `/name`) [doc: skills].
- **Hooks** [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks]:
  - Events (current): SessionStart, Setup, UserPromptSubmit, UserPromptExpansion, PreToolUse, PermissionRequest, PermissionDenied, PostToolUse, PostToolUseFailure, PostToolBatch, Notification, MessageDisplay, SubagentStart, SubagentStop, TaskCreated, TaskCompleted, Stop, StopFailure, TeammateIdle, InstructionsLoaded, ConfigChange, CwdChanged, DirectoryAdded, FileChanged, WorktreeCreate, WorktreeRemove, PreCompact, PostCompact, PreModelSwitch, PostModelSwitch, Elicitation, ElicitationResult, SessionEnd [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks#hook-lifecycle].
  - Config: JSON `hooks` in `~/.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.local.json`, managed settings, plugin `hooks/hooks.json` (needs a top-level `"hooks"` wrapper), skill/subagent frontmatter. Shape: `{"hooks":{"<Event>":[{"matcher":"<m>","hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"...","timeout":30}]}]}}`. Handler types: `command`, `http`, `mcp_tool` (calls a tool on a configured MCP server; `server`, `tool`, `input`), `prompt`, `agent`. Matchers: exact names or `a|b`, else JS regex. Same events fire in terminal, IDE extensions and the Desktop app [doc: hooks#hook-locations].
  - stdin common fields: `session_id`, `prompt_id`, `transcript_path`, `cwd`, `permission_mode`, `hook_event_name` (+ `agent_id`/`agent_type` in subagents) [doc: hooks#common-input-fields].
  - **SessionStart**: matcher = `startup|resume|clear|compact|fork`; input adds `source`, optional `model`, `agent_type`, `session_title`; only `command` and `mcp_tool` handlers; plain stdout IS added to Claude's context, or return `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` (also `initialUserMessage`, `sessionTitle`, `watchPaths`, `reloadSkills`); hooks run in the background at interactive start but Claude's first response waits for them [doc: hooks#sessionstart].
  - **UserPromptSubmit**: input adds `prompt`; plain stdout or `additionalContext` is injected "alongside the submitted prompt" as a system reminder; can block with `decision:"block"`; default timeout 30 s, and on timeout the output (including context) is discarded; also fires for scheduled-task and subagent-report turns [doc: hooks#userpromptsubmit].
  - **SessionEnd**: input adds `reason` (`clear|resume|logout|prompt_input_exit|other`); NO decision control and output is discarded; default budget 1.5 s (raise via per-hook `timeout` up to 60 s or `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS`); it is an archiving hook (read `transcript_path`) [doc: hooks#sessionend]. Fires on `/clear` and interactive `/resume` switches too.
  - Injection cap: each of `additionalContext` / `systemMessage` / `initialUserMessage` and plain stdout is capped at 10,000 chars (excess is spilled to a file with a 2,000-char preview) [doc: hooks#json-output]. Stdout must be only the JSON object when using JSON.
  - Stop/SubagentStop/PostToolUse/PreCompact etc. can also feed `additionalContext` or block; `PreCompact`/`PostCompact` bracket compaction (SessionStart fires again with `source:"compact"`) [doc: hooks#add-context-for-claude, hooks#sessionstart].
- **Plugins**: manifest `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`; components at plugin root: `skills/`, `commands/`, `agents/`, `hooks/hooks.json`, `.mcp.json`, `output-styles/`, `workflows/`, `monitors/`, `bin/` (put on PATH), `settings.json`; `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` path variable; distribution via marketplaces: `claude plugin marketplace add <source>`, `claude plugin install <plugin>@<marketplace>`, `--plugin-dir` for dev; settings keys `enabledPlugins`, `extraKnownMarketplaces`; "mods" are plugin-registered in-process function hooks [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins-reference, https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/cli-reference]. A single plugin can therefore ship recall's MCP server + SessionStart hook + skill. 8 plugins enabled locally; no hooks defined in user settings [observed].
- **Headless**: `claude -p "<prompt>"` with `--output-format text|json|stream-json`, `--input-format text|stream-json`, `--bare` (skips hooks, MCP, CLAUDE.md, auto memory, plugins; recommended for scripts), `--no-session-persistence`, `--resume/-r <id|name|path>`, `--continue/-c`, `--fork-session`, `--session-id <uuid>`, `--mcp-config`, `--strict-mcp-config`, `--settings`, `--plugin-dir`, `--append-system-prompt[-file]`, `--include-hook-events`; `--init`/`--init-only`/`--maintenance` run Setup hooks. Without `--bare`, `-p` still runs project hooks and `.mcp.json` servers without a trust prompt [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/headless, https://code.claude.com/docs/en/cli-reference]. Agent SDK exists for TypeScript/Python.

**Best recall injection point:** SessionStart command hook (matcher `startup|resume|clear|compact`) printing a bounded (<10k chars) memory digest to stdout, plus optional UserPromptSubmit for per-prompt recall; a SessionStart `mcp_tool` hook can call recall's own MCP tool directly. Register the server with `claude mcp add --scope user` or ship everything as one plugin; fallback is an `@import` line in `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`. Because SessionStart also fires on `compact`, memory is re-injected after compaction.
**Confidence / unknowns:** high (live docs 2026-10-06 plus local 2.1.287); unknown: whether `~/.claude.json` relocates under `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, and the exact transcript entry schema across versions (docs declare it unstable).

---

## Claude desktop app, macOS (Anthropic; closed source Electron app)


Bundled Claude Code binaries sit at `~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude-code/<ver>/<hash>/claude.app/Contents/MacOS/claude` [observed].
**Version verified:** app 2.19675.1 [observed]; docs read 2026-10-06: Claude Code on desktop [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/desktop], local data layout [doc: https://claude.com/docs/third-party/claude-desktop/data-storage], Cowork pages [doc: https://support.claude.com/en/articles/15520349-use-claude-cowork-on-web-desktop-and-mobile].

### 1. Detection
- Bundle `/Applications/Claude.app`, id `com.anthropic.claudefordesktop`; user data `~/Library/Application Support/Claude` (Windows `%APPDATA%\Claude`, MSIX `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Packages\Claude_pzs8sxrjxfjjc\LocalCache\Roaming\Claude` or `AnthropicPBC.Claude_fnn82j28hfe8t`; Linux `~/.config/Claude` [3p: kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/cowork_paths.go#L8-L24]). The third-party-inference variant uses `~/Library/Application Support/Claude-3p` [doc: data-storage page]. `CLAUDE_USER_DATA_DIR` / `--user-data-dir` relocate the profile [observed] (app.asar constants). Code-tab transcripts live in Claude Code's own store (default `~/.claude`; `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` handling by the Desktop-spawned CLI: [inferred], not verified).
- An extra app profile (a second user-data dir, launched with `--user-data-dir` or `CLAUDE_USER_DATA_DIR`) uses the same app binary with its own Chromium profile (cookies, IndexedDB), `claude-code-sessions/` and `local-agent-mode-sessions/` sidecars; its Code transcripts still land in the shared `~/.claude/projects` [observed]. The app itself scans sibling `Claude*` dirs under Application Support that contain `claude-code-sessions` or `local-agent-mode-sessions` as import sources, so glob `Claude*` [observed] (app.asar constants).
- `<accountId>/<orgId>` are the two directory levels (app builds `userData/<baseDir>/<accountId>/<orgId>`) [observed] (app.asar constants); agentsview calls them org/workspace [3p: kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/cowork.go#L23-L40].

### 2. History location
| Surface | Where | Local? |
|---|---|---|
| claude.ai Chat (desktop, web, mobile) | server-side; local caches only: `IndexedDB/https_claude.ai_0.indexeddb.*` (LevelDB 1.6 MB + 9 blob files; names seen in it: `react-query-cache`, `keyval-store`, `blink-idb-value-wrapper`, `chat-draft`, `composer-draft-images`, `epitaxy-draft`; the substrings `chat_messages` and `current_leaf_message_uuid` occur 6 times each, so a few conversation payloads are cached), `Local Storage/leveldb` [observed] | no (partial V8-serialized cache) |
| Code tab, local/SSH | transcripts in Claude Code's store `~/.claude/projects/`; sidecar dir holds working folder, settings, title [doc: data-storage page]; 41/41 sidecars with a `cliSessionId` resolve to `~/.claude/projects/<enc>/<cliSessionId>.jsonl` [observed] | yes |
| Cowork, local | `local-agent-mode-sessions/<acct>/<org>/local_<uuid>.json` + dir `local_<uuid>/` {`.claude/` (private Claude config dir with `projects/<enc>/<cliSessionId>.jsonl`, `projects/<enc>/<cliSessionId>/subagents/agent-*.jsonl`, `todos`, `tasks`, `debug`), `outputs/`, `uploads/`, `audit.jsonl` (HMAC-chained)} [doc: data-storage page], [observed] (7/7 resolved) | yes |
| Dispatch agent | `<acct>/<org>/agent/local_ditto_<uuid>.json` + dir (same layout), `agent/memory/` (empty here) [observed] | yes |
| Cowork cloud, Code cloud | server; from 2026-10-06 new Pro/Max Cowork tasks run in the cloud and "Only on your computer" is removed; existing local tasks stay local and offer a transcript download; full-history download exists, format undocumented [doc: 15520349 above] | no |
- Other: the Code sidecar org dir also has `scheduled-tasks.json`, `backlog/tasks.json` and `deleted_<uuid>` files (tombstones [inferred]); the Cowork org dir has `cowork_settings.json`, `cowork_plugins/`, `rpm/plugin_<id>/manifest.json`, `.claude.json`; the Cowork root has sibling `skills-plugin/<id>/<id>/{manifest.json,.claude-plugin/plugin.json,skills/<name>/SKILL.md}` [observed]. Not history: `bridge-state.json` (remote-control bridge: id -> `enabled, userConsented, environmentId, localSessionId, remoteSessionId, processedMessageUuids[], pendingProcessedAcks[]`), `git-worktrees.json`, `git-shadow/<hash>` shadow git dirs (checkpointing; purpose [inferred]), `vm_bundles/claudevm.bundle/` (Cowork VM image), `claude-code-vm/<ver>/`, `pending-uploads/` [observed], [doc: data-storage page] (`vm_bundles`, `claude-code-vm`). User-visible outputs go to `~/Claude/` (legacy `~/Documents/Claude/`) [doc: data-storage page]. Scratch Code sessions use cwd `~/Library/Application Support/Claude/scratch-workspaces/<acct>/<org>/scratch-<date>-<hex6>` [observed].

### 3. Storage format
- Transcripts are standard Claude Code JSONL (same uuid/parentUuid DAG, `message.usage`, event types) so the Claude Code parser applies; Cowork host-loop project dir is `-...-local-<uuid>-outputs`, VM sessions `-sessions-<name>`, so locate by `<cliSessionId>.jsonl` rather than rebuilding the name [3p: kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/cowork.go#L23-L40; jazzyalex/agent-sessions@6fa9a73:docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-19-claude-cowork-labeling.md#L7]. Origin tag in every JSONL line: `entrypoint` = `claude-desktop` (Code tab) or `local-agent` (Cowork, Dispatch) [observed]. Line types seen: `user, assistant, system, attachment, queue-operation, last-prompt, mode, ai-title, custom-title, file-history-snapshot, ...`.
| Concept [observed; 3p parsers agree] | Code tab | Cowork local |
|---|---|---|
| session id | sidecar `sessionId` = `local_<uuid>`; JSONL id = `cliSessionId` | same |
| title | `title`, `titleSource`; JSONL `ai-title.aiTitle`, `custom-title` | `title`; JSONL `ai-title` |
| project / cwd | `cwd`, `originCwd` (repo when cwd is a worktree), `gitAnchors[]` | `cwd`, `userSelectedFolders[]`; JSONL cwd `/sessions/<name>` (VM) or `.../local_<uuid>/outputs` |
| created / updated | `createdAt`, `lastActivityAt` (epoch ms); JSONL `timestamp` | same |
| text / tools | JSONL `message.content[]`, `tool_use`, `tool_result`, `toolUseResult` | same |
| other | `model`, `isArchived`, `permissionMode`, `effort`, `completedTurns`, `cliSessionId` (absent when `transcriptUnavailable`), `remoteMcpServersConfig`, `enabledMcpTools` | `model`, `isArchived`, `initialMessage`, `slashCommands[]`, `memoryEnabled`, `skillsEnabled`, `pluginsEnabled`, `systemPrompt` (~40 KB), PII `accountName`, `emailAddress` (drop) |
Sidecar keys observed [observed] (key shapes only; counts when not on every file): Code (45 files): `sessionId, cwd, originCwd, createdAt, lastActivityAt, model, isArchived, title, permissionMode, alwaysAllowedReasons[], sessionPermissionUpdates[{type,rules[{toolName,ruleContent}],behavior,destination}]`, `remoteMcpServersConfig[{uuid,name,url,tools[]}]` (42), `cliSessionId` (41), `effort, titleSource, chromePermissionMode, spawnSeed, completedTurns, titleTurn, lastAssistantUuid, latestUserFrameAt, promptAppendSnapshot{append,cliVersion,settingsKey}, toolSurfaceSnapshot, postTurnSummary{status_category,status_detail,needs_action,summarizes_uuid}, bridgeSessionIds[], lastFocusedAt, enabledMcpTools{<tool>:bool}` (36-41), `gitAnchors[{gitRoot,commonDir,trustedAt}]` (25), `writtenBranches[], publishedArtifacts[{url,title,sourcePath,updatedAt}], remoteControlSpawn{...}, transcriptUnavailable` (4), `planPath, cuAllowedApps[]`. Cowork (7 files): `sessionId, processName, vmProcessName, cliSessionId, cwd, userSelectedFolders[], createdAt, lastActivityAt, model, isArchived, title, initialMessage, slashCommands[], systemPrompt, accountName, emailAddress, remoteMcpServersConfig` (6), `fsDetectedFiles[{hostPath,fileName,timestamp}], hostLoopMode, webFetchAllowedUrls[], enabledMcpTools, egressAllowedDomains[], orgCliExecPolicies, memoryEnabled, skillsEnabled, pluginsEnabled, memoryGuidelinesTemplate, mcqAnswers, isAgentCompleted, isStarred, coworkSyspromptMap, error`; Dispatch adds `sessionType`. Third-party readers use `sessionId, cliSessionId, title, userSelectedFolders, createdAt, lastActivityAt` (agentsview) and `title, cwd, model, cliSessionId, sessionId, permissionMode, lastActivityAt, createdAt` (cass) [3p: kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/cowork.go#L42-L49; Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/claude_code.rs#L183-L230].
- Format changes: JSONL `ai-title` events replace older titles; transcripts are kept at any age since Claude Code 2.1.248 unless `desktopSessionCleanupPeriodDays` is set (older versions swept them after `cleanupPeriodDays`) [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/claude-directory#cleaned-up-automatically]; culled bodies leave a sidecar only [3p: cass claude_code.rs#L183-L230].
- Incremental: JSONL append-only; sidecars are rewritten (title renames touch only the sidecar), so key on max(transcript mtime, sidecar mtime) and `lastActivityAt` [3p: agentsview cowork.go#L190-L205]; skip `local_*` dirs when walking the root and `deleted_*` tombstones; prefix ids (`cowork:`) to avoid colliding with CLI sessions. OpenAI's unified app keeps import-state files for external agents (`~/.codex/external_agent_session_imports.json` records `source_path, content_sha256, imported_thread_id, imported_at, source_modified_at, connector_names, title`; `~/.codex/claude-cowork-import-history.json` has `accountSetupItems[{accountOrgDir,importedAtMs,itemType}]`) [observed], which suggests it imports Claude Code/Cowork transcripts [inferred] and gives a usable content-hash dedupe pattern.
- Prior art: cass scans `~/.claude/projects` plus both Desktop roots and treats `local_*.json` as metadata-only sidecars (author `claude_code_desktop`) [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/claude_code.rs#L59-L70, #L157-L166]; agentsview has a Cowork provider that reuses its Claude parser and overlays sidecar title/timestamps (ids `cowork:<id>`) [3p: agentsview cowork.go#L21, #L192]; agent-sessions walks `local-agent-mode-sessions/**/local_*/.claude/projects/**/*.jsonl` and tags `originSource: local-agent-mode` vs `claude-desktop` [3p: jazzyalex/agent-sessions@6fa9a73:docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-19-claude-cowork-labeling.md#L7]; SpecStory CLI has no Desktop/Cowork provider [3p: specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a:specstory-cli/pkg/providers].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Conversations, settings and sidecars are not encrypted (owner-only permissions; FileVault is the protection); OAuth tokens (`oauth:tokenCache`, `oauth:tokenCacheV2` keys in `config.json`) are stored encrypted with the OS secure storage, so never read them [doc: data-storage page for the 3P variant], [observed] (key names), [inferred] for 1P.
- claude.ai chat history, cloud Cowork and cloud Code sessions: server-side, exportable only via Settings > Privacy (see export section; excludes Code and Cowork local) or the Compliance API. [doc: https://support.claude.com/en/articles/9450526-export-your-claude-data]
- LOCAL-ONLY importer: can import Code-tab sessions (already handled by the Claude Code importer; add sidecar title and `isArchived` join) and local Cowork/Dispatch transcripts; cannot get chat or cloud sessions. Export importer needs the ZIP (previous section). [observed] [inferred]

### 5. Integration surfaces
- MCP: `~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude_desktop_config.json` (`mcpServers`, stdio) is loaded into Desktop chat and local Code sessions; the standalone CLI does not read it (`claude mcp add-from-claude-desktop` copies) [doc: https://modelcontextprotocol.io/docs/develop/connect-local-servers; https://code.claude.com/docs/en/desktop]. Here `mcpServers` is absent (file has `preferences`, `coworkUserFilesPath`) [observed]. Snippet: `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{}}}}`. Code tab also reads `~/.claude.json` and `.mcp.json` [doc: desktop].
- Extensions (.mcpb): unpacked to `Claude Extensions/<id>/manifest.json`, registry `extensions-installations.json`, per-extension `Claude Extensions Settings/<id>.json` (`isEnabled`, `userConfig`) [observed]; minimal manifest `{manifest_version:"0.3", name, version, description, author{name}, server{type:"node", entry_point, mcp_config{command,args}}}` [src: modelcontextprotocol/mcpb@70fe3b3:MANIFEST.md#L10-L36]; install by double-click, drag-and-drop, or Settings > Extensions > Advanced settings > Install Extension [doc: https://claude.com/docs/connectors/building/mcpb.md]. Remote connectors are account-level (Customize > Connectors) and reached from Anthropic's side, so a localhost server needs an MCP tunnel [doc: https://claude.com/docs/connectors/custom/add-unlisted.md].
- Global instructions: Code tab `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`, project `CLAUDE.md`/`CLAUDE.local.md` [doc: desktop]. Chat and cloud Cowork: "Instructions for Claude" in Settings > General in the new experience (earlier: Global instructions under Settings > Cowork), account-side [doc: https://support.claude.com/en/articles/13345190-get-started-with-claude-cowork]. Local Cowork: the Global instructions editor writes a `CLAUDE.md` under `local-agent-mode-sessions/.../memory/` (documented for the 3P variant) [doc: data-storage page]; `agent/memory/` is empty here [observed]. Projects and memory are server-side; local Cowork tasks do not use memory [doc: 11817273].
- Skills: local Code sessions load `~/.claude/skills/` plus account skills; Cowork sources skills, plugins and connectors from the account (Customize), mirrored at `local-agent-mode-sessions/skills-plugin/.../skills/<name>/SKILL.md` [doc: desktop], [observed].
- Hooks: the Code tab runs hooks defined in settings ("Hooks and skills defined in settings apply to both") [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/desktop], i.e. `~/.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.local.json`, plugin `hooks/hooks.json`, skill/subagent frontmatter [doc: https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks]. Events: SessionStart, Setup, InstructionsLoaded, UserPromptSubmit, UserPromptExpansion, MessageDisplay, PreToolUse, PermissionRequest, PostToolUse, PostToolUseFailure, PostToolBatch, PermissionDenied, SubagentStart, SubagentStop, TaskCreated, TaskCompleted, TeammateIdle, ConfigChange, PreCompact, PostCompact, PreModelSwitch, PostModelSwitch, Stop, SessionEnd. `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` reaches the model as a system reminder: SessionStart/SubagentStart at conversation start, UserPromptSubmit/UserPromptExpansion beside the prompt, tool events beside the result, Stop/SubagentStop at turn end; each string is capped at 10,000 characters [doc: same hooks page]. Cloud sessions do not read local settings [doc: same]. Plugin hooks load in Cowork and Claude Code and are ignored in chat [doc: https://claude.com/docs/plugins/platform-support.md]. The Cowork per-session `.claude/` has no `settings.json` here, so user-level hooks are probably not read there [observed], [inferred].
- Plugins: Customize > Plugins (account-scoped, synced into Claude Code at next session start), "Upload plugin" zip or GitHub marketplace; skills, commands, agents, hooks, MCP load in Cowork [doc: platform-support]. Slash commands: plugin `commands/*.md` (`/plugin:command` in Cowork); Cowork sidecar lists `slashCommands[]`; custom `~/.claude/commands` in the Code tab: not documented [observed].
- Headless: Desktop is interactive only (no `--output-format`); `claude --desktop` hands a session to Desktop [doc: desktop]. The bundled CLI above is a normal `claude` binary [inferred].

**Best recall injection point:** Code tab: SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit hooks in `~/.claude/settings.json` plus MCP in `~/.claude.json` (same as Claude Code). Cowork and chat have no user hook surface: ship a plugin (hooks + skill + local MCP) or a `.mcpb` extension, and register MCP in `claude_desktop_config.json` for chat.
**Confidence / unknowns:** high on layout (docs, app code, 3 parsers, local counts agree); unknown: Cowork full-history download format, whether `claude_desktop_config.json` servers reach Cowork, custom commands in Code tab, Windows/Linux paths beyond the 3p list.

---

## Codex (OpenAI / `openai/codex`: Codex CLI + Codex desktop app)


`~/.codex/` holds config.toml, AGENTS.md, skills/, plugins/, memories/, sessions/, archived_sessions/, sqlite/, state_5.sqlite, thread_history_1.sqlite and session_index.jsonl [observed]. The CLI ships inside the desktop app bundle.
**Version verified:** `openai/codex@822e58c` (HEAD 2026-10-06 03:58Z; latest stable `rust-v0.160.1` 2026-10-05; installed 0.160.0) [src: openai/codex@822e58c]; docs read 2026-10-06 at learn.chatgpt.com/docs (developers.openai.com/codex/* 308-redirects there; pages undated) [doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks]

### 1. Detection
- **App:** `/Applications/ChatGPT.app` is the Codex desktop app (CFBundleIdentifier `com.openai.codex`; no separate Codex.app). Bundled CLI: `Contents/Resources/codex-cli/bin/codex` (sh wrapper to `CodexCLI.app/Contents/MacOS/codex`, id `com.openai.codex.cli`; `codex-package.json` version 0.160.0); `codex app` launches the desktop app; `codex --version`, `codex doctor --json` and `codex features list` probe version, health and effective flags [observed: --help].
- **Standalone CLI:** `curl -fsSL https://chatgpt.com/codex/install.sh | sh` puts `codex` + `codex-code-mode-host` in `${CODEX_INSTALL_DIR:-~/.local/bin}` with payload in `$CODEX_HOME/packages/standalone`; also `npm i -g @openai/codex` (bin `codex`) and `brew install --cask codex` [src: openai/codex@822e58c:README.md; doc: https://chatgpt.com/codex/install.sh].
- **Data dir:** `$CODEX_HOME`, else `~/.codex`; CODEX_HOME must already exist and be a directory, else startup errors [src: openai/codex@822e58c:codex-rs/utils/home-dir/src/lib.rs#L13-L50]. Windows is `%USERPROFILE%\.codex` [inferred: `dirs::home_dir()`].
- **Env overrides:** `CODEX_HOME`; `CODEX_SQLITE_HOME` (config `sqlite_home`) relocates the `*.sqlite` state DBs (rollouts stay under CODEX_HOME [inferred]) [src: codex-rs/state/src/lib.rs#L133; doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/config-file/config-reference]; `CODEX_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `CODEX_CA_CERTIFICATE`/`SSL_CERT_FILE` [src: codex-rs/login/src/lib.rs#L47-L54; codex-rs/http-client/README.md#L11]. Tool subprocesses receive `CODEX_THREAD_ID`, `CODEX_SESSION_ID`, `CODEX_VERSION`, `CODEX_PERMISSION_PROFILE` (a recall CLI/MCP child can learn the live thread id) [src: codex-rs/protocol/src/shell_environment.rs#L6-L7; codex-rs/core/src/exec_env.rs#L14-L18].
- **Config layers (low to high):** `/etc/codex/config.toml`, cloud-managed bundle, user `config.toml`, `$CODEX_HOME/<name>.config.toml` (`-p <name>`), project `.codex/config.toml` (trusted projects only), `-c k=v`, then legacy `managed_config.toml` (file/MDM); `requirements.toml` adds admin constraints; `--ignore-user-config`, `--strict-config` [src: codex-rs/config/src/loader/README.md; doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/config-file/config-reference].
- **Desktop vs CLI:** one shared engine and one `~/.codex`; desktop threads are `threads.source='vscode'` (originator `Codex Desktop`/`codex_work_desktop`), `codex exec` is `source='exec'`/`codex_exec`; `SessionSource` default is VSCode [observed; src: codex-rs/protocol/src/protocol.rs#L2805-L2816]. Desktop-only: `~/.codex/sqlite/codex-dev.db` (tables `automations`, `automation_runs`, `inbox_items`, `local_thread_catalog*`, `thread_timeline_ledger`; not in the open-source repo), `[desktop]` config table, `.codex-global-state.json`, Electron profile `~/Library/Application Support/Codex` (Chromium caches, no history) [observed]. Cursor also hosts the `openai.chatgpt` Codex extension here [observed].

### 2. History location
- Already verified by lead: rollouts `~/.codex/sessions/YYYY/MM/DD/rollout-<ts>-<uuid>.jsonl` and desktop `~/.codex/sqlite/codex-dev.db` `local_thread_catalog` [observed: verified by the lead].
- Cold rollouts become `rollout-*.jsonl.zst`: the Codex rollout compression worker (`codex-rs/rollout/src/compression.rs`, PR #25089 and follow-ups; `local_thread_store_compression`, default off, or the app-server `rollout/compress` RPC) zstd-compresses (level 3, content size recorded in the frame header) any rollout untouched for 7 days, in `sessions/` as well as `archived_sessions/`, keeps its modified time, and removes the plain file. Resuming the thread decompresses it back to `.jsonl`. Readers prefer the plain file when both exist [src; observed: 220 of 256 rollouts here, all with ctime in one minute on 2026-10-14, each at least 7.4 days after its mtime].
- NEW: `archived_sessions/` (flat, same names; `threads.archived=1`); the open-source index is `state_5.sqlite` table `threads` (~210 rows; every `rollout_path` resolves on disk) [observed; src: codex-rs/state/migrations/0001_threads.sql]. Same layout on Linux/Windows under `$CODEX_HOME` [src: home-dir lib.rs].

### 3. Storage format
| Concept | Field / column |
|---|---|
| session id | rollout line 1 `session_meta.payload.id` (+ `session_id` = root thread; `forked_from_id`, `parent_thread_id`); filename suffix; `threads.id` [observed; src: protocol.rs#L3102-L3142] |
| title | `threads.title` / `threads.name`; `session_index.jsonl` `{id, thread_name, updated_at}`; `local_thread_catalog.display_title` (desktop) [observed] |
| project / cwd | `session_meta.payload.cwd` (+ `runtime_workspace_roots[]`, `git`), `threads.cwd`, `git_branch`, `git_origin_url`; worktree threads have cwd under `~/.codex/worktrees/<hash>/` (5 here) [observed] |
| created / updated | `session_meta.payload.timestamp`; every line has `timestamp` + `ordinal`; `threads.created_at/updated_at` (s) and `*_ms`, `recency_at_ms` [observed] |
| user text | `event_msg` `item_completed` with `item.type=UserMessage` (real prompts); `response_item` role=user also carries synthetic context (AGENTS.md/env), so do not import it blindly; `threads.first_user_message` [observed] |
| assistant text | `response_item` `message` role=assistant `content[].output_text`; items `AgentMessage` [observed] |
| tool calls / results | `response_item` `function_call`/`custom_tool_call` (name, arguments/input, call_id) and `*_output`; `item_completed` types `CommandExecution`, `McpToolCall`, `FileChange`, `ImageView`, `SubAgentActivity`, `CollabAgentToolCall`, `ContextCompaction` [observed] |
| other | `turn_context` (model, cwd, approval/sandbox, effort), `token_usage_record`, `event_msg.token_count`, `compacted`, `world_state` (embeds AGENTS.md + skills body: skip), `reasoning.encrypted_content` (opaque), `threads.model/tokens_used/cli_version` [observed] |

- **Format changes (NEW):** every rollout line now has `ordinal`; new line types `world_state`, `token_usage_record`, `inter_agent_communication_metadata`, `compacted`; `session_meta` gained `session_id`, `runtime_workspace_roots`, `thread_source`, `history_mode`, `base_instructions` (21 KB system prompt: skip) [observed]. `threads.history_mode` (default `legacy`, migration 0040) is `paginated` for every local thread (~210); `thread_history_1.sqlite` (418 MB; `thread_turns`, `thread_items.item_json`, `rollout_byte_offset`) is a derived projection while rollout JSONL stays canonical (`LocalThreadStore` writes JSONL + SQLite metadata); `codex migrate-rollouts [--apply]` converts legacy sessions [src: codex-rs/thread-store/README.md#L22-L24; observed; --help]. Rollouts may become `.jsonl.zst` (cold-file compression; flag under development, none here) and reverted threads use `rollout-<ts>-<thread>_<rollout-id>.jsonl` (1 here), so parse names with a regex allowing both [src: codex-rs/rollout/src/compression.rs#L29,#L68; codex-rs/rollout/src/rollout_file_name.rs; observed].
- **Incremental import:** JSONL is append-only; per-file cursor = byte offset or last `ordinal` (Codex itself keeps `thread_history_projection_state.next_rollout_byte_offset`); cheap change feed = `threads.updated_at_ms`/`recency_at_ms` (WAL: `state_5.sqlite-wal` ~1 MB, so open `mode=ro` not `immutable=1` when the app runs); `thread-writer-locks/` holds writer locks [observed; src: codex-rs/rollout/src/writer_lock.rs].
- **Dedupe hazard (NEW):** the desktop app imports Claude Code/Cursor sessions as ordinary Codex threads (`[desktop] external-agent-import-sync-enabled`); the ledger `~/.codex/external_agent_session_imports.json` `{records[{source_path, content_sha256, imported_thread_id, imported_at, source_modified_at, connector_names, title}]}` lists them ; `session_meta` does not mark them, so skip `imported_thread_id`s [observed; src: codex-rs/external-agent-migration/src/sessions/ledger.rs].


#### Lead census (local, structure-only)
- Observed in recent `~/.codex/sessions/YYYY/MM/DD/rollout-*.jsonl` files [observed]: every line has top-level keys `timestamp`, `ordinal` (per-file monotonically increasing line number), `type`, `payload`, and sometimes `metadata`.
- Line `type` values seen: `response_item`, `event_msg`, `token_usage_record`, `turn_context`, `session_meta`, `world_state`, `compacted`, `inter_agent_communication_metadata`.
- `response_item.payload.type`: `message` (roles `user` / `developer` / `assistant`; content blocks `input_text`, `input_image`, `output_text`), `reasoning`, `function_call` + `function_call_output`, `custom_tool_call` + `custom_tool_call_output`, `agent_message`. `event_msg.payload.type`: `item_completed` (most frequent), `token_count`, `task_started`, `task_complete`, `thread_settings_applied`, `thread_goal_updated`.
- `session_meta.payload` key names: `id`, `session_id`, `timestamp`, `cwd`, `git`, `originator`, `cli_version`, `source`, `model_provider`, `base_instructions`, `context_window`, `runtime_workspace_roots`, `forked_from_id`, `parent_thread_id`, `thread_source`, `subagent_history_start_ordinal`, `history_mode`, `multi_agent_version`, `agent_nickname`, `agent_path`, `creator_account_id`, `creator_user_id`. 28 `session_meta` lines appeared across 25 files, so a file can contain more than one session header (forks / sub-agent history); key de-duplication on `session_meta.id` plus `ordinal`, and treat `forked_from_id` / `parent_thread_id` as lineage.
- Which lines hold the REAL user prompts: in the same 25 rollouts, `event_msg` / `item_completed` items had `item.type` values `UserMessage` (115), `AgentMessage`, `CommandExecution`, `McpToolCall`, `Reasoning`, `Extension`, `FileChange`, `ImageView`, `ContextCompaction`, `SubAgentActivity`, `CollabAgentToolCall`, `Plan`. Of 157 `response_item` role=`user` messages (a second pass over the same files, which were still being written), 112 were plain text (about the same number as the 115 `UserMessage` items) and 45 started with `# AGENTS.md instructions...`, `<environment_context>`, `<recommended_plugins>`, `<external_codex_apps_open_page>`, `<skill>`, `<manually_attached_skills>`, `<task-notification>`, `<send_user_message_question_reply>` or `<available_subagent_types>`. An importer should take prompts from `UserMessage` items (or filter those leading tags) and skip `developer` messages [observed]. Hook stdout is documented as "extra developer context" [doc: https://developers.openai.com/codex/hooks.md#sessionstart]; where it is persisted in the rollout was not checked.

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Plaintext JSONL/SQLite locally; only `reasoning.encrypted_content` is opaque; `auth.json` must not be read [observed]. Persistence can be off: `[history] persistence="none"` (default `save-all`) or `codex exec --ephemeral` [src: codex-rs/config/defaults.toml; --help]. No export command found; programmatic reads go through `codex app-server` (JSON-RPC over `stdio://`, `unix://`, `ws://`; e.g. `thread/items/list`, `thread/archive`, `thread/delete`; `thread/list`/`thread/read` not verified) [src: codex-rs/app-server/README.md#L521-L535; --help]. CLI: `codex archive|unarchive|delete <id>`, `codex exec --json` for new runs [--help].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP:** `~/.codex/config.toml` or trusted-project `.codex/config.toml` `[mcp_servers.<name>]` (snippet below); keys `command, args, env, env_vars, cwd | url, bearer_token_env_var, http_headers, env_http_headers, http_headers_helper, auth, oauth, scopes | enabled, required, startup_timeout_sec (10), tool_timeout_sec (60), enabled_tools, disabled_tools, default_tools_approval_mode (auto|prompt|writes|approve)`. CLI: `codex mcp add <name> [--env K=V] -- <cmd...>` or `--url`, `list [--json]`, `get`, `remove`, `login`, `logout`. `codex mcp-server` no longer exists in 0.160.0 [src: codex-rs/config/src/mcp_types.rs#L223-L304,#L367-L434; --help; doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/extend/mcp?surface=cli].
```toml
[mcp_servers.recall]
command = "recall"
args = ["serve"]            # whatever recall's stdio entrypoint is
startup_timeout_sec = 10
default_tools_approval_mode = "auto"
```
- **Global instructions:** `$CODEX_HOME/AGENTS.override.md` else `AGENTS.md`; project: each dir from project root (`project_root_markers`, default `[".git"]`) to cwd checks `AGENTS.override.md`, `AGENTS.md`, then `project_doc_fallback_filenames`; root-to-cwd concatenation capped by `project_doc_max_bytes` (32768); user and project parts joined by `--- project-doc ---` [src: codex-rs/core/src/agents_md.rs#L43-L49; codex-rs/config/defaults.toml; doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/agent-configuration/agents-md].
- **Skills (Agent Skills):** `SKILL.md` (+ optional `agents/openai.yaml` `policy.allow_implicit_invocation`); roots: `<dir>/.agents/skills` for each dir project-root to cwd, `<repo>/.codex/skills`, `~/.agents/skills`, `$CODEX_HOME/skills` (deprecated; `.system` = bundled), `/etc/codex/skills`, plugin skills; disable with `[[skills.config]] path=..., enabled=false`. Here `~/.codex/skills/*` are symlinks into `~/.agents/skills` [src: codex-rs/ext/skills/src/host_roots.rs#L24-L26,#L85-L135,#L138-L175; doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/build-skills; observed].
- **Hooks (stable, on by default; `features.hooks`, alias `codex_hooks`):** sources `~/.codex/hooks.json`, `<repo>/.codex/hooks.json`, inline `[hooks]` in the same config layers, plugin `hooks/hooks.json` or manifest `hooks`, managed `requirements.toml` [src: codex-rs/features/src/lib.rs#L1298; codex-rs/hooks/src/engine/discovery.rs#L94-L206,#L339; doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks]. Schema `{"hooks":{"<Event>":[{"matcher":"..","hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"..","timeout":600,"async":false,"statusMessage":"..","additionalContextLimit":2500} | {"type":"mcp_tool","server":"..","tool":"..","input":{"k":"${tool_input.x}"}}]}]}}` (`prompt`/`agent` types are skipped) [src: codex-rs/config/src/hook_config.rs#L12-L190]. Events (12): `SessionStart` (matcher = `startup|resume|clear|compact|fork`), `UserPromptSubmit`, `PreToolUse`/`PostToolUse`/`PermissionRequest` (matcher = tool name incl. `mcp__server__tool`), `PreCompact`/`PostCompact` (`manual|auto`), `SubagentStart`, `SubagentStop`, `Stop`, `SessionEnd` (1 s timeout), `Interrupt`; matcher is `*`/empty, `A|B` exact list, or regex, and is ignored for UserPromptSubmit/Stop/Interrupt [src: codex-rs/hooks/src/lib.rs#L43-L53; codex-rs/hooks/src/engine/matcher.rs#L8-L25]. stdin JSON: `session_id`, `transcript_path` (string or null; presumably the rollout file [inferred]), `cwd`, `hook_event_name`, `model`, `permission_mode`, plus `turn_id`, `prompt`, `source`, `tool_name/tool_input/tool_use_id/tool_response`, `last_assistant_message`, `stop_hook_active`, `reason` [src: codex-rs/hooks/schema/generated/*.schema.json].
  - **Context injection: YES for SessionStart and UserPromptSubmit:** plain (non-JSON) stdout is added as developer context, and so is JSON `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext`; output over `additionalContextLimit` (default 2,500 tokens) is spilled to a file reference; SubagentStart and Pre/PostToolUse also accept `additionalContext`; Stop/SubagentStop accept `decision:"block"` + `reason`; SessionEnd/Interrupt/PreCompact/PostCompact have no context output field [src: codex-rs/hooks/src/events/session_start.rs#L221-L223; user_prompt_submit.rs#L218; doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks].
  - **Trust gate:** non-managed hooks (user, project, plugin) are skipped until reviewed in `/hooks`; trust is stored as `[hooks.state."<source>:<snake_event>:<group>:<handler>"] trusted_hash="sha256:.."` and any edit re-flags it; `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust` skips it per run; managed hooks are trusted [src: codex-rs/hooks/src/engine/discovery.rs#L796-L813; codex-rs/config/src/hook_config.rs#L28-L33; observed]. Legacy `notify = [argv...]` appends an `agent-turn-complete` JSON argument (fire-and-forget, no injection) [src: codex-rs/hooks/src/legacy_notify.rs].
- **Plugins:** manifest `.codex-plugin/plugin.json` (also reads `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`); keys `name, version, description, author, homepage, repository, license, keywords, skills, mcpServers (path or inline), apps, hooks (path/list/inline), commands, interface{displayName, shortDescription, longDescription, developerName, category, capabilities, websiteURL, privacyPolicyURL, termsOfServiceURL, defaultPrompt, brandColor, composerIcon, logo, logoDark, screenshots}` [src: codex-rs/core-plugins/src/manifest.rs#L49-L80; observed: bundled plugin.json]. Marketplace `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json` (`~/.agents/plugins/` personal) `{name, interface, plugins[{name, source{source: local|url|git-subdir|npm, path}, policy{installation, authentication}, category}]}`; `codex plugin add <plugin>@<marketplace>`, `plugin list`, `plugin remove`, `plugin marketplace add|list|upgrade|remove` (path, `owner/repo[@ref]`, git URL); cache `~/.codex/plugins/cache/<marketplace>/<plugin>/<version|local>/`; enable via `[plugins."<name>@<marketplace>"] enabled`; `[marketplaces.<n>] source_type/source/ref` (Claude-format marketplaces `claude-plugins-official`, `claude-cowork` are consumed here); plugin hooks get `PLUGIN_ROOT`/`PLUGIN_DATA` (+ `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT/DATA`) [doc: https://developers.openai.com/plugins/build/plugins; --help; observed; src: discovery.rs#L265-L270]. A recall plugin can ship skill + MCP + SessionStart hook in one install, but its hook still needs trust.
- **Slash commands:** `~/.codex/prompts/*.md` (`/prompts:<name>`, `$1..$9`, `$ARGUMENTS`) is deprecated in favour of skills [doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/custom-prompts].
- **Headless:** `codex exec [PROMPT|-]` with `--json` (JSONL `thread.started{thread_id}`, `turn.started`, `turn.completed{usage}`, `turn.failed`, `item.started|updated|completed` for `agent_message`, `reasoning`, `command_execution`, `file_change`, `mcp_tool_call`, `collab_tool_call`, `web_search`, `todo_list`, `error`), `-o`, `--output-schema`, `--ephemeral`, `--skip-git-repo-check`, `-s`, `-C`, `-m`, `-p`, `--ignore-user-config`, `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust`; `codex exec resume|fork`, `codex resume --last|--all`; CI auth `CODEX_API_KEY` [src: codex-rs/exec/src/exec_events.rs#L10-L36,#L106-L132; --help; doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/non-interactive-mode].
- **Native memory (overlaps recall):** `features.memories` (stable, default off; on here with `[memories] generate_memories/use_memories`); at session start a background pipeline extracts per-rollout memories (idle at least 6 h, age at most 30 d, 16 per startup; skips ephemeral/subagent sessions) into `memories_1.sqlite` and a consolidation agent writes `~/.codex/memories/{memory_summary.md, MEMORY.md, raw_memories.md, rollout_summaries/, skills/, extensions/}` (git-tracked); `memory_summary.md` (cap 2,500 tokens) is injected into every new session and tools `memories.{list,read,search,add_ad_hoc_note}` are exposed; `memories.disable_on_external_context` drops threads that used MCP [src: codex-rs/memories/README.md#L29-L130; codex-rs/ext/memories/src/lib.rs#L10-L21; doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/customization/memories]. Do not write into `~/.codex/memories` (generated, diff-baselined).

**Best recall injection point:** a user-level `~/.codex/hooks.json` `SessionStart` (matcher `startup|resume|clear`) command whose stdout (plain text or `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext`) is injected, optionally `UserPromptSubmit` for per-prompt recall; it needs a one-time `/hooks` trust, so also ship an `AGENTS.md` block telling the agent to call the recall MCP tool as the no-trust fallback. Least fragile MCP registration: `codex mcp add recall -- <recall serve command>` (writes `[mcp_servers.recall]` in `~/.codex/config.toml`, shared by CLI, desktop and IDE extension).
**Confidence / unknowns:** high on hooks/MCP/AGENTS/skills/plugins (source @822e58c plus docs); medium on desktop-only behaviour (closed-source `codex-dev.db`, hook-trust UI in the app); unverified: `thread/list`/`thread/read` RPC names, Windows/Linux runtime paths.

---

## ChatGPT desktop app, macOS (OpenAI; closed source)


The desktop app is an Electron bundle (`Contents/Resources/app.asar`, `Frameworks/Codex Framework.framework`) that bundles `Resources/codex-cli` and is not sandboxed [observed]. Leftover Classic data, where present, sits in `~/Library/Application Support/com.openai.chat/`; the new app's Chromium profile is `~/Library/Application Support/Codex/` [observed].
