**Version verified:** app 26.930.51102 [observed]; docs read 2026-10-06: learn.chatgpt.com what's-new [doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/whats-new], help.openai.com 9268871 "Updated 2 months ago" [doc: https://help.openai.com/en/articles/9268871-how-is-data-retained-in-the-macos-app].

### 1. Detection
- Two products share the name. (a) Native app, bundle id `com.openai.chat`, renamed "ChatGPT Classic" on 2026-07-09 [3p: https://9to5mac.com/2026/07/17/openai-fixes-chat-access-in-the-chatgpt-app-for-mac/]. (b) Electron app, `/Applications/ChatGPT.app`, bundle id `com.openai.codex`: OpenAI states the Codex app merged into the ChatGPT desktop app on 2026-07-09 (macOS, Windows) and shipped a Linux preview (.deb/.rpm) in Aug 2026 [doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/whats-new].
- The unified bundle's own updater code names `ChatGPT.app`, `ChatGPT Classic.app` and legacy ids `com.openai.chat-sparkle-updater` / `com.openai.chat-sparkle-progress` [observed] (app.asar constants). A migration bug can leave a duplicate `/Applications/Codex.app` [3p: https://codex.danielvaughan.com/2026/07/17/codex-chatgpt-unified-desktop-app-cli-migration-codex-app-detection-workarounds/]. Classic keeps `com.openai.chat` as its data dir [inferred]: nothing newer than 2026-07-13 was written there after the swap.
- Detect by bundle id (`mdfind kMDItemCFBundleIdentifier`), not by name. Relocation vars: `CODEX_HOME` (default `$HOME/.codex`), `CODEX_ELECTRON_USER_DATA_PATH` and `--user-data-dir` (Electron profile) [observed] (app.asar constants). No relocation var found for Classic.
- Windows legacy app data (pre-merger): `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Packages\OpenAI.ChatGPT-Desktop_2p2nqsd0c76g0\LocalCache\Roaming\ChatGPT\` with `IndexedDB/https_chatgpt.com_0.indexeddb.leveldb` [3p: seanthegeek/coding-agent-collector@26a7af2:collectors/research/chatgpt-desktop.md]; post-merger Windows/Linux layout: not found.

### 2. History location
| Surface | Location | Local content? |
|---|---|---|
| Classic native chats | `~/Library/Application Support/com.openai.chat/conversations-v3-<uuid>/<id>.data`; 80 files, 10.5 MB, mtimes 2026-04-13..2026-07-13 [observed] | encrypted cache |
| Unified app, Chat mode | OpenAI servers; macOS-app chats follow web retention and uploaded files live in the cloud, not locally [doc: https://help.openai.com/en/articles/9268871-how-is-data-retained-in-the-macos-app]. Chromium caches under `Codex/Default/IndexedDB` (1.8 MB) and `Local Storage` (3.5 MB) are too small to hold history [observed] | none usable |
| Unified app, local Work + Codex | `~/.codex/sessions/YYYY/MM/DD/rollout-*.jsonl` + `~/.codex/state_5.sqlite`; `threads.originator` = `codex_work_desktop` (Work, 13 of 213 threads here) vs `Codex Desktop` (Codex view) [observed]. OpenAI: local Work conversations stay on the computer; Codex keeps a separate history [doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/whats-new] | yes (Codex format) |
| Synced Work (cloud-coordinated) | server; "local execution does not mean content stays only on that computer" [doc: https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001548-agent-security-and-local-work-sync-in-chatgpt] | no |
- Other Classic dirs: `gizmos-<uuid>/{bootstrap,projects}.data`, `models-<uuid>/response.data`, `drafts-v2-<uuid>/<uuid>.data`, `project-g-p-<hex>/<uuid>.data` (all high-entropy), plus plain `workspace-data/user-<id>__<uuid>/system-hints/response.data` (bplist of UI hints), `automation-repository-*/automations.json`, `ChatGPTHelper/helper_cache.data` (bplist); the Codex-side dirs `codex-taskItems-v2-{default,archived}-<uuid>`, `codex-taskDetails-v1-<uuid>`, `codex-environments-<uuid>` and `pinned-items-*`, `order-*` are all empty (0 files); `app_pairing_extensions/` has 56 small JSON files (purpose unknown) [observed]. `~/Library/Group Containers/group.com.openai.chat` and `Containers/com.openai.chat.Widgets/Data` are TCC-protected ("Operation not permitted") and were not inspected [observed].
- `~/.codex/.chatgpt-projects/g-p-<id>/` holds per-project working folders (AGENTS.md, deliverables, sources) that look like the local side of ChatGPT Projects: all 3 ids also exist among the 8 Classic `project-g-p-<hex>` dirs [observed], [inferred].

### 3. Storage format
**Classic `.data` (structure-only evidence, 80 files):** `file(1)` = "data" for 75, chance magic hits (OpenPGP key, DOS COM) for 5. First 256 KiB of the largest file: Shannon entropy 7.9992 bits/byte, printable-ASCII ratio 0.383 (uniform random bytes give 98/256 = 0.383), chi-square 295 (255 dof). Per-file first 64 KiB: entropy min/median/max 7.963/7.997/7.998. All 80 files have distinct 4-byte, 12-byte and last-16-byte patterns (no shared header or magic); sizes mod 16 occupy 15 of 16 residues (not block-padded); min size 4,925 B [observed]. Reading: AEAD or stream-cipher container with no plaintext header; it does not identify the cipher.
**Work/Codex threads (cross-reference the Codex section for the full map):**
| Concept | Field |
|---|---|
| session id | `threads.id`; rollout line 1 `session_meta.payload.id` [observed] |
| title / cwd | `threads.title`, `threads.name`, `threads.cwd` [observed] |
| created / updated | `threads.created_at(_ms)`, `updated_at(_ms)` [observed] |
| user / assistant / tool text | rollout `response_item` and `event_msg` lines; newer `thread_history_1.sqlite` tables `thread_turns`, `thread_items` [observed] |
| origin tag | `threads.originator` (`codex_work_desktop`), `thread_source`, `source`; PII to drop: `creator_user_id`, `creator_account_id` [observed] |
- Directory-name history: `conversations-<uuid>/` plain (original release); `conversations-v2-<uuid>/` encrypted, shipped in ChatGPT for Mac 1.2024.171 [3p: https://pvieito.com/2024/07/chatgpt-unprotected-conversations] (press confirms OpenAI shipped encryption [3p: https://9to5mac.com/2024/07/03/chatgpt-macos-conversations-plain-text/]); `conversations-v3-<uuid>/` already listed beside v1/v2 on 2025-12-02 [3p: Dicklesworthstone/coding_agent_session_search@546c054:src/connectors/chatgpt.rs#L7-L13], so v3 predates Dec 2025; exact release: not found. Only v3 exists here.
- Incremental: nothing to track for Classic (frozen since the app was replaced). Work/Codex: rollouts are append-only; `state_5.sqlite` has `-wal`/`-shm` files beside it, so `immutable=1` (used for this research) ignores recent rows: an importer should copy db+wal+shm or open read-only without `immutable` [inferred from SQLite semantics]; `codex migrate-rollouts` and `history_mode = paginated` (213/213 threads) show a move to paginated thread history, so verify which store is authoritative [observed].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Classic v2/v3 are encrypted. Reverse-engineering write-ups: v2 key is Keychain item `com.openai.chat.conversations_v2_cache` [3p: pvieito.com above]; one parser claims AES-256-GCM, layout nonce(12) + ciphertext + tag(16), key in Keychain access group `2DC432GLL2.com.openai.shared` (matches the unified app's entitlement [observed]) but only says files "appear to use" it [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/chatgpt.rs#L3-L30, #L397-L427]. v3 item name: not found (probably `..._v3_cache` [inferred] from the v2 pattern). Secure Enclave: no evidence either way.
- No official decryptor and no community extractor found. cass can decrypt only with a user-supplied key (`CHATGPT_ENCRYPTION_KEY` base64, or `~/.config/cass/chatgpt_key.bin`) and assumes the plaintext is a `mapping` export-style object or a `messages[]` list, unvalidated [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/chatgpt.rs#L105-L160, #L477-L500, #L596-L640]. AILogExplorer takes the other route: it only flags `conversations-v3` `.data` files as an encrypted cache and tells the user to import the official export [3p: ayoungh/AILogExplorer@a5bfe1f:lib/server/jobs.ts#L187-L190]. SpecStory (no ChatGPT provider) and agent-sessions (no ChatGPT parser) do not cover it [3p: specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a:specstory-cli/pkg/providers; jazzyalex/agent-sessions@6fa9a73]. A third-party process without OpenAI's signing identity gets a Keychain prompt or denial [inferred]. Recall should not read the Keychain (secret store; prompts the user) and this research did not.
- Official export is the supported route (next section). Unified-app Chat history is server-side: no local chat store was found [observed], and OpenAI says macOS-app chats follow the web retention policy with files kept in the cloud [doc: https://help.openai.com/en/articles/9268871-how-is-data-retained-in-the-macos-app].
- LOCAL-ONLY importer: can detect the app and import local Work and Codex threads through the Codex importer (tag `originator`); cannot read any Classic chat or any Chat-mode chat. Export importer needs the ZIP. [inferred from the Codex rollout and SQLite facts above]

### 5. Integration surfaces
- MCP, Codex/Work view: `~/.codex/config.toml` or `<repo>/.codex/config.toml`, table `[mcp_servers.<name>]` with `command`, `args`, `env`, `env_vars`, `cwd`; `codex mcp` manages them [doc: https://developers.openai.com/codex/mcp], [observed] (`codex --help`). Minimal: `[mcp_servers.recall]` / `command = "recall"` / `args = ["serve"]`. Chat view: remote servers only (Settings > Apps > Create, developer mode; Secure MCP Tunnel for dev machines) [doc: https://help.openai.com/en/articles/12584461-developer-mode-and-mcp-apps-in-chatgpt].
- Global instructions: `~/.codex/AGENTS.md` (present here) and repo `AGENTS.md`; ChatGPT personalization is server-side [observed], [doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/whats-new]. Skills: `$HOME/.agents/skills`, `$CWD/.agents/skills` up to repo root, `/etc/codex/skills` [doc: https://developers.openai.com/codex/skills].
- Hooks (Codex/Work view): `~/.codex/hooks.json`, inline `[[hooks.<Event>]]` in `config.toml`, repo `.codex/hooks.json`; SessionStart plain stdout and `additionalContext` are added as developer context, PreToolUse/PostToolUse plain stdout is ignored [doc: https://developers.openai.com/codex/hooks]. Command hooks and hooks from local config or plugins are not supported when Work uses cloud orchestration with local computer access [doc: https://developers.openai.com/codex/mcp]. Chat view and Classic: none.
- Plugins (bundles of skills, connectors, MCP) since 2026-03 [doc: https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/whats-new]; bundled `Resources/plugins/openai-bundled` [observed]. Headless: `codex exec` (alias `e`) from the bundled binary [observed]. Custom slash commands: see Codex section.

**Best recall injection point:** Codex/Work view: SessionStart (and UserPromptSubmit) hook in `~/.codex/hooks.json` plus a `[mcp_servers.recall]` entry in `~/.codex/config.toml`. Chat view and Classic have no local injection surface; use the export importer.
**Confidence / unknowns:** high on app identity, Classic encryption and Work-thread location; unknown cipher/key name for v3, v3 release date, post-merger Windows/Linux paths, and whether `thread_history_1.sqlite` or rollout JSONL is authoritative.

---

## ChatGPT data export (OpenAI account export, `conversations.json` archive)


**Version verified:** OpenAI help articles 7260999 ("Updated last month"), 9106926 and 20001279 ("Updated 2 months ago") read 2026-10-06 [doc: https://help.openai.com/en/articles/7260999-exporting-your-chatgpt-history-and-data]; community spec v3.0 dated 2026-02-05 plus code with a July 2026 note [3p: mohamed-chs/convoviz@6c22e73:docs/dev/chatgpt-spec.md].

### 1. Detection
- Input detection: ZIP (or extracted dir) whose root has `conversations.json` or numbered shards matching `^conversations-\d+\.json$` [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:convoviz/io/loaders.py#L17]; payload is a JSON array whose items carry `mapping` (importer discriminator `jsonData[0]?.mapping`) [3p: danny-avila/LibreChat@f10b1d9:api/server/utils/import/importers.js#L93-L108]. Match shard names exactly: `shared_conversations.json` also ends in `conversations.json` but holds id/title stubs only [3p: moorcheh-ai/memanto@d6545ff:examples/migrations/chatgpt-claude-okf/EXPORTING.md].
- Nothing is installed or relocatable; there is no env var. [inferred: the export is a user-supplied file]

### 2. History location (trigger and delivery)
- Path: profile menu > Settings > Data controls > Export data > Export > Confirm export. Alternative: Privacy Portal > Make a Privacy Request > "I have a consumer ChatGPT account" > Download my data [doc: https://help.openai.com/en/articles/7260999-exporting-your-chatgpt-history-and-data].
- Delivery: email (SMS if the account has only a phone number) "Download data export"; up to 7 days; link expires 24 h after receipt; download while signed in to the requesting account; deleted chats are not restored [doc: same]. The Android app exposes the same Data controls > Export Data (some versions open the web flow) [doc: https://help.openai.com/en/articles/8167885-how-to-export-your-data-from-the-chatgpt-android-app].
- Plans: Free, Go, Plus, Pro, and ChatGPT Edu workspaces whose admin enabled export and without data residency; not for Business, Enterprise or Healthcare workspaces; not when logged out [doc: 7260999 and https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001279-exporting-data-from-a-chatgpt-edu-workspace].
- Official contents: "conversations.json, or numbered conversation JSON files for larger exports", files and assets used in conversations, account and conversation metadata [doc: 20001279; https://help.openai.com/en/articles/9106926-transfer-exported-conversations-between-chatgpt-accounts].

### 3. Storage format
**Archive root (community-observed, not documented by OpenAI):** `conversations.json` or `conversations-000.json`, `-001`... (sharding seen by Feb 2026; one write-up says 100 conversations per shard) ; `chat.html` (rendered copy); `user.json`; `message_feedback.json`; `shared_conversations.json`; `model_comparisons.json`; 2025 additions `group_chats.json`, `shopping.json`, `sora.json` (often empty); `textdocs/` (Canvas); `dalle-generations/`; `user-<id>/`; root `file-*` uploads (`file-<alnum>-<name>`, `file_<hex>-sanitized.<ext>`); one source also lists `tool_messages.json` [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:docs/dev/chatgpt-spec.md#L13-L42; moorcheh-ai/memanto@d6545ff:examples/migrations/chatgpt-claude-okf/EXPORTING.md; https://portable-ai-memory.org/providers/openai/].
| Concept | Field (none documented by OpenAI; observed [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:docs/dev/chatgpt-spec.md#L44-L237; kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/chatgpt.go#L23-L66; LibreChat fixture api/server/utils/import/__data__/chatgpt-export.json]) |
|---|---|
| session id | `conversation_id` (fallback `id`); URL `https://chatgpt.com/c/<id>` |
| title | `title` (nullable) |
| project / GPT | `gizmo_id`, `gizmo_type`; no cwd concept |
| created / updated | `create_time`, `update_time` (Unix seconds, float); per message `create_time`, `update_time` (nullable) |
| tree | `mapping{node_id -> {id, parent, children[], message (object or null)}}`, `current_node` (leaf of the active branch) |
| user / assistant text | `message.author.role` in user/assistant; `message.content.parts[]` (strings, or objects for `image_asset_pointer`) |
| tool calls | assistant node whose `message.recipient` != `all` (`python`, `web.run`, `dalle.text2im`, `bio`, `canmore.create_textdoc`) and `content_type: code` |
| tool results | role `tool`, `author.name`; `content_type` `execution_output`, `tether_quote`, `tether_browsing_display`, `sonic_webpage`, `system_error`, `multimodal_text` |
| model | `message.metadata.model_slug`; conversation `default_model_slug`, `model_slug` |
| other | `is_archived`, `is_starred`, `pinned_time`, `voice`, `moderation_results`, `safe_urls`; 2025+: `is_study_mode`, `is_do_not_remember`, `memory_scope`, `context_scopes`, `owner`, `conversation_origin` |
Message keys: `id, author{role,name,metadata}, create_time, update_time, content, status, end_turn, weight, recipient, metadata`; July 2026 exports may omit `status`/`weight` [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:convoviz/models/message.py#L72]. Observed `content_type` values: `text`, `multimodal_text`, `code` (`text`, `language`), `execution_output` (`text`), `tether_quote` (`text`,`title`,`url`,`domain`), `tether_browsing_display`, `sonic_webpage`, `system_error`, `thoughts` (`thoughts[].summary/content`), `reasoning_recap` (`content`), `user_editable_context` (custom instructions repeated in every conversation; skip), plus rarer `computer_output`, `super_widget`, `citable_code_output`; inline citation markers of the form U+3010 n U+2020 text U+3011 sit inside assistant text and need stripping [3p: convoviz spec; LibreChat importers.js#L467-L478, #L567-L598; memanto EXPORTING.md; NateBJones-Projects/OB1@238df6c:recipes/chatgpt-conversation-import/chatgpt_parser.py#L31-L45]. A `channel` field: not found in any parser.
- Linearize: start at `current_node`, follow `parent` to the root, reverse; skip nodes with `message == null`, hidden messages (`metadata.is_visually_hidden_from_conversation`, system role unless `is_user_system_message`, tool noise) and cap the walk against cycles [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:docs/dev/chatgpt-spec.md#L127-L146, #L266-L305; convoviz@6c22e73:convoviz/models/conversation.py#L47-L60; kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/chatgpt.go#L223-L250]. Importing all nodes instead of the active path duplicates edited and regenerated turns [3p: LibreChat importers.js#L446-L480].
- Assets: `image_asset_pointer.asset_pointer` uses `sediment://<file id>` (current) or `file-service://<file id>` (legacy), resolved to a file in the ZIP root or `dalle-generations/` [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:convoviz/message_logic.py#L26-L33].
- Format changes: unannounced and frequent ("OpenAI frequently changes the internal schema") [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:docs/dev/chatgpt-export-discovery.md]; sharding appeared by 2026-02-23 [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:docs/dev/CHANGELOG.md#L8].
- Incremental: each export is a full snapshot; no delta mechanism is documented [doc: https://help.openai.com/en/articles/7260999-exporting-your-chatgpt-history-and-data]. Dedupe on `conversation_id` and re-import when `update_time` grows [inferred]. The file is typically one minified line, 50 MB or more on big accounts, so stream-parse [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:docs/dev/chatgpt-export-discovery.md]. Tolerate a `{"conversations": [...]}` wrapper [3p: convoviz@6c22e73:convoviz/io/loaders.py#L141-L163].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Plain JSON inside the ZIP; the emailed link is the only access control. Memories are not reliably included (as of Feb 2026) [3p: portable-ai-memory.org above; memanto EXPORTING.md]. Codex or Work local threads are not in this export.
- LOCAL-ONLY importer: nothing. Export importer needs: the ZIP or directory path from the user, the shard glob, a streaming JSON reader, the `current_node` walk, an asset resolver for `sediment://` and `file-service://`, and a per-source id prefix (agentsview uses `chatgpt:<conversation_id>`, project `chatgpt.com`) [3p: kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/chatgpt.go#L206-L207]. Prior art: `cass import chatgpt <conversations.json>` splits the export into one JSON file per conversation under `conversations-web-export/` and registers that dir as a scan root in `sources.toml` [3p: Dicklesworthstone/coding_agent_session_search@76bc79b:src/lib.rs#L20354-L20482, tests/e2e_import_chatgpt.rs#L1-L10]; its connector then sorts `mapping` nodes by `create_time` rather than walking `current_node` [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/chatgpt.rs#L510-L513]; SpecStory has no ChatGPT provider [3p: specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a:specstory-cli/pkg/providers]; agent-sessions has no ChatGPT web parser [3p: jazzyalex/agent-sessions@6fa9a73].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- Not applicable (data file). No MCP, hooks, skills or headless mode; OpenAI also offers no history API for personal accounts [3p: moorcheh-ai/memanto@d6545ff EXPORTING.md].

**Best recall injection point:** none; import-only source (`recall import chatgpt <zip>`). Recall can emit the same MCP/memory for the agent that the user later opens.
**Confidence / unknowns:** high on trigger path, delivery and linearization; file list beyond `conversations*.json` and the 100-per-shard figure rest on community write-ups; OpenAI documents neither the JSON schema nor the shard naming.

---

## Claude.ai data export (Anthropic account export)


**Version verified:** Anthropic help article 9450526 dated July 8, 2026 [doc: https://support.claude.com/en/articles/9450526-export-your-claude-data]; memory article 11817273 ("Updated this week") [doc: https://support.claude.com/en/articles/11817273-use-claude-s-chat-search-and-memory-to-build-on-previous-context]; third-party parsers read 2026-10-06.

### 1. Detection
- Input: ZIP with `conversations.json`, a JSON array whose items have `chat_messages` (importer discriminator `jsonData[0]?.chat_messages`) [3p: danny-avila/LibreChat@f10b1d9:api/server/utils/import/importers.js#L93-L108]. No env var or install path.

### 2. History location (trigger and delivery)
- Path: click initials (lower left) > Settings > Privacy > Export data; available on the web app or Claude Desktop, not from iOS/Android. Free, Pro, Max individuals; on Team/Enterprise only the Primary Owner exports (organization export is a separate article) [doc: https://support.claude.com/en/articles/9450526-export-your-claude-data].
- Delivery: link by email to the account address; must be signed in; expires 24 h after delivery; re-run to get a new link; an export cannot be imported into another personal Claude account [doc: same].
- Memory: "All memory data is included in data exports"; a legacy-memory export option in Settings > Memory ran until 2026-09-09 [doc: 11817273].

### 3. Storage format
**Archive files:** `conversations.json` (certain). Community reports disagree on the rest: `users.json` (`uuid, full_name, email_address, verified_phone_number`), `projects.json` (`uuid, name, description, prompt_template, docs, creator`), `memories.json` (array of `{conversations_memory: str, project_memories?: {<project uuid>: str}}`) [3p: https://portable-ai-memory.org/providers/anthropic/ (observed Feb 2026); anima-research/connectome-host@dec7658:scripts/evacuator.ts#L636-L650]; one SEO guide claims `user_profile.json` and `usage.json` and no projects/memory, another `login_history.json` [3p: https://takeoutday.org/guides/how-to-export-claude-data; memanto EXPORTING.md]: treat as unverified and test for presence. Claude Code and Cowork sessions are not in this export [3p: memanto EXPORTING.md].
| Concept | Field [3p: zero8dotdev/smriti@bfc1fdb:src/ingest/claude-web.ts#L24-L106; kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/claude_ai.go#L12-L58] |
|---|---|
| session id | conversation `uuid` |
| title | `name` (may be empty; LibreChat falls back to a placeholder); `summary` (optional) |
| project | none per conversation (projects live in `projects.json`; link: not found); `account.uuid` |
| created / updated | `created_at`, `updated_at` (RFC 3339 UTC strings, fractional seconds optional) on conversation and message |
| user / assistant text | `chat_messages[].sender` = `human` or `assistant`; join `content[]` blocks with `type: text` (`text`); fall back to `text` (older exports keep the body there; newer may keep only a preview) |
| thinking | block `type: thinking` (`thinking`, `summaries[]`, `cut_off`); no signatures |
| tool calls | block `tool_use` (`id`, `name`, `input`, optional `integration_name`, `mcp_server_url`); artifacts appear as `name: artifacts` with `input{command,id,type,title,content,language,version_uuid,old_str,new_str}` |
| tool results | block `tool_result` (`tool_use_id`, `name`, `content[]`, `is_error`) |
| other blocks | `voice_note` (`title`,`text`), `token_budget` (discard); text blocks may carry `citations[]`, `start_timestamp`, `stop_timestamp` |
| attachments / files | `attachments[]{file_name,file_size,file_type,extracted_content}` (text only); `files[]{file_uuid,file_name}` (no bytes for images) |
| tree | `parent_message_uuid` per message; root sentinel `00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000`; no current-leaf pointer in the export (the live web API has `current_leaf_message_uuid` [observed]) |
- Linearize: unbranched = list order. Branched (edits/retries) = pick a path; one importer takes, at each fork, the subtree whose deepest leaf has the latest `created_at` (heuristic) [3p: connectome-host@dec7658:scripts/import-claudeai-export.ts#L28-L35, #L161-L182]. Others ignore the tree and import list order [3p: LibreChat importers.js#L202-L260].
- Format changes: older exports body in `text`, newer in typed `content[]` [3p: memanto EXPORTING.md]; block types vary (voice notes, MCP app fields) [3p: smriti; connectome-host].
- Incremental: each export is a full snapshot and the link expires in 24 h [doc: https://support.claude.com/en/articles/9450526-export-your-claude-data]; key on conversation `uuid` and re-import when `updated_at` changes [inferred]. Parsers stream the top-level array object by object [3p: kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/claude_ai.go#L62-L100].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Plain JSON; PII in `users.json` (name, email, phone): do not import. Thinking signatures are absent, so thinking cannot be replayed to the API [3p: connectome-host@dec7658 docs/claudeai-evacuation.md].
- LOCAL-ONLY importer: nothing; claude.ai history is server-side. Export importer needs: ZIP path, `conversations.json` stream, a per-source id prefix (agentsview uses `claude-ai:<uuid>`, project `claude.ai`) [3p: kenn-io/agentsview@597a06b:internal/parser/claude_ai.go#L219-L220], the text/thinking/tool block rules above, and optional `memories.json` (`conversations_memory`) for memory import. Prior art: LibreChat (`importClaudeConvo`), smriti (`ingestClaudeWeb`), agentsview; cass has no Claude web import, SpecStory has no Claude web provider [3p: LibreChat importers.js#L202-L285; smriti claude-web.ts; specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a:specstory-cli/pkg/providers].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- Not applicable (data file). Team/Enterprise admins can retrieve content with the Compliance API instead of exports [doc: https://support.claude.com/en/articles/14479288-claude-cowork-architecture-overview].

**Best recall injection point:** none; import-only source (`recall import claude <zip>`).
**Confidence / unknowns:** high on trigger path, expiry and conversation/message/block shape (three independent parsers agree); the file list beyond `conversations.json` and the branch rule are community-only; no OpenAPI-style schema from Anthropic.

---

## Cursor (Anysphere: Cursor IDE + `cursor-agent` / `agent` CLI)


Observed `~/.cursor/` entries: agents/, commands/, plugins/, projects/, skills/, skills-cursor/, mcp.json, cli-config.json, agent-cli-state.json, ide_state.json, argv.json, ai-tracking/, chat-migration-backups/ (no hooks.json) [observed]. The app bundle id is `com.todesktop.230313mzl4w4u92`; `agent` and `cursor-agent` can be symlinks into `~/.local/share/cursor-agent/versions/<ver>` [observed].
**Version verified:** vendor docs read 2026-10-06 (hooks, third-party-hooks, rules, skills, plugins, mcp, cli/*; changelog to 2026-09-23, CLI changelog to the 2026-08-26 release); installer script currently ships CLI `2026.10.01-e373342`; IDE 3.22.12 bundle and CLI 2026.01.23 bundle inspected as strings only [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/hooks.md; doc: https://cursor.com/docs/cli/changelog.md; observed]

### 1. Detection
- **IDE:** `/Applications/Cursor.app`, `product.json`: `applicationName=cursor`, `dataFolderName=.cursor`, `urlProtocol=cursor`, `serverApplicationName=cursor-server`, quality stable; data in `~/Library/Application Support/Cursor/User/{globalStorage,workspaceStorage}`; Nightly keeps data in `~/Library/Application Support/Cursor Nightly` + `~/.cursor-nightly` [observed]. Launcher `Contents/Resources/app/bin/cursor` (not on PATH here) has `--user-data-dir`, `--extensions-dir`, `--profile`, `--chat`, `--add-mcp <json>` (VS Code-derived; target file unverified) and an `agent` subcommand [observed: --help]. Linux/Windows data dirs `~/.config/Cursor`, `%APPDATA%\Cursor` [inferred: VS Code convention].
- **CLI:** binary `agent` (primary) and `cursor-agent` (legacy alias); `curl https://cursor.com/install -fsS | bash` installs `~/.local/share/cursor-agent/versions/<ver>/cursor-agent` and symlinks `~/.local/bin/{agent,cursor-agent}` (Windows `irm 'https://cursor.com/install?win32=true' | iex`); the IDE also keeps a private copy under `globalStorage/anysphere.cursor-agent-worker/agent-cli/` [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/cli/installation.md; observed]. `origin` in `~/.local/bin` is Cursor's git-host CLI, not an agent [observed: --help].
- **Env overrides:** `CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR` (else `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/cursor`, else `~/.cursor`) for `cli-config.json` [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/configuration.md]; `CURSOR_DATA_DIR` relocates `<dir>/projects/<slug>/` (undocumented, CLI bundle only); `CURSOR_API_KEY`, `CURSOR_AUTH_TOKEN`, `AGENT_CLI_CREDENTIAL_STORE=file`, `NO_OPEN_BROWSER`, `HTTPS_PROXY` [observed: CLI 2026.01.23 bundle; doc: https://cursor.com/docs/cli/changelog.md]. IDE: `--user-data-dir`, `--extensions-dir`. `<slug>` = workspace path with every `[^a-zA-Z0-9]` replaced by `-`, runs collapsed, edge dashes trimmed (lossy: decode by probing the filesystem; projectless chats use `empty-window`) [observed: CLI bundle; src: openai/codex@822e58c:codex-rs/external-agent-migration/src/detect/sessions/cur.rs#L36-L110].
- **Cross-agent imports:** Settings > Agents > Third-Party Imports "Include Third-Party Plugins, Skills, and Other Configs" (state key `cursor/thirdPartyExtensibilityEnabled`, default true in the 3.22.12 bundle, false here) and a separate "Import Claude Code Conversations" (`cursor/importClaudeCodeAgents`, default false; reads `~/.claude/projects/*/*.jsonl` into state.vscdb [3p: https://zenn.dev/honeymarron/articles/claude-code-cursor-session-handoff]); plugins from Claude are imported on boot (`cursor/importPluginsFromClaudeOnBoot` default true; `~/.cursor/plugins/{cache,marketplaces}/claude-plugins-official` exist) [observed; doc: https://cursor.com/docs/reference/third-party-hooks.md].

### 2. History location
- Already verified by lead: `~/.cursor/projects/<slug>/agent-transcripts/<id>/<id>.jsonl` and `state.vscdb` cursorDiskKV `composerData:<id>` / `bubbleId:<id>:<bubbleId>` [observed: verified by the lead].
- NEW: sub-agent transcripts `agent-transcripts/<id>/subagents/<sub-id>.jsonl` (703 files under 244 parents) [observed]; the CLI writes the same transcripts and keeps its own chat store `<configDir>/chats/<md5(cwd)>/<chatId>/store.db` (SQLite blob store, CLI 2026.01.23; absent here as the CLI is unused) [observed: CLI bundle; doc: https://cursor.com/docs/cli/changelog.md]; `globalStorage/conversation-search.db` is a derived FTS5 index added with conversation search in 3.11 (2026-07-10) (`conversations` + `conversation_fts(title, body, branches)`; 2,347 rows: 2,336 `local`, 11 `cloud-cache`; body avg 7 KB, max 161 KB, so possibly truncated [inferred]) [observed; doc: https://cursor.com/changelog/side-chat]; `~/.cursor/ai-tracking/ai-code-tracking.db` links `conversationId` to AI-written file hashes (`conversation_summaries` is empty) [observed].

### 3. Storage format
| Concept | Field / column (beyond lead's `composerData` / `bubbleId`) |
|---|---|
| session id | `composerId` = hook `conversation_id` = transcript dir/file name = `composerHeaders.composerId` [doc: hooks.md; observed] |
| title | `composerData.name`; `composerHeaders.value` JSON; `conversation-search.db conversations.title` [observed] |
| project / cwd | `composerHeaders.workspaceId`; `composerData.workspaceIdentifier`, `trackedGitRepos`; `<slug>` of the transcript dir; hook `workspace_roots[]` [observed; doc] |
| created / updated | `composerHeaders.createdAt/lastUpdatedAt/checkpointAt/recency` (ms) [observed] |
| user / assistant text, tools | bubbles ordered by `composerData.fullConversationHeadersOnly`, or the transcript JSONL (lead); hook `transcript_path` points at the transcript [doc: hooks.md] |
| other | `isArchived`, `isSubagent`, `subagentComposerIds`, `status`, `modelConfig`, `usageData`, `unifiedMode` [observed] |

- **Format changes (NEW):** the 3.22.12 global `state.vscdb` has three tables: `ItemTable`, `cursorDiskKV` and **`composerHeaders(composerId PK, workspaceId, createdAt, lastUpdatedAt, isArchived, isSubagent, recency, checkpointAt, value TEXT, subagentTypeName)`** (2,059 rows), which replaced the ItemTable blob `composer.composerHeaders` `{allComposers[...]}` (migration backups `~/.cursor/chat-migration-backups/20260806-*{,-agentLocation,-fill-locations,-to-glass}`; "glass" = the Agents Window shipped with Cursor 3.0 on 2026-04-02) [observed; doc: https://cursor.com/docs/agent/agents-window.md]. New cursorDiskKV families: `agentKv:blob:<hex>` (598k agent-runtime blobs; some high-entropy and `composerData.blobEncryptionKey` exists, so treat as opaque), `checkpointId:`, `messageRequestContext:`, `codeBlockDiff:` [observed]. Composers with no `bubbleId` rows are empty drafts (514/514 non-subagent; `conversationState` length at most 1) and can be skipped; transcripts exist for 644 conversations, mostly since ~2026-03 [observed].
- **Cursor 3.x timeline (docs):** 3.0 on 2026-04-02 Agents Window; 3.4 on 2026-05-13; 3.11 on 2026-07-10 side chats + local conversation search; 2026-06-04 SDK stores (SQLite/JSONL); 2026-08-19 subscriptions, custom modes, `/goal`; CLI 2026-08-26 `agent persist` [doc: https://cursor.com/changelog; https://cursor.com/docs/cli/changelog.md].
- **Incremental import:** use `composerHeaders` as the change feed (`lastUpdatedAt`/`checkpointAt` greater than a stored mark); state.vscdb is about 12 GB in WAL mode (open `mode=ro`); transcripts are appended per checkpoint (CLI saves only new entries) so track size/mtime, and a `stop` hook receives `transcript_path` for event-driven ingest [observed; doc: https://cursor.com/docs/cli/changelog.md].


#### Lead census (local, structure-only)
- Global `~/Library/Application Support/Cursor/User/globalStorage/state.vscdb` is ~12 GB with tables `ItemTable`, `cursorDiskKV`, and a newer `composerHeaders` [observed]. Open it read-only (`file:...?mode=ro`; the schema census here used `immutable=1`, which hides uncheckpointed WAL rows) and never copy it.
- `cursorDiskKV(key TEXT, value BLOB)` key prefixes by row count: `agentKv:blob:<hex>` (~598k content-addressed blobs; the new agent runtime's conversation state, format not decoded), `bubbleId:` (~306k), `checkpointId:`, `codeBlockPartialInlineDiffFates:`, `codeBlockDiff:`, `ofsContent:`, `composerData:` (~3.9k), `messageRequestContext:`, `inlineDiff:`, `composerVirtualRowHeights:`, `composerRootWriterDiagnostic:`, `composerDraft:`, `bcCachedDetails:` [observed].
- `composerHeaders` columns: `composerId`, `workspaceId`, `createdAt`, `lastUpdatedAt`, `isArchived`, `isSubagent`, `recency`, `checkpointAt`, `value` (JSON text), `subagentTypeName`; ~2.1k rows. This is a cheap index of sessions (title and workspace live in `value`/workspace mapping) [observed].
- `globalStorage/conversation-search.db` (~75 MB, SQLite): Cursor's own search index. `conversations(fts_rowid, source CHECK IN ('local','cloud-cache'), scope, id, title, branches, updated_at, is_archived, root_fingerprint, cache_fingerprint)` plus FTS5 virtual table `conversation_fts(title, body, branches)` (tokenizer `unicode61 remove_diacritics 2`, prefix 2 3) and `conversation_search_candidates(id, updated_at)`, `conversation_search_reconciliation`, `conversation_search_settings(effective_conversation_cap)`; ~2.3k rows, `source='local'` for local chats and `'cloud-cache'` for cloud agents [observed]. It exposes title + searchable body for every conversation, including cloud-agent ones whose full transcript is not on disk; `body` completeness/truncation was not checked.
- The `agent-transcripts/<id>/<id>.jsonl` files remain the simplest source for text (lead-verified); `composerData` / `bubbleId` cover older composers.

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Local data is plaintext (lead); `agentKv` blobs are opaque and cloud-agent conversations live server-side (only cached as `cloud-cache`) [observed; doc: https://cursor.com/docs/cloud-agent.md]. No end-user export; the Cursor SDK exposes `run.conversation()` for SDK runs and a pluggable `LocalAgentStore` (SQLite or JSONL) for SDK agents (`<slug>/sdk-agent-store` seen once) [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/sdk/typescript.md; observed].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP:** `~/.cursor/mcp.json` (user; auto-approved in the CLI) and `<project>/.cursor/mcp.json` (needs approval, stored in `~/.cursor/projects/<slug>/mcp-approvals.json`); interpolation `${env:NAME}`, `${userHome}`, `${workspaceFolder}`, `${workspaceFolderBasename}`, `${pathSeparator}`; stdio keys `type`, `command`, `args`, `env`, `envFile`; remote keys `url`, `headers`, `auth{CLIENT_ID, CLIENT_SECRET, scopes}`. CLI: `agent mcp login|list|list-tools|enable|disable` (no `add`; community confirms [3p: https://forum.cursor.com/t/can-cursor-cli-support-add-mcp-server-parameter/167812]); `--approve-mcps` for headless; extension API `vscode.cursor.mcp.registerServer()` [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/mcp.md; https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/parameters.md; observed].
```json
{ "mcpServers": { "recall": { "command": "recall", "args": ["serve"], "env": {} } } }
```
- **Global instructions:** project `.cursor/rules/**/*.mdc` (frontmatter `description`, `globs`, `alwaysApply`; plain `.md` ignored), `AGENTS.md` at root and nested; the CLI also applies root `CLAUDE.md`; User Rules exist only in Settings > Customize > Rules (no file), Team Rules via dashboard; precedence Team > Project > User [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/rules.md; https://cursor.com/docs/cli/using.md].
- **Skills:** `.agents/skills`, `.cursor/skills`, `~/.agents/skills`, `~/.cursor/skills`, plus compat `.claude/skills`, `.codex/skills`, `~/.claude/skills`, `~/.codex/skills`; nested `.cursor/skills` in subdirs; frontmatter `name` (= folder), `description`, optional `paths`, `disable-model-invocation`, `icon`, `color`, `metadata`; managed built-ins in `~/.cursor/skills-cursor/` [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/skills.md; observed]. Subagents: `.cursor/agents/*.md` (+ `.claude/agents`, `.codex/agents`, user equivalents) [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/subagents.md].
- **Hooks:** files `~/.cursor/hooks.json`, `<project>/.cursor/hooks.json` (trusted workspaces), team (dashboard), enterprise `/Library/Application Support/Cursor/hooks.json` | `/etc/cursor/hooks.json` | `C:\ProgramData\Cursor\hooks.json`, plugin `hooks/hooks.json`; all sources run and responses merge (deny > ask > allow; messages concatenated) [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/hooks.md]. Schema `{"version":1,"hooks":{"<event>":[{"command":"..","type":"command|prompt","timeout":s,"matcher":"regex","loop_limit":5,"failClosed":false}]}}`; user hooks run with cwd `~/.cursor/`, project hooks from the project root [doc: hooks.md]. Events (21): `sessionStart`, `sessionEnd`, `preToolUse`, `postToolUse`, `postToolUseFailure`, `subagentStart`, `subagentStop`, `beforeShellExecution`, `afterShellExecution`, `beforeMCPExecution`, `afterMCPExecution`, `beforeReadFile`, `afterFileEdit`, `beforeSubmitPrompt`, `preCompact`, `stop`, `afterAgentResponse`, `afterAgentThought`, Tab `beforeTabFileRead`/`afterTabFileEdit`, app `workspaceOpen` (returns `pluginPaths`) [doc: hooks.md]. stdin JSON common: `conversation_id`, `generation_id`, `model`, `model_id`, `model_params`, `hook_event_name`, `cursor_version`, `workspace_roots`, `user_email`, `transcript_path`; sessionStart adds `session_id`, `is_background_agent`, `composer_mode`; stop adds `status`, `loop_count`; beforeSubmitPrompt adds `prompt`, `attachments`; env `CURSOR_PROJECT_DIR`, `CURSOR_VERSION`, `CURSOR_USER_EMAIL`, `CURSOR_TRANSCRIPT_PATH`, `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` [doc: hooks.md]. Output must be JSON (exit 0; exit 2 denies; permission hooks block on invalid JSON); plain-text stdout raises a parse error [doc: hooks.md; 3p: https://github.com/rtk-ai/icm/issues/120].
  - **Context injection (verified live 2026-10-06):** `sessionStart` returns `{env, additional_context}` (documented as added to the initial system context, yet "fire-and-forget"); Cursor staff acknowledged a timing bug where it is dropped on a new chat (Apr-May 2026), still reported on 3.14.x (Aug 2026) and broken for the SDK local runtime (1.0.28) [3p: https://forum.cursor.com/t/sessionstart-hook-additional-context-is-never-injected-into-agents-initial-system-context/158452; https://github.com/mem0ai/mem0/issues/6844; https://forum.cursor.com/t/cursor-sdk-1-0-28-sessionstart-runs-but-additional-context-never-reaches-the-model/168441]; the 3.22.12 bundle carries `hooks_additional_context` in the agent request and the CLI 2026.01.23 code appears to await the sessionStart result before the first turn [observed; inferred from minified code]. `postToolUse`/`postToolUseFailure` `additional_context` is documented as injected after the tool result (community: discarded for non-MCP tools [3p: https://forum.cursor.com/t/native-posttooluse-hooks-accept-and-log-additional-context-successfully-but-the-injected-context-is-not-surfaced-to-the-model/155689]). `beforeSubmitPrompt` only returns `{continue, user_message}`: NO injection. `stop`/`subagentStop` `followup_message` auto-submits the next user message (loop_limit default 5). `sessionEnd`/`afterAgent*`/`preCompact` are observe-only [doc: hooks.md].
  - **Third-party Claude hooks:** with the toggle on, Cursor also loads `~/.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.local.json` and maps `PreToolUse, PostToolUse, UserPromptSubmit (to beforeSubmitPrompt), Stop, SubagentStop, SessionStart, SessionEnd, PreCompact` (Notification/PermissionRequest unsupported), accepting nested `hookSpecificOutput`; so a recall hook registered for Claude Code can also fire in Cursor (double-injection risk; a Claude `UserPromptSubmit additionalContext` will not inject there [inferred from the beforeSubmitPrompt contract]); detect Cursor via payload `cursor_version` / env `CURSOR_VERSION` [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/reference/third-party-hooks.md]. Toggle also loads skills from `.claude/.codex` dirs, subagents from `.claude/agents`, and (bundle strings) `CLAUDE.md`/`CLAUDE.local.md`, `.claude/commands`, `.claude/plugins`, `.grok/skills`; MCP import from `.mcp.json`/`~/.claude.json` is not documented or found in the bundle [observed; doc].
  - **CLI/cloud:** the CLI loads enterprise, team, user and project hooks plus Claude settings when enabled, passes the sessionStart payload (`conversation_id, generation_id, model, session_id, is_background_agent, composer_mode`), and sends payloads on stdin; plugin hooks run from installed plugins and `--plugin-dir`; cloud agents run only project/team/enterprise command hooks (no sessionStart/sessionEnd/MCP hooks) [observed: CLI bundle; doc: https://cursor.com/docs/cli/changelog.md; hooks.md].
- **Plugins:** Cursor Plugin = `.cursor-plugin/plugin.json` (`name` required; `description, version, author, homepage, repository, license, keywords, logo, rules, agents, skills, commands, hooks, mcpServers, variables`); Agent Plugin = root `plugin.json` with `$schema` (skills + MCP only); default dirs `rules/`, `skills/`, `agents/`, `commands/`, `hooks/hooks.json`, `mcp.json`; `${CURSOR_PLUGIN_ROOT}`/`${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` expand (not `${PLUGIN_ROOT}`). Local dev dir `~/.cursor/plugins/local/<name>`; registry `~/.cursor/plugins/installed.json` `{version, user[], projects{}, team{}, local{}}`; cache `~/.cursor/plugins/cache/<marketplace>/<plugin>/<hash>`; marketplace manifest `.cursor-plugin/marketplace.json`; CLI `--plugin-dir`, `/plugin marketplace add <git-url>`, `~/.cursor/settings.json` `enabled_plugins` [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/plugins.md; https://cursor.com/docs/reference/plugins.md; observed]. Cursor's own `continual-learning` plugin (a `stop` hook that reads `transcript_path` and returns `followup_message` to update AGENTS.md) is the first-party pattern for memory [observed].
- **Slash commands:** `.cursor/commands/*.md` (project) and `~/.cursor/commands/*.md` (user; 2 files here); no standalone docs page remains and `/migrate-to-skills` converts them to skills [observed; doc: https://cursor.com/docs/skills.md].
- **Headless:** `agent -p "<prompt>"` with `--output-format text|json|stream-json`, `--stream-partial-output`, `--model`, `--mode plan|ask`, `-f/--force/--yolo`, `--sandbox enabled|disabled`, `--approve-mcps`, `--trust`, `--workspace`, `--resume [chatId]`, `--continue`, `-w/--worktree`, `--plugin-dir`, `--api-key`/`CURSOR_API_KEY`; `agent ls|resume|create-chat|acp`; stream-json events `system(init){session_id,cwd,model,permissionMode}`, `user`, `assistant`, `tool_call{started|completed}`, `result{success,duration_ms,result,session_id,request_id}` [doc: https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/parameters.md; https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/output-format.md; observed: --help of 2026.01.23 lacks `--plugin-dir`/`--trust`].

**Best recall injection point:** no reliable prompt-time hook exists. Least fragile: an always-applied project rule (`.cursor/rules/recall.mdc` with `alwaysApply: true`) or an `AGENTS.md` block telling the agent to call the recall MCP tool first (works in IDE and CLI; a User Rule can only be set in the Settings UI); secondary: `~/.cursor/hooks.json` `sessionStart` returning a tiny `additional_context` immediately (race risk), plus a `stop` hook that ingests `transcript_path` incrementally. Register MCP by writing `~/.cursor/mcp.json` `mcpServers.recall` (no `agent mcp add`); guard against double hook firing via the Claude-import toggle.
**Confidence / unknowns:** high on hooks/rules/skills/plugins/MCP (live docs 2026-10-06) and local storage structure; medium on sessionStart reliability in 3.22.12 and on exactly what the third-party toggle imports (bundle strings, not behaviour); unverified: `cursor --add-mcp` target file, latest CLI (2026.10.01) behaviour, Linux/Windows data paths.

---

## OpenCode (Anomaly, formerly SST / anomalyco/opencode)


The desktop app (`ai.opencode.desktop`) bundles `Contents/Resources/opencode-cli` [observed]. `~/.local/share/opencode/` holds `opencode.db` (with `-wal` and `-shm`), `storage/`, `snapshot/`, `log/`, `bin/` and `auth.json` (never read); `~/.config/opencode/` holds `skills/` symlinks into `~/.agents/skills`. The DB uses the v1 shape (six drizzle migrations, no v2 tables) [observed].
**Version verified:** two live release lines. Stable CLI v1.18.34 (2026-09-30; dev HEAD `3f393d7`, 2026-10-06) [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/opencode/package.json]; v2 line tags v2.0.15 (`6f3639d`, 2026-09-23, the installed app) [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:package.json#L5] to v2.0.24 (`e7a34f0`, 2026-10-06) [doc: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/tags]; v2 tags are not GitHub Releases (`releases/tags/v2.0.15` returns 404 via the GitHub API; the newest Release is v1.18.34, matching the changelog page; the desktop updater uses the opencode.ai feed). [doc: https://opencode.ai/changelog] [observed] Canonical repo is `github.com/anomalyco/opencode` (`gh api repos/sst/opencode` resolves to it; default branch `dev`; homepage opencode.ai). Docs: `opencode.ai/docs` (v1) and `opencode.ai/v2/docs` (v2, source `services/www/src/docs/content`); the v1 docs index announces "OpenCode v2" [doc: https://opencode.ai/docs/].

### 1. Detection
- macOS: `/Applications/OpenCode.app`, `ai.opencode.desktop`; sidecar `Contents/Resources/opencode-cli`; update feed `https://opencode.ai/update/api/latest/desktop/opencode/` (`channel: latest`). [observed] The app is Electron (`packages/desktop`, electron-builder) and spawns `<cli> serve --service`, so desktop and CLI share one database. [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/desktop/src/main/service/background-service.ts#L47]
- CLI binary `opencode`. v2 installs: `curl -fsSL https://opencode.ai/v2/install | bash`, `brew install anomalyco/tap/opencode-v2`, `npm i -g @opencode/cli`; v1 and v2 share the command name and clash on PATH. [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/migrate-v1.mdx] No CLI install path exists here to check.
- Relocation env: `XDG_DATA_HOME` (data = `$XDG_DATA_HOME/opencode`, else `~/.local/share/opencode`), `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`, `OPENCODE_DB` (filename relative to data dir, absolute path, or `:memory:`), `OPENCODE_DISABLE_CHANNEL_DB`, `OPENCODE_CONFIG`, `OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR`, `OPENCODE_CONFIG_CONTENT`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_PROJECT_CONFIG`, `OPENCODE_TEST_HOME`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/core/src/global.ts#L10-L31]
- DB filename is `opencode.db` for channels latest/beta/prod (v2 also dev/next), else `opencode-<channel>.db`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/core/src/database/database.ts#L43-L55] [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/cli/src/database-path.ts#L3-L11] `opencode db path` (v1) / `opencode debug paths [db]` (v2) print it. [doc: https://opencode.ai/docs/cli/] [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/cli/commands.mdx#L500-L530]

### 2. History location
- macOS and Linux: `~/.local/share/opencode/opencode.db`. [observed] [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/core/src/global.ts#L10-L31] Windows: same `xdg-basedir` rule per a 3rd party [3p: SpecStory getspecstory specstory-cli/pkg/providers/opencode/OPENCODE-FORMAT.md], but the v1.2.0 notes say "%APPDATA% on windows"; unresolved. [doc: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/releases/tag/v1.2.0]
- Legacy JSON (pre-1.2) still on disk: `storage/{project,session/<projectID>,message/<sessionID>,part/<messageID>,todo,session_diff}/*.json` + 1-byte `storage/migration` counter (here `2`). Counts equal the DB (8/332/1406/3 files; 8 session_diff, 3 todo), so it is a stale duplicate. [observed] [src: anomalyco/opencode@ffc000d:packages/opencode/src/storage/storage.ts#L145-L154]
- Other: `snapshot/<project-hash>/` (git snapshots), `log/`, `bin/`; v2 adds `shell/<project-id>/`, `tool-output/`, `repos/`. [observed] [3p: SpecStory OPENCODE-FORMAT.md]
- Desktop UI state `~/Library/Application Support/ai.opencode.desktop/`: `opencode.global.dat`, `opencode.settings.dat` (`{}`), `opencode.workspace.<b64>.<hash>.dat`, `default.dat`, `.window-state.json`; JSON key to string-encoded-JSON stores (keys `prompt-history`, `layout`, `server`, `globalSync.project`, `command.catalog.v1`, `model`, `workspace:prompt`, `workspace:terminal`). UI state only, not transcripts. [observed] [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/desktop/src/main/storage/store.ts#L7-L42]

### 3. Storage format
Single SQLite file, WAL mode (`journal_mode=WAL`, `busy_timeout=5000`, `foreign_keys=ON`). [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/core/src/database/database.ts#L27-L32] Local file header bytes 18/19 = 2 (WAL). [observed] Timestamps are epoch ms; ids are `ses_`/`msg_`/`prt_` + 26 time-ordered chars. [observed]

| Concept | v1 line (tables `session`,`message`,`part`; local DB) | v2 line (`session_v2`,`session_message`; desktop 2.0.x) |
|---|---|---|
| session id / title | `session.id`, `session.title` (`slug` empty on migrated rows) | `session_v2.id`, `session_v2.title` (nullable), `slug` |
| project / cwd | `session.directory`, `session.project_id` -> `project.worktree`; `project.id` = root-commit hash or `global` | same, plus `session_v2.path`, `workspace_id` |
| created / updated | `session.time_created`, `time_updated` (+`time_archived`, `time_compacting`) | same, plus `time_idle`, `time_viewed` |
| subagent link | `session.parent_id` (6 of 8 local sessions are children) | `parent_id`, `fork_session_id` |
| user text | `part.data` `$.type='text'` -> `$.text`, where the owning `message.data.$.role='user'` | `session_message.data.$.text` where `type='user'` (+`files[]`,`agents[]`,`skills[]`) |
| assistant text | `part.data` `type='text'` (`text`, `time{start,end}`, `synthetic?`); `type='reasoning'` (`text`; OpenAI `metadata.openai.reasoningEncryptedContent`) | `type='assistant'` -> `data.content[]` items `text`/`reasoning`/`tool` |
| tool call | `part.data` `type='tool'`: `callID`, `tool`, `state.input{...}` | content item `type:'tool'`: `id`, `name`, `state.input` |
| tool result | `state.status` (`completed`/`error`), `state.output` (str), `state.title`, `state.metadata`, `state.time{start,end,compacted}`, `state.error` | `state.status` streaming/running/completed/error; `state.content[]` (`{type:text,text}` or `{type:file,uri,mime,name}`), `state.error` |
| model / usage | assistant `message.data`: `providerID`,`modelID`,`agent`,`mode`,`cost`,`tokens{input,output,reasoning,cache{read,write}}`,`finish`,`path{cwd,root}`,`error`,`time{created,completed}`; user: `model{providerID,modelID}`,`agent`,`summary{title,body,diffs[]}`,`tools{}` | assistant: `agent`,`model{id,providerID,variant}`,`tokens`,`cost`,`finish`,`snapshot`,`error`,`retry`,`time{created,streamed,completed}`; session cols `cost`,`tokens_*`,`agent`,`model` |
| other records | part types seen locally: `step-start`,`step-finish`,`tool`,`text`,`patch`,`reasoning`,`file`; tables `todo`,`session_share`,`permission` | message `type`: `user`,`assistant`,`synthetic`,`system`,`skill`,`shell`,`compaction`,`idle`,`agent-switched`,`model-switched`,`location-switched` |

Id/ordering columns: v1 `message.data` and `part.data` do not repeat `id`/`sessionID`/`messageID` (they are columns), unlike the legacy JSON files, which do. [observed] v2 orders by `session_message.seq` (unique per session; sparse), and `event`/`event_sequence` hold the event log. [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/core/src/session/sql.ts#L79-L98] [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/core/src/event/sql.ts#L4-L26] v2 message shapes: [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/schema/src/session-message.ts#L32-L320]. v1 tables: [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/core/src/session/sql.ts#L22-L117].

**Format changes across versions.**
- Pre-v1.2.0 (to Feb 2026): JSON files. v1.2.0 (2026-02-14) switched to one SQLite DB and migrates "all flat files" on first run; the originals are not deleted, and deleting `opencode.db*` re-triggers it. [doc: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/releases/tag/v1.2.0] The migrator globs `project/*.json`, `session/*/*.json`, `message/*/*.json`, `part/*/*.json`, `todo/*.json`, `permission/*.json`, `session_share/*.json` and derives ids from paths. [src: anomalyco/opencode@ffc000d:packages/opencode/src/storage/json-migration.ts#L113-L122] v1.2.6 added `opencode db migrate` ("merges with existing data"); gone by 1.18.34. [src: anomalyco/opencode@v1.2.6:packages/opencode/src/cli/cmd/db.ts#L64-L65] An earlier SQLite attempt shipped around v1.1.61/1.1.65 (migration progress UI) and v1.2.0 says "Revert to SQLite database implementation". [doc: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/releases/tag/v1.2.0]
- v2 (2.0.x, Sep 2026) is event-sourced. On first start `V1Migration` copies each `session`/`message`/`part` set into `session_v2`/`session_message` inside the same `opencode.db` (progress in `kv`; may also import a pre-release `opencode-next.db`) and does not drop the legacy tables. [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/core/src/database/v1-migration.bun.ts#L516-L600] [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/core/src/database/v1-migration.bun.ts#L684-L688] v2.0.15 ships 48 drizzle migrations and dev/1.18.34 ships 38 (this DB has 6; either line should apply the rest in place on next launch [inferred: standard drizzle migrator behaviour]). [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/core/src/database/migration] The dev/1.18 line shares `packages/core` and keeps writing `session`/`message`/`part`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/core/src/session/sql.ts#L22-L98]
- Importer must read both table families and de-duplicate by session id (migrated sessions exist in both; prefer `session_v2`). [inferred from the migration code above]

**Incremental-import notes.** Open with `mode=ro` (not `immutable=1`: immutable ignores the `-wal`, which holds the newest rows; the schema checks here used immutable, so counts above exclude the 511 KB WAL). [observed] [inferred: SQLite URI semantics] In v2 a background service is the only writer and rewrites the assistant row in place while streaming. [3p: SpecStory OPENCODE-FORMAT.md] Cheap change detection: v1 `session.time_updated`/`message.time_updated`; v2 `session_v2.time_updated`, newest `session_message.time_updated`, `event_sequence.seq`. [inferred from schemas above]

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Plaintext SQLite and JSON; nothing encrypted. Secrets are in `auth.json` and tables `control_account`/`credential`/`account` (never read). OpenAI reasoning parts carry provider-encrypted `reasoningEncryptedContent`. [observed] [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/core/src/database/migration/20260611035744_credential.ts]
- Export: v1 `opencode export [sessionID] [--sanitize]` prints `{info: Session, messages: [{info: Message, parts: Part[]}]}`; `opencode import <file|share URL>`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/opencode/src/cli/cmd/import.ts#L56-L92] v2 `opencode session export <id> [--sanitize]` / `session import <file> [--directory]` use `{info, messages: SessionMessage[]}` (settled messages, asc). [src: anomalyco/opencode@6f3639d:packages/core/src/session/transfer.ts#L59-L77] `opencode session list --max-count N --format json`, `opencode stats`, `opencode db "<SQL>" --format json|tsv` (v1). [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/web/src/content/docs/cli.mdx#L410-L433]
- HTTP: v1 `opencode serve` -> `GET /session`, `GET /session/:id/message`, `GET /global/event` (SSE), basic auth via `OPENCODE_SERVER_PASSWORD`; v2 `opencode api GET /api/session`, `@opencode/client` `client.session.list()`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/web/src/content/docs/server.mdx#L94-L178] [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/cli/commands.mdx]

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP.** Files: `~/.config/opencode/opencode.json(c)`, `<project>/opencode.json(c)`, `<project>/.opencode/opencode.json(c)`; also `OPENCODE_CONFIG`, `OPENCODE_CONFIG_CONTENT`. v2 reads the same files and still accepts the v1 shape, so write v1: `{"$schema":"https://opencode.ai/config.json","mcp":{"recall":{"type":"local","command":["recall","serve"],"environment":{},"enabled":true}}}` (remote: `type:"remote"`, `url`, `headers`). v2 native shape is `mcp.servers.<name>` with `disabled` instead of `enabled`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/migrate-v1.mdx] [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/mcp-servers.mdx] v2 CLI: `opencode mcp add <name> [--global] [--env K=V] -- <cmd...>` or `--url`; v1 has an `mcp` command group (flags not checked). [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/web/src/content/docs/cli.mdx#L224]
- **Global instructions.** `~/.config/opencode/AGENTS.md` plus `AGENTS.md` files walked up from the cwd. v1 falls back to project `CLAUDE.md` (when no AGENTS.md) and `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` unless `OPENCODE_DISABLE_CLAUDE_CODE[_PROMPT]`, and honours config `instructions: [files, globs, URLs]`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/web/src/content/docs/rules.mdx#L65-L131] v2 reads AGENTS.md only (no CLAUDE.md fallback), picks up mid-session edits as an "instruction update", and accepts but does not yet resolve `instructions` (as of v2.0.24). [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/instructions.mdx]
- **Skills.** Global `~/.config/opencode/skills`, compat `~/.claude/skills`, `~/.agents/skills`; project `.opencode/skills`, `.claude/skills`, `.agents/skills` (walks up to project root); `skills` config array adds paths/URLs; layout `<id>/SKILL.md`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/skills.mdx]
- **Hooks.** No shell-command hooks and no hook config file; hooks live in plugins. Event names (via the `event` hook) include `session.created`, `session.idle`, `session.compacted`, `message.updated`, `tool.execute.before/after`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/web/src/content/docs/plugins.mdx#L142-L210]
- **Plugins (v1, CLI 1.18).** JS/TS in `.opencode/plugins/` or `~/.config/opencode/plugins/`, or npm names in config `plugin`; the exported function returns `Hooks`: `event`, `config`, `tool`, `auth`, `provider`, `chat.message`, `chat.params`, `chat.headers`, `permission.ask`, `command.execute.before`, `tool.execute.before/after`, `shell.env`, `experimental.chat.messages.transform`, `experimental.chat.system.transform`, `experimental.session.compacting`, `experimental.compaction.autocontinue`, `experimental.text.complete`, `tool.definition`. Context-injecting: `experimental.chat.system.transform` (push to `output.system`, per LLM call), `chat.message` (edit `output.message/parts` of the new user message), `experimental.chat.messages.transform`, `experimental.session.compacting` (`output.context`). `session.created` is an event only (no injection by itself). [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/plugin/src/index.ts#L222-L335]
- **Plugins (v2).** v1 plugins do not run in v2. `export default Plugin.define({id, async setup(ctx){...}})` from `@opencode/plugin`; config key `plugins`; local files in `.opencode/plugins/` (also `plugin/`) and `~/.config/opencode/plugins/`; `opencode plugin add <pkg>`. Hooks: `ctx.session.hook("prompt")` (before durable admission; edit prompt text/files, runs once per prompt), `ctx.session.hook("context")` (before each model request; `event.system.push({type:"text",text})`, edit messages/tools/options), `"compaction"`, `"generate"`, `"title"`, `"model.request"`, plus `ctx.event.subscribe()`, `ctx.session.synthetic()/prompt()`. Context-injecting: `prompt` and `context`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/build/plugins/migrate-v1.mdx] [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/build/plugins/index.mdx#L1281-L1345]
- **Slash commands / agents.** `~/.config/opencode/commands/*.md`, `.opencode/commands/*.md` (`$ARGUMENTS`; nested dirs become `a/b`; v1 also accepts singular `command/`); config `command` (v1) / `commands` (v2). Agents: `~/.config/opencode/agents/*.md`, `.opencode/agents/*.md`. [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/commands.mdx] [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/web/src/content/docs/config.mdx#L58]
- **Headless.** `opencode run "<msg>" [--format json (raw events)] [-c] [-s <id>] [--fork] [--model p/m] [--agent a] [-f file] [--attach <url>] [--dir d] [--title t]`; v2 adds `--standalone` (private server) / `--server <url>`; `opencode serve [--port --hostname]`, `opencode acp`, `opencode mini` (v2). [src: anomalyco/opencode@3f393d7:packages/web/src/content/docs/cli.mdx#L339-L387] [src: anomalyco/opencode@e7a34f0:services/www/src/docs/content/cli/commands.mdx]

**Best recall injection point:** write the memory preamble into `~/.config/opencode/AGENTS.md` (loaded by both lines at session start; v2 re-injects edits mid-session) and register MCP by merging `mcp.<name>` into `~/.config/opencode/opencode.json(c)` (v1 shape works for v1 and v2). For per-prompt/dynamic memory ship a plugin: v1 `experimental.chat.system.transform`, v2 `ctx.session.hook("context")` (two separate code paths).
**Confidence / unknowns:** high for the v1 DB/JSON (local + source); v2 shapes come from v2.0.15/2.0.24 source and one 3rd-party doc since no v2-written DB exists here; not checked: v1 part types beyond those seen locally (agent/subtask/compaction/retry), Windows data dir, v1 `opencode mcp add` flags.

## T3 Code (T3 Tools / pingdotgg/t3code)


**Version verified:** v0.0.45 stable (2026-10-02); nightlies 0.0.46; `pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80` (main, 2026-10-06); site t3.codes; docs in-repo `docs/user/*`. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/desktop/package.json#L3]

### 1. Detection
- What it is: an "agent harness control surface" (Electron desktop, web app, iOS/Android) that drives agents installed on the machine; README lists Codex, Claude Code, Cursor, Grok Build, OpenCode, Antigravity, and the source also has a `PiProvider`. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:README.md#L3-L24] [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/provider/PiProvider.ts]
- macOS app `T3 Code (Alpha).app` (nightly `T3 Code (Nightly)`), CFBundleIdentifier `com.t3tools.t3code`; Homebrew cask `t3-code`; also `~/Library/Application Support/T3 Code (Alpha)`. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:scripts/build-desktop-artifact.ts#L57] [3p: Homebrew/homebrew-cask Casks/t/t3-code.rb] CLI `t3` (`curl -fsSL https://t3.codes/install.sh | sh`, `npx t3@latest`, `t3 service install`). [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:README.md#L28-L36]
- Base dir `~/.t3`, override `T3CODE_HOME` or `--base-dir`; runtime state under `<base>/userdata` (`<base>/dev` in dev builds). Other env: `T3CODE_PORT`, `T3CODE_HOST`, `T3CODE_LOG_LEVEL`, `T3CODE_NO_BROWSER`. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/os-jank.ts#L104-L112] [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/desktop/src/app/DesktopStatePaths.ts#L18-L31]

### 2. History location
- T3's own DB: `~/.t3/userdata/statev2.sqlite`; also `settings.json`, `keybindings.json`, `attachments/`, `logs/`, `secrets/`, `browser-artifacts/`, worktrees in `~/.t3/worktrees`. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/config.ts#L137-L164] The v1 `state.sqlite` is left in place when v2 first starts. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:docs/internals/legacy-orchestration-migration.md]
- The conversations themselves are stored by the wrapped agents: Claude runs through the user's installed `claude` binary and the Claude Agent SDK (`getSessionMessages`, `forkSession`) with config dir = instance `homePath` -> `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` -> `~/.claude`; Codex uses `CODEX_HOME` (default `~/.codex`, shared with an optional "shadow home" for second accounts). So the importer should read Claude/Codex/etc. stores (existing connectors), and use T3's DB only for titles, project/branch/worktree and the thread-to-provider-thread mapping. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/provider/Drivers/ClaudeHome.ts#L10-L30] [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/claudeHistoryWorker.ts#L1-L32] [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:docs/user/providers-codex.md] [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:docs/user/providers-claude.md#L1-L25]

### 3. Storage format
Event-sourced SQLite (`statev2.sqlite`); timestamps are ISO-8601 TEXT; `payload_json` key shapes were NOT inspected.

| Concept | v2 table.column (migration 055) |
|---|---|
| thread id / title | `orchestration_v2_projection_threads.thread_id`, `title` |
| project | `.project_id` (project rows: legacy `projection_projects.title`, `workspace_root`) |
| created / updated / archived / deleted | `created_at`, `updated_at`, `archived_at`, `deleted_at` |
| provider | `default_provider`, `active_provider_thread_id`; `orchestration_v2_projection_provider_threads(provider_thread_id, thread_id, provider, provider_session_id, status)` |
| user / assistant text | `orchestration_v2_projection_messages(message_id, thread_id, run_id, role, streaming, created_at, updated_at, payload_json)` |
| tool calls / results | `orchestration_v2_projection_turn_items(turn_item_id, thread_id, run_id, provider_turn_id, ordinal, type, status, payload_json)` |
| event log (incremental) | `orchestration_v2_events(sequence AUTOINCREMENT, event_id, thread_id, run_id, provider, event_type, occurred_at, payload_json)` |

[src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/persistence/Migrations/055_OrchestrationV2.ts#L23-L301] Legacy v1 (`state.sqlite`): `orchestration_events`, `projection_threads(thread_id,project_id,title,model,branch,worktree_path,...)`, `projection_thread_messages(message_id,thread_id,turn_id,role,text,is_streaming,...)`, `projection_thread_activities(kind,tone,summary,payload_json)`, `projection_thread_sessions(provider_name,provider_session_id,provider_thread_id)`. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/persistence/Migrations/005_Projections.ts#L8-L110]
**Format changes:** Orchestration v2 copies `state.sqlite` to `statev2.sqlite` on first launch, imports thread shells first and the user/assistant transcript lazily when a client reads or continues the thread, and does not translate provider session identity, tool calls/activities, approvals or plans; so a v2 DB can hold shells without transcripts. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:docs/internals/legacy-orchestration-migration.md] Client/server protocol version 2 is enforced over `/ws`. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:docs/internals/legacy-orchestration-migration.md]
**Incremental-import notes:** `orchestration_v2_events.sequence` is monotonic, so `WHERE sequence > ?` is a cheap cursor; open read-only (WAL status not verified). Prefer importing from the wrapped agents and treat T3 rows as metadata. [inferred from the schema above]

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- No encryption seen in the schema; secrets are in `userdata/secrets/` (never read). No export command found; threads are served to clients over a versioned WebSocket API (not investigated). [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/config.ts#L137-L164]

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP / instructions / skills / hooks.** T3 Code has no user-facing registry for them: it "uses Claude Code's login and configuration" and Codex's own login/config, so MCP, CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md, skills and hooks are the wrapped agent's (configure those agents directly); per-instance `homePath`/environment variables are set in Settings > Providers. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:docs/user/providers-claude.md#L1-L25] T3 itself injects an internal stdio MCP toolkit (`t3 acp-mcp-bridge`) into ACP-based agents. [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/cli/acpMcpBridge.ts#L1-L25] Whether the wrapped Claude's user/project `settings.json` hooks fire inside T3-launched sessions was not verified (the capability probe passes `settingSources`, the turn adapter options do not show it). [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:apps/server/src/provider/ClaudeProvider.ts#L198-L202]
- **Plugins / slash commands / headless.** No plugin system found; composer commands and skills are those of the wrapped agent (not verified); no headless chat CLI (the `t3` CLI starts the server/web UI, `service`, `update`, `pair`, `connect`). [src: pingdotgg/t3code@9bd1d80:README.md#L28-L36]

**Best recall injection point:** none specific to T3; integrate at the wrapped agents (Claude Code CLAUDE.md/hooks/MCP, Codex AGENTS.md/config) and import from their native stores; read `statev2.sqlite` only to enrich titles/projects.
**Confidence / unknowns:** medium: schema verified from migrations at `9bd1d80`; `payload_json` shapes, hook inheritance, whether T3 sessions are always resumable from the native CLI, WAL mode and the WebSocket API are unverified; nothing local.
