## Google Antigravity (IDE, 2.0 desktop app and `agy` CLI; Google, closed source)


Legacy v1 layout observed: `~/.gemini/antigravity/` holds `brain/<uuid>/` (with `task.md`, `implementation_plan.md` and `walkthrough.md`, each with `.metadata.json` and `.resolved` revisions), `conversations/` (`<uuid>.pb`), `implicit/` (`<uuid>.pb`), `code_tracker/`, `playground/`, `browser_recordings/`, `context_state/`, `mcp_config.json`, `user_settings.pb` and `installation_id`; the VS Code-style state lives in `~/Library/Application Support/Antigravity/` [observed].
**Version verified:** docs changelog 2026-10-06: Antigravity 2.0 desktop v2.19.1 (2026-09-30), CLI `agy` v1.2.14 (2026-09-30), IDE v2.5.5 [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/changelog]; Homebrew casks `antigravity` 2.19.1, `antigravity-ide` 2.5.5, `antigravity-cli` 1.3.0 [3p: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/antigravity.json]. Storage formats are NOT in Google's docs or changelog; everything on history below is [3p] unless stated.

### 1. Detection
- Three products [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/getting-started]: Antigravity 2.0 desktop agent manager (`/Applications/Antigravity.app`, bundle `com.google.antigravity`), Antigravity IDE (VS Code-fork editor, `/Applications/Antigravity IDE.app`, `com.google.antigravity-ide`, shims `antigravity-ide`/`agy-ide`), Antigravity CLI `agy` (Go) [3p: Homebrew casks]. `Antigravity.app` was the Nov-2025 IDE and now hosts 2.0 [inferred: same cask token, bundle id and data dir name].
- `agy`: `curl -fsSL https://antigravity.google/cli/install.sh | bash` -> `~/.local/bin/agy` (Windows `C:\Users\<u>\AppData\Local\agy\bin`) [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/getting-started?tab=cli]; `agy --version` prints a bare semver [3p: SpecStory ANTIGRAVITY-FORMAT.md section 8]. Successor to Gemini CLI (announced 2026-05-19, cutover 2026-06-18) [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/gcli-migration].
- Data roots (all share `~/.gemini/` with Gemini CLI, whose own state is `tmp/`, `history/`, `projects.json`, `settings.json`, `extensions/`, `oauth_creds.json`): `~/.gemini/antigravity/` (2.0 app + legacy IDE v1), `~/.gemini/antigravity-ide/` (IDE 2.x), `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/` (agy: `settings.json`, `plugins/`, `import_manifest.json`), `~/.gemini/config/` (shared customization root), dot dirs `~/.antigravity`, `~/.antigravity-ide`, app data `~/Library/Application Support/Antigravity` and `Antigravity IDE` [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/settings/, https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/features/] [3p: Homebrew zap lists]. Upgraded installs may also hold `~/.gemini/antigravity-backup/brain` [3p: agent-sessions guide].
- Env vars that relocate data: none documented (the CLI reference and settings pages list no environment variables) [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/reference]; the Python SDK has `LocalAgentConfig(app_data_dir=..., save_dir=...)` [src: google-antigravity/antigravity-sdk-python@12f9a4c:examples/getting_started/app_data_dir_override.py#L15-L32]. `agy` injects `ANTIGRAVITY_PROJECT_ID` / `ANTIGRAVITY_TRAJECTORY_ID` into child processes [3p: SpecStory].

### 2. History location
| Product / era | Conversation store | Plaintext transcript | Artifacts |
|---|---|---|---|
| agy 1.x (since ~2026-06-18, 1.0.12) | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/conversations/<uuid>.db` (+`-wal/-shm`) | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/brain/<uuid>/.system_generated/logs/transcript_full.jsonl` and `transcript.jsonl` | `brain/<uuid>/*.md` |
| IDE 2.5.x | `~/.gemini/antigravity-ide/conversations/<uuid>.db` | same layout under `antigravity-ide/brain` | same |
| 2.0 desktop | `~/.gemini/antigravity/conversations/<uuid>.db` (inferred from the official hook example path `~/.gemini/antigravity/brain/<uuid>/.system_generated/logs/transcript.jsonl` [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/hooks/]) | same layout under `antigravity/brain` | same |
| IDE v1.x (Nov 2025 until the 2.x line) | `~/.gemini/antigravity/conversations/<uuid>.pb`, `implicit/<uuid>.pb` | none | `brain/<uuid>/{task,implementation_plan,walkthrough}.md` (+`.metadata.json`, `.resolved[.N]`) [observed] |

Sources for the table rows above [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/antigravity.rs#L1-L67; kenn-io/agentsview issue #1995; neilberkman/ccrider issue #26; nicktoumpelis/clocwork PR #91; https://jazzyalex.github.io/agent-sessions/guides/antigravity-cli-local-history.html]; the `~/.gemini/antigravity-ide/` and `~/.gemini/antigravity/` roots also appear in the Homebrew zap lists [3p: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/antigravity-ide.json].
- CLI sidecars: `history.jsonl` (interactive TUI prompts only: `{display,timestamp(ms),workspace,conversationId?}`; `agy -p` is NOT logged), `conversation_summaries.db`, `log/cli-*.log`, `settings.json`; shared `~/.gemini/config/projects/<projectId>.json`; IDE writes none of the CLI sidecars [3p: specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a:specstory-cli/pkg/providers/antigravitycli/ANTIGRAVITY-FORMAT.md; nicktoumpelis/clocwork#91]. Knowledge Items: `knowledge/<ki-id>/{metadata.json,artifacts/}`, `knowledge.lock` [3p: damadorPL/agent-memories].

### 3. Storage format
Primary read path = `brain/<uuid>/.system_generated/logs/transcript_full.jsonl` (append-only, one step per line); `transcript.jsonl` has the same steps but truncates long tool output (`truncated_fields`) and double-JSON-encodes tool arg values [3p: SpecStory spec sections 3, 3.6; agent-sessions guide]. The official hook payload exposes this path as `transcriptPath` [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/hooks/].

| Concept | Field / location |
|---|---|
| session id | `<uuid>` = `brain/<uuid>/` name = `conversations/<uuid>.db` stem = `agy --conversation <uuid>`; hooks `conversationId`; NOT inside the transcript |
| title | not in transcript; CLI `conversation_summaries.db`; v1 artifacts `*.md.metadata.json` `summary`; else first `<USER_REQUEST>` |
| project / cwd | not in transcript. CLI: `history.jsonl` `workspace` or log line `project: using project "<path>" (id=..)` + `config/projects/<id>.json` (`name`, `projectResources.resources[].gitFolder.folderUri`); IDE: `.db` `trajectory_metadata_blob` field 1.1 (folder) / 1.2 (git root) / 7; hooks `workspacePaths[]` |
| created / updated | per-step `created_at` (RFC3339 UTC, 1 s); session start = step 0; local TZ in `<ADDITIONAL_METADATA>`; file mtime |
| user text | `type:"USER_INPUT"` (`source:"USER_EXPLICIT"`) `content` = `<USER_REQUEST>..</USER_REQUEST>` + `<ADDITIONAL_METADATA>` (+ first turn `<USER_SETTINGS_CHANGE>` naming the model) |
| assistant text | `type:"PLANNER_RESPONSE"` (`source:"MODEL"`) `content`; reasoning in `thinking` |
| tool calls | `PLANNER_RESPONSE.tool_calls[]` `{name,args}` (`run_command`, `view_file`, `write_to_file`, `replace_file_content`, `grep_search`, `list_dir`, `search_web`, `invoke_subagent`, MCP tools...); args carry `toolAction`, `toolSummary` |
| tool results | the NEXT step: `type` `RUN_COMMAND`/`VIEW_FILE`/`CODE_ACTION`/`GREP_SEARCH`/`LIST_DIRECTORY`/`SEARCH_WEB`/`READ_URL_CONTENT`/`INVOKE_SUBAGENT`/`ASK_QUESTION`/`GENERIC`, `content`; no call id (positional); failed shell commands still `status:"DONE"` |
| other | `SYSTEM_MESSAGE`, `CHECKPOINT` (compaction), `CONVERSATION_HISTORY` (marker); `status` DONE/RUNNING/ERROR; model also in hooks `modelName`; per-call token usage only in `.db` `gen_metadata` |

- `.db` (stock SQLite, not encrypted; tables `trajectory_meta`, `steps(idx,step_type,status,step_payload,...)`, `gen_metadata(idx,data)`, `executor_metadata`, `parent_references`, `trajectory_metadata_blob`); payloads are undocumented protobuf with readable UTF-8 inside. `agy --conversation` resumes from the `.db`, not the transcript [3p: SpecStory spec section 1.1].
- Format changes: v1.x IDE = encrypted `.pb` + Markdown artifacts only (the newest such artifact seen by a tracker is 2026-03-04); from ~2026-06-18 (agy 1.0.12) = SQLite + JSONL under `antigravity-cli/`; IDE 2.x/2.0 adopted the same stack under `antigravity-ide/` and `antigravity/` [3p: agent-sessions spec 2026-06-26-antigravity-cli-jsonl-migration-design.md; kenn-io/agentsview#1995].
- Incremental: transcripts append-only (`agy -c` appends to the same file; `step_index` monotonic with one gap at index 4); detect change via transcript mtime/size, keep a `step_index` high-water mark; the `.db` is held open by a live process (WAL): open read-only with a busy timeout, never copy-modify; a `.db` can exist without a transcript (v1/upgrade) and vice versa [3p: SpecStory ANTIGRAVITY-FORMAT.md; agent-sessions spec].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Legacy v1 `conversations/*.pb` and `implicit/*.pb`: encrypted. Observed: 8.000 bits/byte entropy, 38.3% printable (= uniform random), a generic protobuf wire-format parse fails at the first bytes, `file(1)` says "data" [observed]; third parties call it AES (CTR/CBC/GCM probed) needing a key from the OS keychain or env `ANTIGRAVITY_KEY` [3p: kenn-io/agentsview internal/parser/antigravity_crypto.go; arashz/antigravity_decryptor]. Do not attempt; only `brain/*.md` and `.metadata.json` (keys `artifactType, summary?, updatedAt, version`) are readable from v1 data [observed]. `user_settings.pb` (43 B) is plain protobuf (settings); `globalStorage/state.vscdb` key `jetskiStateSync.agentManagerInitState` is base64 protobuf (5.8 KB decoded, contents deliberately not read; may carry the conversation list), and later builds are reported to sync `antigravityUnifiedStateSync.trajectorySummaries` (absent here) [observed] [3p: https://dev.to/ndondadaniel2020/how-to-restore-lost-chat-history-in-google-antigravity-ide-reverse-engineering-the-plan-mystery-15og].
- Current builds: `.db` plaintext SQLite and JSONL transcripts [3p]. Google ships no export command (CLI has `/copy`, `/fork`, `/rewind`, `/resume`); community exporters exist [3p: winters27/google-antigravity-export]. Conversations are local-first; no server-side copy found.

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP**: global `~/.gemini/config/mcp_config.json`, workspace `.agents/mcp_config.json` [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/mcp/]. Schema: `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{},"cwd":"."}}}`; remote `{"serverUrl":"https://..","headers":{..}}` (`url`/`httpUrl` unsupported in the CLI); optional `disabled`, `disabledTools[]`. UI: IDE side panel "MCP Servers", 2.0 Settings > Customizations, CLI `/mcp` manager; `agy mcp add|list|remove|enable|disable` appears only in third-party cheat sheets [3p: https://computingforgeeks.com/antigravity-cli-cheat-sheet/]. IDE v1 used `~/.gemini/antigravity/mcp_config.json` (empty file here [observed]); installers write both [3p: docs.claude-mem.ai/antigravity-cli/setup].
- **Global instructions**: `~/.gemini/GEMINI.md`, `~/.gemini/AGENTS.md`, `~/.gemini/config/{GEMINI,AGENTS}.md`, modular `~/.gemini/config/rules/*.md` (YAML frontmatter `trigger: always_on/model_decision/glob/manual`); project `AGENTS.md`/`GEMINI.md` in any directory, `.agents/{AGENTS,GEMINI}.md`, `.agents/rules/*.md`; more specific directory wins [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/rules/].
- **Skills**: workspace `.agents/skills/<n>/SKILL.md` (legacy `.agent/skills`); global `~/.gemini/config/skills/` (2.0/IDE; legacy `~/.gemini/antigravity/skills/`) and `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/skills/` (CLI); plugin `plugins/<n>/skills/`; agentskills.io format, frontmatter `name` (optional), `description` (required); `~/.agents/skills` is not documented [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/skills/].
- **Hooks** [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/hooks/] (corroborated by agy's shipped spec quoted in [3p: thedotmack/claude-mem#4274]): 5 events `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `PreInvocation` (before every model call), `PostInvocation`, `Stop`; NO session-start / prompt-submit / session-end events. Files: `~/.gemini/config/hooks.json` (global, all surfaces; the CLI also reads `hooks` inside `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/settings.json`), `.agents/hooks.json` (workspace), plugin `hooks.json`. Named-hook schema: `{"recall":{"enabled":true,"PreInvocation":[{"type":"command","command":"<cmd>","timeout":30}],"PostToolUse":[{"matcher":"run_command","hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"<cmd>"}]}]}}` (tool events grouped with `matcher` = `*`, exact, `a|b`, or regex such as `browser_.*`; the others are flat arrays; timeout in seconds, default 30). stdin camelCase JSON: `conversationId, workspacePaths[], transcriptPath, artifactDirectoryPath, modelName` + `invocationNum, initialNumSteps` (invocation events) / `toolCall{name,args}, stepIdx` (tool events). stdout protojson: `PreToolUse` `{"decision":"allow/deny/ask/force_ask/deny_unless_prior_grant"}`; `PostToolUse` `{}`; `PreInvocation`/`PostInvocation` `{"injectSteps":[{"userMessage":".."},{"ephemeralMessage":".."},{"toolCall":{"name":"..","args":{}}}]}`; `Stop` `{"decision":"continue","reason":".."}`. INJECTS into model context only via `injectSteps` (Pre/PostInvocation) and the `Stop` continue reason; tool-event output is not injected. Gemini-style event names or `{"continue":true,"systemMessage":..}` output are silently ignored/rejected [3p: thedotmack/claude-mem#4057].
- **Plugins**: dir with `plugin.json` (`name` required for CLI, `description`, `$schema` https://antigravity.google/schemas/v1/plugin.json) + optional `mcp_config.json`, `hooks.json`, `skills/`, `agents/`, `rules/`; installed to `~/.gemini/config/plugins/`, `.agents/plugins/`, CLI staging `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/plugins/<name>/`; `agy plugin install <path>`, `/plugin install <name>@<marketplace>`, `agy plugin import gemini` (Gemini extensions -> plugins, commands -> skills) [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/plugins/, https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/gcli-migration]. The IDE is a VS Code fork on Open VSX by default (VSIX install possible) [3p: appsoftware.com / medium guides].
- **Slash commands**: none documented for the CLI beyond skills/plugins; IDE workflows = Markdown files invoked as `/name` in `.agents/workflows/` (global `~/.gemini/config/global_workflows/` [3p: medium.com/google-cloud "Where does Antigravity look for Rules and Workflows?"]), deprecated in favor of skills by 2026-11-01 [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/ide/workflows/].
- **Headless** [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/headless/]: `agy -p/--print/--prompt "<q>"`; `--output-format text/json/stream-json` (json envelope keys `conversation_id,status,response,duration_seconds,num_turns,usage,error?,structured_output?`; stream-json NDJSON events `init, step_update, result`), `--input-format stream-json`, `-c/--continue`, `--conversation <id>`, `--model`, `--effort low/medium/high`, `--agent`, `--json-schema`, `--print-timeout` (5m), `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, `--add-dir`, `--sandbox`. Non-interactive runs still write `brain/<uuid>/` transcripts [3p: agent-sessions spec].

**Best recall injection point:** a `PreInvocation` command hook (global `~/.gemini/config/hooks.json`, or packaged in a plugin with the MCP server) printing `{"injectSteps":[{"ephemeralMessage":"<memory>"}]}`, gated on stdin `invocationNum == 1` (it fires before every model call; there is no session-start event). Lowest-fragility alternative: a managed block in `~/.gemini/GEMINI.md` (global rules read by Antigravity IDE/2.0/CLI and by Gemini CLI) or `~/.gemini/config/rules/recall.md` with `trigger: always_on`. MCP: add `mcpServers.recall` to `~/.gemini/config/mcp_config.json` (plus `~/.gemini/antigravity/mcp_config.json` for IDE v1.x).
**Confidence / unknowns:** MCP/rules/skills/hooks/plugins/headless are Google-documented (page text read through summaries, hook JSON examples reproduced verbatim and matched by a second third-party source); history formats are undocumented, reverse-engineered by several parsers and have changed twice in a year; unverified: exact `.db` DDL differences between IDE and agy, whether the 2.0 app uses `.../antigravity/` or `.../antigravity-ide/` on a given build, Windows/Linux paths, and the v1 `.pb` key.

---

## Gemini CLI (Google / google-gemini/gemini-cli)


A `~/.gemini/` directory can hold only Antigravity and skill-sync leftovers (`antigravity/`, `skills/` symlinks into `~/.agents/skills`), not Gemini CLI state [observed].
**Version verified:** stable v0.62.0 (2026-09-29), nightly v0.64.0-nightly.20261006, main @fb972b2 (2026-10-02); docs read from the repo @fb972b2 and geminicli.com [doc: https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/releases].
**Lifecycle note:** on 2026-06-18 Gemini CLI stopped serving free-tier and Google AI Pro/Ultra logins (moved to Antigravity CLI `agy`); Code Assist Standard/Enterprise and paid API keys keep working; repo stays Apache-2.0 and is still released weekly [doc: https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/discussions/27274]. `~/.gemini/tmp/<slug>/chats` therefore still applies to every machine that runs Gemini CLI (Enterprise / API key users, older history); `agy` writes elsewhere (`~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/`) and its migration guide covers settings, extensions, MCP and skills only, not chat history [doc: https://antigravity.google/docs/cli/gcli-migration]. Keep importing Gemini CLI, expect fewer new sessions.

### 1. Detection
- Binary `gemini` (npm `@google/gemini-cli`, `bin.gemini`; `brew install gemini-cli`, MacPorts, conda, npx) [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/get-started/installation/] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/cli/package.json#L12-L14]. No `.app`, no CFBundleIdentifier.
- Data root `~/.gemini` (`GEMINI_DIR='.gemini'` under `homedir()`); env `GEMINI_CLI_HOME` replaces the HOME the CLI appends `.gemini` to (it also relocates `~/.agents`) [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/utils/paths.ts#L13-L27] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L54-L68] [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/reference/configuration/]. Under macOS Seatbelt (`SANDBOX=sandbox-exec`) runtime state moves to `~/.cache/.gemini` [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L92-L108]. Linux/Windows use `~/.gemini` / `%USERPROFILE%\.gemini` [inferred: `os.homedir()`].
- System layer: macOS `/Library/Application Support/GeminiCli/{settings.json,system-defaults.json,policies/}`; Linux `/etc/gemini-cli`; Windows `C:\ProgramData\gemini-cli`; overrides `GEMINI_CLI_SYSTEM_SETTINGS_PATH`, `GEMINI_CLI_SYSTEM_DEFAULTS_PATH`, `GEMINI_CLI_TRUSTED_FOLDERS_PATH` [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L174-L190] [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/reference/configuration/].

### 2. History location
- `~/.gemini/tmp/<slug>/chats/session-<YYYY-MM-DDTHH-MM>-<sessionId[0:8]>.jsonl` (same-minute collision: `...-<n>-<id8>`) [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L777-L799]. Subagent sessions: `chats/<parentSessionId>/<subSessionId>.jsonl` (`kind:"subagent"`), not returned by `listProjectChatFiles` [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L761-L773] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L413-L446].
- `<slug>` = slugified basename of the project root (+ `-N` on clash), registered in `~/.gemini/projects.json` = `{"projects":{"<abs project path>":"<slug>"}}`; `tmp/<slug>/.project_root` holds the abs path [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/projectRegistry.ts#L16-L24] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L288-L300]. Before PR #17901 (2026-02-06) the dir was `sha256(projectRoot)`; legacy dirs are renamed on first run [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L305-L325]. The public docs still say `<project_hash>` [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/session-management/] (stale).
- Siblings in `tmp/<slug>/`: `logs.json` (user prompts only: `{sessionId,messageId,timestamp,type:"user",message}`), `checkpoint-<tag>.json` (`/chat save`: `{history,authType}`), `checkpoints/`, `tool-outputs/`, `plans/`, `tasks/`, `memory/`, `shell_history` [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/core/logger.ts#L14-L37] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L364-L410].
- RETENTION: sessions are auto-deleted after 30 days by default (`general.sessionRetention` `{enabled:true,maxAge:"30d",maxCount?,minRetention:"1d"}`) together with their tool-outputs/logs [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/session-management/#session-retention] -> import early, never mirror deletions.

### 3. Storage format
First line = metadata; then one JSON object per line (types: `ConversationRecord`, `MessageRecord`, `ToolCallRecord`) [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingTypes.ts#L12-L123].

| Concept | Field / location |
|---|---|
| session id | line-1 `sessionId` (UUID; file name has its first 8 chars); resume `gemini -r latest/<index>/<uuid>` |
| title | `summary` (one-line, appended as `{"$set":{"summary":...}}`, optional) else text of the first user message [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L1189-L1196] |
| project / cwd | line-1 `projectHash` = sha256(abs project root) [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/utils/paths.ts#L318-L320]; abs path via `.project_root` / `projects.json`; `directories[]` = extra workspaces (`/dir add`) |
| created / updated | `startTime`, `lastUpdated` (ISO-8601), per-message `timestamp`; file mtime |
| user text | `{"id","timestamp","type":"user","content":<string or Part[] e.g. [{"text":..}]>,"displayContent"?}` |
| assistant text | `type:"gemini"` `content`; reasoning in `thoughts[]` `{subject,description,timestamp}` |
| tool calls | `type:"gemini"` -> `toolCalls[]` `{id,name,args,status,timestamp,displayName,description,agentId?}`; MCP tools are named `mcp_<server>_<tool>` [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/hooks/reference/] |
| tool results | `toolCalls[].result` (Part[]; `functionResponse.{id,name,response}` [3p: specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a:specstory-cli/pkg/providers/geminicli/json_parser.go#L14-L36]) plus UI `resultDisplay` |
| other | `model`, `tokens` `{input,output,cached,thoughts,tool,total}` on `gemini` messages; `type` `info`/`error`/`warning` = UI notices; `kind` main/subagent |

- Record kinds when replaying a `.jsonl`: (a) metadata line (`sessionId`+`projectHash`), (b) message line (has string `id`; same `id` seen again REPLACES the earlier copy - tool results and tokens are re-appended), (c) `{"$set":{...}}` merges metadata (`lastUpdated`, `summary`, `directories`, `memoryScratchpad`), (d) `{"$rewindTo":"<messageId>"}` deletes that message and everything after, (e) main-branch only: `{"$patch":{id?,content?,toolCalls?,updates?,removeIds?,orderIds?}}` [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L306-L346] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L418-L505] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingTypes.ts#L123-L155].
- Format changes: <=v0.38 one pretty JSON object `session-<ts>-<id8>.json` `{sessionId,projectHash,startTime,lastUpdated,messages[],summary?}` rewritten wholesale; JSONL since v0.39.0 (2026-04-23; PR #23749 merged 2026-04-09), legacy `.json` still listed/readable (`.json` or `.jsonl`) [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L416-L421] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L1565-L1618]; resuming a legacy file writes a sibling `.jsonl` and I saw no unlink of the `.json` [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L708-L735] (dedupe by `sessionId`) [inferred]. `$patch` + bounded in-memory window arrived 2026-10-01 (PR #29568, commit d1cc08a), NOT in stable v0.62.0 (verified: 0 occurrences of `$patch` in chatRecordingService.ts at tag v0.62.0) [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@d1cc08a:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts].
- Incremental: normal writes are `fs.appendFileSync` of one line [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L892-L906]; rare full rewrite via tmp+rename with the old file kept as `*.unreadable-<ms>` [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L915-L972]. Store per-file (size, mtime, byte offset); on growth replay only the new lines through the same state machine; on shrink / mtime-only change / `$rewindTo` re-parse the whole file (files are small). Cheap new-session detection: list `chats/*.json{,l}` by mtime.

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
Plaintext JSON(L), no encryption, no server-side copy (plain `fs.appendFileSync` of `JSON.stringify` lines) [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/services/chatRecordingService.ts#L892-L906]. Official export: `/chat share [file.md/file.json]` and `/resume`; `--output-format stream-json` for live capture; hooks pass `transcript_path` (the live `.jsonl`) [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/reference/commands/]. Credentials sit next to the data and must never be read: `~/.gemini/oauth_creds.json`, `google_accounts.json`, `mcp-oauth-tokens.json` [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L70-L76] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L127-L129] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L255-L257].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP**: `mcpServers` in `~/.gemini/settings.json` (user) or `<proj>/.gemini/settings.json` (project; wins); extensions may ship `mcpServers` in `gemini-extension.json` (settings.json wins on name clash). Transports stdio / SSE (`url`) / streamable HTTP (`httpUrl`); keys `command,args,env,cwd,timeout,trust,includeTools,excludeTools,headers,oauth` [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/mcp-server/]. Snippet: `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{},"timeout":30000,"trust":false}}}`. CLI: `gemini mcp add <name> <command> [args..] --scope user|project` (default `project`), `gemini mcp list|remove` [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/cli/src/commands/mcp/add.ts#L160-L166] [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/cli-reference/].
- **Global instructions**: `~/.gemini/GEMINI.md`; project/parent/subdirectory `GEMINI.md`, JIT-loaded when tools touch a dir; `@file.md` imports; `context.fileName` (string or array, default `GEMINI.md`) can add `AGENTS.md`; `/memory show|reload`; the agent itself writes durable notes into these files [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/gemini-md/, https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/memory/]. The same `~/.gemini/GEMINI.md` is also a global rules file for Antigravity.
- **Skills**: `~/.gemini/skills/` and `~/.agents/skills/` (user), `.gemini/skills/` and `.agents/skills/` (workspace; `.agents` wins within a tier), extension `skills/`; agentskills.io `SKILL.md`; `gemini skills install|link|list|enable|disable` [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/skills/] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L138-L148] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/config/storage.ts#L348-L358].
- **Hooks** (11 events, `settings.json` key `hooks`, merged project > user > system > extensions; extension hooks live in `<ext>/hooks/hooks.json`; `hooksConfig.enabled` default true) [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/hooks/types.ts#L20-L53] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/hooks/hookRegistry.ts#L170-L200] [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/hooks/reference/]. Shape: `{"hooks":{"SessionStart":[{"matcher"?:"startup","hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"<cmd>","name"?:..,"timeout":10000}]}]}}` (timeout ms, default 60000; matcher = regex for tool events, exact string for lifecycle events). stdin JSON: `session_id, transcript_path, cwd, hook_event_name, timestamp` + per event (`SessionStart.source` startup/resume/clear; `BeforeAgent.prompt`; `AfterAgent.prompt,prompt_response,stop_hook_active`; `BeforeTool/AfterTool.tool_name,tool_input,tool_response`; `SessionEnd.reason`; `PreCompress.trigger`) [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/hooks/hookEventHandler.ts#L371-L385]. stdout must be only JSON; exit 2 = block (stderr as reason), other non-zero = warning. Env `GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR` (alias `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`); project hooks need a trusted folder and are fingerprinted [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/hooks/].
  - INJECTS into model context: `SessionStart` `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` (interactive: added as the first user turn; `-p`: prepended to the prompt), `BeforeAgent` (appended to that turn's prompt), `AfterTool` (appended to the tool result); text is wrapped in `<hook_context>` and `<`/`>` are escaped [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/cli/src/gemini.tsx#L945-L958] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/cli/src/ui/AppContainer.tsx#L488-L510] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/core/client.ts#L945-L970] [src: google-gemini/gemini-cli@fb972b2:packages/core/src/hooks/types.ts#L263-L275]. Output fields `decision` (deny/block), `reason`, `continue:false`, `systemMessage` (shown to user only), `suppressOutput`. `SessionEnd`, `Notification`, `PreCompress` are advisory (no flow control, no injection); `BeforeModel/AfterModel/BeforeToolSelection` can rewrite requests; `AfterAgent` deny = retry with `reason` as new prompt; `hookSpecificOutput.clearContext`.
- **Plugins / extensions**: `~/.gemini/extensions/<name>/gemini-extension.json` (`name,version,description,mcpServers,contextFileName,excludeTools,settings[],themes[],plan,migratedTo`) + `commands/*.toml`, `hooks/hooks.json`, `skills/`, `agents/*.md`, `policies/*.toml`, `GEMINI.md`; `gemini extensions install <git-url/path> [--ref --auto-update --consent]`, `link`, `new`, `validate`; vars `${extensionPath}`, `${workspacePath}` [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/extensions/reference/]. A third party can ship MCP + SessionStart hook + context in one repo.
- **Slash commands**: `~/.gemini/commands/**/*.toml` (user) and `<proj>/.gemini/commands/` (project wins); subdir = `:` namespace; fields `prompt` (required), `description`; `{{args}}`, `!{shell}`, `@{file}` [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/custom-commands/].
- **Headless**: `gemini -p "<q>"` (or non-TTY stdin), `-i` (run then stay interactive), `-r/--resume`, `--list-sessions`, `--delete-session`, `--approval-mode default/auto_edit/yolo/plan`, `--allowed-mcp-server-names`, `-e/--extensions`, `--include-directories`, `-o/--output-format text/json/stream-json`. json = `{response,stats,error?}`; stream-json events `init,message,tool_use,tool_result,error,result`; exit codes 0/1/42/53 [doc: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/headless/, https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/cli-reference/].

**Best recall injection point:** a user-level `SessionStart` command hook returning `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"additionalContext":"<memory>"}}` (registered in `~/.gemini/settings.json`, or shipped in an extension's `hooks/hooks.json` together with the MCP server) - it covers startup/resume/clear and `-p`; fallback with the least moving parts is a managed block in `~/.gemini/GEMINI.md`. Least fragile MCP registration: `gemini mcp add --scope user recall recall serve` (writes `mcpServers` into `~/.gemini/settings.json`).
**Confidence / unknowns:** high for format, hooks and paths (read from source @fb972b2 and docs); `$patch` lines exist only on main so the parser must tolerate unknown `$`-records; legacy-`.json` + new-`.jsonl` duplicates after resume are inferred from code, not observed; Windows/Linux paths inferred.

## Windsurf / Devin Desktop (Cognition, formerly Codeium; Windsurf Next)


Observed layout: `~/.codeium/windsurf/` holds `cascade/` (`<uuid>.pb`), `implicit/` (`.pb`), `memories/global_rules.md`, `skills/` (symlinks), `database/<hash>/3/MODEL_EMBED_6591/embedding_database.sqlite` (a code index, not chat history) and `user_settings.pb`; VS Code-style state lives in `~/Library/Application Support/Windsurf/` [observed].
**Version verified:** Devin Desktop v3.10.48 (2026-09-29) via docs.devin.ai changelog; Homebrew cask `devin-desktop` 3.10.48; Devin CLI cask 3000.11.3 [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/changelog]. Windsurf was renamed Devin Desktop on 2026-06-02 (docs.windsurf.com now 307-redirects to docs.devin.ai/desktop); the Cascade agent was REMOVED in v3.9.19 (2026-09-08): Devin Local (the Devin CLI harness) is the only agent [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/devin-desktop-faq, https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/changelog].

### 1. Detection
- Pre-rebrand `/Applications/Windsurf.app` (`com.exafunction.windsurf`), Next `Windsurf - Next.app` (`com.exafunction.windsurfNext`); now `/Applications/Devin.app` and `Devin - Next.app` with the SAME bundle ids (cask `devin-desktop`, old token `windsurf`) [3p: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/devin-desktop.json]. Shell commands: `devin-desktop` (new); legacy `windsurf`/`surf` still ship in `~/.codeium/windsurf/bin/` [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/devin-desktop-faq] [observed]. Linux package `devin-desktop` (`windsurf` transitional) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/install].
- Data dirs after the rebrand: `~/.codeium/windsurf` kept (Next `~/.codeium/windsurf-next`, insiders `windsurf-insiders`); `~/Library/Application Support/Windsurf` -> `Devin` (Next `Devin - Next`); `~/.windsurf` -> `~/.devin` (Next `~/.devin-next`) [3p: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/devin-desktop.json zap list; not stated in the official docs I read; the pre-rebrand names `~/Library/Application Support/Windsurf` and `~/.windsurf` are what exist here [observed]]; official docs still reference `~/.windsurf/{transcripts,worktrees,acp/registry.json}` [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/hooks, https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/worktrees, https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/acp]. The JetBrains plugin uses `~/.codeium/` (maintenance mode) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/devin-desktop-faq].
- No env var that relocates data is documented; `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` is honored for the `devin` config dir (stated for `mcp_config.json`) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/mcp]. Sibling product Devin CLI (`devin`, cask `devin-cli`; `~/.config/devin/`, `~/.local/share/devin/cli/`) shares the Devin Local harness [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/devin-local].

### 2. History location
- Cascade (legacy): `~/.codeium/windsurf/cascade/<trajectory-uuid>.pb` (Windows `C:\Users\<u>\.codeium\windsurf\cascade`); deleting that dir clears chat history [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/troubleshooting/windsurf-common-issues]. Auto-generated memories also live in `~/.codeium/windsurf/memories/` (format undocumented; none here) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/memories]. `implicit/<uuid>.pb`: purpose undocumented, encrypted-looking [observed].
- Devin Local (current): sessions in one SQLite DB `~/.local/share/devin/cli/sessions.db` (the official docs confirm `~/.local/share/devin/cli/` as the CLI data dir on macOS/Linux [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/cli/troubleshooting]; the DB name is [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/devin.rs#L1-L30; jazzyalex/agent-sessions@6fa9a73:AgentSessions/Devin/DevinSqliteReader.swift#L1-L20]); Desktop GUI additionally keeps `User/acp-messages/<session-uuid>.db` and `User/globalStorage/state.vscdb` keys `windsurfSpace.*` under its app-data root (`~/Library/Application Support/Devin/` [inferred]; Linux `~/.config/Devin/`) [3p: Icaro0310/devin-internals-spec@09149fa docs/SCHEMA.md]. Whether Desktop-launched Devin Local also writes `sessions.db` is unconfirmed (same harness [doc]).
- Hook-captured copy (legacy Cascade only): `post_cascade_response_with_transcript` writes the full JSONL to `~/.windsurf/transcripts/<trajectory_id>.jsonl` (mode 0600, directory capped at 100 files, oldest pruned) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/hooks].

### 3. Storage format
- Cascade `.pb`: not parseable without a key (see 4). Third parties describe the decrypted payload as a `CortexTrajectory` protobuf (field 1 trajectory id, 2 repeated steps, 7 scope with `workspace_uri`/`git_root_uri`/`branch_name`) [3p: vibheksoni/session-export session_sdk/windsurf_pb.py#L1-L20].

| Concept | Hook transcript JSONL (Cascade; doc) / Devin `sessions.db` (3p) |
|---|---|
| session id | `trajectory_id` (UUID; probably the `.pb` file stem [inferred]) / `sessions.id` |
| title | not available / `sessions.title` (nullable) |
| project / cwd | not in hook transcript (hooks give no cwd except worktree events) / `sessions.working_directory`, `workspace_dirs` (JSON) |
| created / updated | file mtime / `sessions.created_at`, `last_activity_at` (epoch seconds per cass and agent-sessions; milliseconds per devin-internals-spec v17: detect by magnitude), `hidden` flag |
| user text | line `{"type":"user_input","status":"done","user_input":{"user_response":"..","rules_applied":{..}}}` / `message_nodes.chat_message` JSON `role:"user"` `content` |
| assistant text | `{"type":"planner_response","planner_response":{"response":".."}}` / `role:"assistant"` `content`, `thinking{signature,thinking}` |
| tool calls | `code_action` and similar typed steps (e.g. `code_action.{path,new_content}`) / assistant `tool_calls[]` `{id,index,kind,name,arguments}` |
| tool results | step payloads (file contents, command output) / `role:"tool"` + `tool_call_id` |
| model | hook stdin `model_name` / `sessions.model`, `agent_mode` |

- `message_nodes` is a forest (`session_id,node_id,parent_node_id,chat_message,created_at`): replay only the parent chain ending at `sessions.main_chain_id`; skip `hidden=1`; schema changes without notice (17 migrations by 2026-09-14, ledger `refinery_schema_history`; gate on `MAX(version)`) [3p: devin-internals-spec; cass devin.rs].
- Format changes: Cascade `.pb` (2025 - mid 2026) -> Devin Local SQLite: Devin Local arrived with the 2026-06-02 rebrand, Cascade stayed available through July and was removed in v3.9.19 (2026-09-08); the "Continue in Devin Local" prompt migrates old Cascade conversations into Local sessions [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/devin-desktop-faq, https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/devin-local, https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/changelog].
- Incremental: `.pb`: only mtime/size are observable (change-detect, cannot import content). `sessions.db`: poll `sessions.last_activity_at` and node `created_at`; live process holds it (WAL): open read-only with a busy timeout [3p: cass devin.rs "opened SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY with a busy timeout"].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Cascade `.pb` (and `implicit/*.pb`): encrypted. Observed: 8.000 bits/byte entropy, 38.3% printable, protobuf wire parse fails immediately, `file(1)` = "data" [observed]; a third party calls it AES-256-GCM protobuf [3p: vibheksoni/session-export README, provider table]. No supported way to read it; use the transcript hook (while Cascade existed) or Devin Local stores. Devin Cloud sessions are server-side only [3p: cass devin.rs].
- Official exports: Cascade "Share Conversation" uploads to Cognition servers; Windsurf Plugins chat `...` > export [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/cascade]; Devin CLI `devin --export [PATH]` (ATIF JSON after each turn), `devin list --format json/csv`, `/rm-session <id>` [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/cli/reference/commands].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP**: legacy Cascade `~/.codeium/windsurf/mcp_config.json` (JetBrains `~/.codeium/mcp_config.json`); the rewritten Cascade page says `~/.config/devin/mcp_config.json` (`%APPDATA%\devin\` on Windows) - docs conflict, but Devin CLI/Local also IMPORTS `~/.codeium/<channel>/mcp_config.json` [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/mcp, https://docs.devin.ai/cli/reference/configuration/read-config-from]. Devin Local: user `~/.config/devin/mcp_config.json`, project `.devin/mcp_config.json`, personal `.devin/mcp_config.local.json` (since v3000.3; older `mcpServers` key inside `config.json`); also imports Claude Code files (`.mcp.json`, `~/.claude.json`, `~/.claude/settings.json`...). Schema `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{},"disabled":false}}}`; remote `{"url":"..","transport":"http/sse","headers":{..}}`; Cascade adds `disabledTools[]` and a 100-tool cap. CLI `devin mcp add [-s user/project/local] <name> -- <cmd> args` [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/cli/extensibility/mcp/configuration].
- **Global instructions**: Cascade `~/.codeium/windsurf/memories/global_rules.md` (always on, 6,000-char cap); workspace `.devin/rules/*.md` (fallback `.windsurf/rules/`, legacy `.windsurfrules`; 12,000 chars each; frontmatter `trigger: always_on/model_decision/glob/manual`, `globs`); `AGENTS.md` (root = always-on, subdir = glob) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/memories]. Devin Local: `~/.config/devin/AGENTS.md`, `~/.devin/rules/*.md`, `~/.devin/global_rules.md`, project `AGENTS.md`/`AGENTS.local.md`, and Claude's `CLAUDE.md` + `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/cli/extensibility/rules].
- **Skills**: `.devin/skills/<n>/SKILL.md` (legacy `.windsurf/skills`), `.agents/skills`; global `~/.codeium/windsurf/skills/`, `~/.config/devin/skills/`, `~/.agents/skills/`, plus `.claude/skills` when Claude import is on; agentskills.io format (`name`, `description`) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/skills, https://docs.devin.ai/cli/extensibility/skills/overview].
- **Hooks (legacy Cascade `hooks.json`)** [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/hooks]: files merged system -> user -> workspace: `~/.codeium/windsurf/hooks.json` (JetBrains `~/.codeium/hooks.json`), `.devin/hooks.json` (fallback `.windsurf/hooks.json`), `/Library/Application Support/Devin/hooks.json` (Linux `/etc/devin/`, Windows `C:\ProgramData\Devin\`). Shape `{"hooks":{"pre_user_prompt":[{"command":"<cmd>","powershell"?:..,"show_output":false,"working_directory"?:..}]}}`. 12 events: `pre_read_code`, `post_read_code`, `pre_write_code`, `post_write_code`, `pre_run_command`, `post_run_command`, `pre_mcp_tool_use`, `post_mcp_tool_use`, `pre_user_prompt` (`tool_info.user_prompt`), `post_cascade_response` (`tool_info.response`), `post_cascade_response_with_transcript` (`tool_info.transcript_path`), `post_setup_worktree`. stdin: `agent_action_name, trajectory_id, execution_id, timestamp, model_name, tool_info`. Exit 2 blocks (pre-hooks only; the agent sees stderr); other codes proceed. NO session-start event and NO documented context injection (stdout only shown in the UI when `show_output`); hooks do not run in Restricted Mode.
- **Hooks (Devin Local / CLI, current)** [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/cli/extensibility/hooks/overview, .../lifecycle-hooks]: events `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `PermissionRequest`, `UserPromptSubmit` (`prompt`), `Stop`, `PostCompaction`, `SessionStart` (`source`), `SessionEnd` (`reason`); files `.devin/hooks.v1.json` (whole file = hooks object), `"hooks"` key in `.devin/config.json`, `.devin/config.local.json`, user `~/.config/devin/config.json`, and Claude-format `.claude/settings*.json`, `~/.claude.json`, `~/.claude/settings*.json` (read by default via `read_config_from.claude`). stdin `hook_event_name, session_id, prompt_id, tool_name, tool_input`; stdout JSON `decision` approve/block, `reason`, `hookSpecificOutput.{hookEventName,additionalContext,updatedInput}`; exit 2 = block. INJECTS `additionalContext` for `SessionStart`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `PostToolUse`. `devin migrate hooks` converts `.windsurf/hooks.json`.
- **Plugins / extensions**: Devin plugins (skills, rules, hooks, MCP servers, subagents; `devin plugins ...`, marketplace) for Devin Local; editor extensions are VS Code-style (separate) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/cli/extensibility/plugins/overview].
- **Slash commands**: Cascade workflows `.devin/workflows/*.md` (fallback `.windsurf/workflows/`), global `~/.codeium/windsurf/global_workflows/*.md`, 12,000-char cap, invoked `/<name>`; unsupported in Devin Local (use skills; `.claude/commands` import as skills) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/cascade/workflows, https://docs.devin.ai/desktop/devin-local].
- **Headless**: the Desktop shell command only opens files/folders (VS Code-style); no Cascade headless mode. Devin Local/CLI: `devin -p "<q>"` (`--prompt-file`, `-c`, `-r <id>`, `--permission-mode`, `--model`, `--export [PATH]`, `--respect-workspace-trust false` for CI) [doc: https://docs.devin.ai/cli/reference/commands].

**Best recall injection point:** for Devin Local (the only agent since 2026-09-08) a `SessionStart` / `UserPromptSubmit` command hook returning `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext`, registered in `~/.config/devin/config.json` `"hooks"` (or the Claude-format `~/.claude/settings.json`, which Devin reads by default - an existing Claude Code recall hook may already apply); fallback `~/.config/devin/AGENTS.md`. Legacy Cascade has no injecting hook: only `memories/global_rules.md` (6,000 chars) or a skill. MCP: `devin mcp add -s user recall -- recall serve` (writes `~/.config/devin/mcp_config.json`); `~/.codeium/windsurf/mcp_config.json` and `~/.claude.json` are also imported.
**Confidence / unknowns:** high on rename, Cascade removal, hooks, rules, skills, MCP (official docs fetched 2026-10-06); Devin store layout and the `.pb` cipher are [3p], unofficial and unstable (17 schema migrations); unresolved: whether Desktop-launched Devin Local shares `~/.local/share/devin/cli/sessions.db`, the macOS GUI data root, the `sessions.db` timestamp unit, and which `mcp_config.json` path current Desktop builds read.
