## VS Code GitHub Copilot Chat (Microsoft / microsoft/vscode, extensions/copilot; Stable and Insiders)


Observed: the chat session index is the `chat.ChatSessionStore.index` key in the `ItemTable` of `globalStorage/state.vscdb`; workspace databases also carry `chat.customModes` and `workbench.panel.chat` keys [observed].
**Version verified:** VS Code Stable 1.140.0 (2026-09-30) [doc: https://github.com/microsoft/vscode/releases/tag/1.140.0]; source microsoft/vscode main@351b261 (2026-10-06), in-tree extension `GitHub.copilot-chat` 0.70.0 [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/package.json#L2-L11]; docs microsoft/vscode-docs@279a4a7 (pages dated 9/30/2026) [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agent-customization/hooks].

### 1. Detection
- App `Visual Studio Code.app`, CFBundleIdentifier `com.microsoft.VSCode` [inferred: docs only name the helper bundle `com.microsoft.VSCode.helper`] [doc: https://github.com/microsoft/vscode-docs/blob/279a4a7/release-notes/v1_28.md#L390]; Insiders `Visual Studio Code - Insiders.app` / `com.microsoft.VSCodeInsiders` [inferred: naming pattern, not found in docs or source]. CLI `code` / `code-insiders` with `chat` and `agent` subcommands and `--add-mcp` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/environment/node/argv.ts#L48-L136].
- Copilot Chat is extension `GitHub.copilot-chat` (in-tree `extensions/copilot`). Chat state keys exist here with no `github.copilot*` under `~/.vscode/extensions`, so do not use the extensions dir as the only signal [observed]. Prefer: `chat.*` keys in `User/globalStorage/state.vscdb`, `workspaceStorage/*/chatSessions/`, `globalStorage/emptyWindowChatSessions/`, `globalStorage/github.copilot-chat/`, `<user-data>/agentSessionData/`.
- Data root `<user-data>/User`: macOS `~/Library/Application Support/Code/User`; Windows `%APPDATA%\Code\User`; Linux `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/Code/User` or `~/.config/Code/User`; Insiders folder `Code - Insiders`; portable `$VSCODE_PORTABLE/user-data/User` (`data/` beside the app; macOS `code-portable-data` beside the .app); relocation by `--user-data-dir`, `VSCODE_APPDATA`, `VSCODE_PORTABLE` [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/configure/settings] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/configure/profiles] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/setup/portable] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/environment/node/userDataPath.ts#L24-L99]. `workspaceStorage` is `<user-data>/User/workspaceStorage` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/environment/common/environmentService.ts#L56-L86].
- Two session engines: "Local" harness (extension host, this file's main subject) and Agent Host harnesses Copilot/Claude/Codex; the Copilot harness discovers `$COPILOT_HOME/session-state` (default `~/.copilot`) [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/concepts/agent-host] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/agentHost/node/copilot/copilotAgent.ts#L988] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/environment/common/copilotHome.ts#L9-L10]. Forks: no Copilot Chat artifacts in Cursor/Windsurf here [observed]; fork behaviour not investigated.

### 2. History location
| Store (under `<user-data>/User/`) | Path | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Local sessions, folder or workspace windows | `workspaceStorage/<hash>/chatSessions/<sessionId>.jsonl` (>=1.109) or `.json` (<1.109) | `<hash>` = workspace id; folder from `workspaceStorage/<hash>/workspace.json` = `{"folder":"<uri>"}` or `{"workspace":"<uri>"}`, written once when the dir is created, never for empty windows [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionStore.ts#L71-L79] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/storage/electron-main/storageMain.ts#L416-L490] |
| Empty windows / transferred | `globalStorage/emptyWindowChatSessions/<id>.jsonl` (old: `workspaceStorage/no-workspace/chatSessions/`); `globalStorage/transferredChatSessions/<id>.json` | [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionStore.ts#L71-L79, L100-L135] |
| Session index | ItemTable key `chat.ChatSessionStore.index` in `workspaceStorage/<hash>/state.vscdb` (folder windows) or `globalStorage/state.vscdb` (empty windows) | scope chosen in `getIndexStorageScope` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionStore.ts#L432-L436] |
| Extension transcript | `workspaceStorage/<hash>/GitHub.copilot-chat/transcripts/<sessionId>.jsonl`, newest 20 kept | id casing of the dir may differ, match case-insensitively [inferred] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/chat/vscode-node/sessionTranscriptService.ts#L23-L66] |
| Extension "Chronicle" store | `globalStorage/github.copilot-chat/session-store.db` (SQLite, WAL) | [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/extension/vscode-node/services.ts#L280-L288] |
| Agent Host | `<user-data>/agentSessionData/<sessionKey>/session.db` plus orchestrator `agent-host.db` (directory not pinned); Copilot harness chats live in `~/.copilot/session-state/` (see CLI section); Claude/Codex harness chats are discovered from those apps' own stores | [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/agentHost/node/sessionDataService.ts#L52-L74] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/agentHost/common/sessionDataService.ts#L17] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/agentHost/node/agentHostServices.ts#L90] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/run/sessions/manage-sessions] |
| Cloud | Sessions upload to the user's GitHub account by default (`chat.sessionSync.enabled` true; needs `github.copilot.chat.localIndex.enabled` true); local files remain the full record | [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/run/sessions/session-history] |

### 3. Storage format
- **Generations.** (1) pre-Mar-2025 ItemTable key `interactive.sessions`, migrated to files only when the index key is missing [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/chatService/chatServiceImpl.ts#L67] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionStore.ts#L611-L625] (date: [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/copilot_vscode.rs#L25-L28]); (2) flat `<id>.json` = one `ISerializableChatData` object (<1.109); (3) `<id>.jsonl` mutation log (>=1.109, used unless setting `chat.useLogSessionStorage` is false; reader prefers `.jsonl`, falls back to `.json`) [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionStore.ts#L653-L690, L731-L742].
- **JSONL entries** (one JSON object per line): `{"kind":0,"v":<whole session>}` initial/compaction snapshot; `{"kind":1,"k":[path...],"v":x}` set at path (path = object keys and array indexes); `{"kind":2,"k":[path...],"v":[items]?,"i":n?}` array push (if `i` is present truncate the array to length `i` first, then append `v`); `{"kind":3,"k":[path...]}` delete. Replay: state = line 1 `.v`, apply later lines in order; no initial line or zero lines is an error [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/objectMutationLog.ts#L195-L215, L345-L405, L470-L501]. Writer appends diffs, but replaces the whole file with a fresh snapshot on first write and when more than 512 entries accumulate [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/objectMutationLog.ts#L315, L410-L450]; requests already complete/cancelled/failed are "sealed" and not diffed again [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionOperationLog.ts#L132-L185].
- **Session object v3**: `version:3`, `sessionId`, `creationDate` (ms), `customTitle`, `initialLocation` (`panel`, `terminal`, `notebook`, ...), `responderUsername`, `requests[]`, `inputState`, `hasPendingEdits`, `repoData`, `pendingRequests[]`, `workingDirectory` (URI string) [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionOperationLog.ts#L203-L216] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatModel.ts#L2196-L2235]. Request: `requestId`, `timestamp` (ms), `message{text,parts[{kind:text/var/tool/toolset/agent/subcommand/slash/...,text,range}]}`, `variableData{variables[{kind,id,name,value}]}`, `modelId`, `modeInfo`, `agent{id,name,extensionId{value}}`, `response[]`, `responseId`, `responseTimestamp`, `result{errorDetails,timings,metadata}`, `modelState{value}` (0 Pending, 1 Complete, 2 Cancelled, 3 Failed, 4 NeedsInput), `promptTokens`, `completionTokens`, `contextUsage`, `copilotCredits`, `elapsedMs`, `isSystemInitiated`, `hiddenFromTranscript` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatModel.ts#L2037-L2085] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/chatService/chatService.ts#L1858-L1864].
- **Response parts**: markdown text is a bare `IMarkdownString` `{value,isTrusted?,supportHtml?,uris?}` with no `kind`; everything else has `kind`: `thinking{value,id,generatedTitle,reasoningDurationMs}`, `toolInvocationSerialized{toolId,toolCallId,invocationMessage,pastTenseMessage,originMessage,isConfirmed,isComplete,resultDetails,resultError,toolSpecificData,source,subAgentInvocationId}`, `inlineReference`, `textEditGroup`, `notebookEditGroup`, `codeblockUri`, `confirmation`, `progressMessage`, `warning`, `info`, `treeData`, `command`, `hook{hookType,stopReason,systemMessage}`, `elicitationSerialized`, `questionCarousel`, `planReview`, `pullRequest`, `undoStop`, `multiDiffData`, `workspaceEdit`, `externalEdit`, `progressTaskSerialized`, `systemNotification`, `disabledClaudeHooks` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionOperationLog.ts#L44-L113] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/chatService/chatService.ts#L635-L680, L1199-L1222]. Tool args/results: `toolSpecificData` is `{kind:'input',rawInput}` or `{kind:'terminal',commandLine{original}}` or `subagent`...; `resultDetails` is `{input:string,output:[{type:'embed',value,isText?,mimeType?} or {type:'ref',uri}],isError?}` or a URI/Location array [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/tools/languageModelToolsService.ts#L286-L294] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/chatService/chatService.ts#L847-L852].

| Concept | Field |
|---|---|
| session id | file name; `sessionId` |
| title | `customTitle` (v3; v2 `computedTitle`); index `entries[id].title` |
| project / cwd | `workspaceStorage/<hash>/workspace.json`; index `entries[id].workingDirectory` (sessions window only); `repoData` |
| created / updated | `creationDate`; index `entries[id].lastMessageDate`, `timing{created,lastRequestStarted,lastRequestEnded}`; per request `timestamp`, `responseTimestamp` |
| user text | `requests[i].message.text` (+ `variableData.variables` attachments) |
| assistant text | concat `requests[i].response[]` parts that have `value` and no `kind`; reasoning in `kind:'thinking'` |
| tool calls / results | `response[]` parts `kind:'toolInvocationSerialized'` (`toolId`, `toolCallId`, `toolSpecificData`, `resultDetails`, `invocationMessage`, `pastTenseMessage`) |
| model / tokens | `modelId`, `modeInfo`, `promptTokens`, `completionTokens`, `copilotCredits`, `elapsedMs`; Copilot sessions: `agent.extensionId.value` starts with `github.copilot` [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/copilot_vscode.rs#L46] |

- **Index entry** (`{version:1,entries:{<sessionId>:{sessionId,title,lastMessageDate,timing,initialLocation,hasPendingEdits,stats,lastResponseState,workingDirectory,isEmpty,isExternal,permissionLevel,inputState}}}`); at most 400 non-external entries are kept (oldest dropped from the index only; whether their files are later removed was not found) [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionStore.ts#L37, L438-L455, L780-L833].
- **Format changes.** v1 (no `version`) -> v2 (`computedTitle`) -> v3 (`customTitle`), `message` string -> object, `isCanceled`/`isHidden` -> `modelState`/`shouldBeRemovedOnSend`, `initialLocation` `editing-session` -> `panel`; `requesterUsername` is not in the v3 schema [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatModel.ts#L2206-L2218, L2422-L2462]. Index entries before 1.109 lack `timing` (backfilled) [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionStore.ts#L575-L590].
- **Incremental import.** Poll `chatSessions/*.jsonl` by (mtime,size); re-replay a changed file in full (cheap, and required after a compaction rewrite); treat a shrink or changed first line as rewritten. Both `.json` and `.jsonl` can exist for one id; prefer `.jsonl`. The index is flushed on state save and shutdown (`flushIndex`, `updateAndFlushIndexSync`, shutdown join) so use the files, not the index, for freshness [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionStore.ts#L87-L106, L185-L205, L349-L385, L422-L436, L759-L770]. `state.vscdb` is plain SQLite (no `-wal` files here [observed]); the Chronicle DB runs in WAL mode, so read it with `mode=ro` because `immutable=1` ignores the WAL and can miss recent commits [inferred]. `transcripts/*.jsonl` is simpler (envelope `{id,parentId,timestamp,type,data}`; types `session.start`, `user.message{content,attachments}`, `assistant.turn_start`, `assistant.message{content,toolRequests,reasoningText}`, `tool.execution_start{toolCallId,toolName,arguments}`, `tool.execution_complete{toolCallId,success,result{content}}`, `assistant.turn_end`) but only 20 are kept and it is written by the Local agent's tool-calling loop [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/chat/vscode-node/sessionTranscriptService.ts#L21-L23, L122-L130, L352-L368] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/intents/node/toolCallingLoop.ts#L1221].
- **Chronicle SQLite** (`session-store.db`, schema_version 3): `sessions(id,cwd,repository,host_type,branch,summary,agent_name,agent_description,created_at,updated_at)`, `turns(session_id,turn_index,user_message,assistant_response,timestamp)`, `checkpoints`, `session_files(file_path,tool_name,turn_index)`, `session_refs(ref_type,ref_value)`, FTS5 `search_index(content,session_id,source_type,source_id)`; no tool calls or token usage; gated by `github.copilot.chat.localIndex.enabled` (default true) [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/platform/chronicle/node/sessionStore.ts#L38, L129, L178-L262] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/run/sessions/session-history].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Plaintext JSON/JSONL and SQLite, no encryption found [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatSessionStore.ts#L349-L385]. Local files exist only for sessions run on the same machine; cloud-synced copies are not exposed through any documented local API [inferred].
- Official export: Command Palette `Chat: Export Chat...` (`workbench.action.chat.export`) writes `chat.json` = `toExport()` = `{initialLocation,requests[],responderUsername}` with the same request/response shapes (no `sessionId`, `creationDate`, `customTitle`); also `Chat: Export Chat as Zip...` (`chat.json` plus `chat.repo.begin.json`/`chat.repo.end.json`) and `Chat: Import Chat...` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/browser/actions/chatImportExport.ts#L23-L105] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/model/chatModel.ts#L3547-L3590] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/electron-browser/actions/chatExportZip.ts#L28-L101]. No CLI export found.

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP.** Preferred (portable): user `$COPILOT_HOME/mcp-config.json` (default `~/.copilot/mcp-config.json`) or workspace `.mcp.json`, top-level `mcpServers`; the Agent Host reads these natively. VS Code format (deprecated for new servers): `.vscode/mcp.json` and `<User>/mcp.json` (profile), top-level `servers`, optional `inputs`, `sandbox`. Stdio snippet `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"type":"stdio","command":"recall","args":["serve"]}}}` (VS Code form: `{"servers":{"recall":{"type":"stdio","command":"recall","args":["serve"]}}}`). CLI: `code --add-mcp '{"name":"recall","command":"recall","args":["serve"]}'` (user profile). Settings: `chat.mcp.discovery.enabled` (off by default) imports Claude Desktop, Copilot CLI, Cursor, Windsurf configs [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agent-customization/mcp-servers] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/reference/mcp-configuration] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/environment/node/argv.ts#L136].
- **Global instructions.** Project: `.github/copilot-instructions.md`, `AGENTS.md` (`chat.useAgentsMdFile`, default true), `CLAUDE.md` (`chat.useClaudeMdFile`, default true), `.github/instructions/**/*.instructions.md` (`applyTo` glob frontmatter), `.claude/rules`. User: `~/.copilot/copilot-instructions.md` (Agent Host sessions), `~/.copilot/instructions/`, `~/.claude/rules/`, and for the Local agent `<User>/prompts/*.instructions.md` [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agent-customization/custom-instructions] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/reference/ai-settings] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/promptSyntax/config/promptFileLocations.ts#L184-L192].
- **Skills** (Agent Skills, `SKILL.md`, `chat.useAgentSkills` default true): `.agents/skills`, `.github/skills`, `.claude/skills`, `~/.agents/skills`, `~/.copilot/skills`, `~/.claude/skills` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/promptSyntax/config/promptFileLocations.ts#L172-L179]. Custom agents `*.agent.md` in `.github/agents`, `.claude/agents`, `~/.copilot/agents`, `~/.claude/agents` (legacy `.github/chatmodes/*.chatmode.md`) [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/promptSyntax/config/promptFileLocations.ts#L25-L30, L107-L122, L201-L206].
- **Hooks (Local harness, Preview, `chat.useHooks` default true).** Files: `.github/hooks/*.json`, `~/.copilot/hooks/*.json`, plus `.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.local.json`, `~/.claude/settings.json` only when `chat.useClaudeHooks` (default false); extra paths via `chat.hookFilesLocations` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/promptSyntax/config/promptFileLocations.ts#L212-L218] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/reference/ai-settings]. Format `{"hooks":{"SessionStart":[{"type":"command","command":"...","windows":"","linux":"","osx":"","cwd":"","env":{},"timeout":30}]}}`; Local ignores `matcher` [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/reference/ai-settings]; Copilot-CLI-format files (top-level `version`, camelCase events, `bash`/`powershell`/`timeoutSec`) are also parsed [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/promptSyntax/hookSchema.ts#L184-L262]. Events (8): `SessionStart` (first prompt of a new session; stdin `source:"new"`,`model`,`agent_type`), `UserPromptSubmit` (`prompt`), `PreToolUse` (`tool_name`,`tool_input`,`tool_use_id`), `PostToolUse` (+`tool_response`), `PreCompact` (`trigger`), `SubagentStart`/`SubagentStop` (`agent_id`,`agent_type`), `Stop` (`stop_hook_active`); every payload also has `timestamp` (ISO), `hook_event_name`, `session_id`, `transcript_path` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/platform/chat/common/chatHookService.ts#L98-L290] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/chat/vscode-node/chatHookService.ts#L126-L131]. Run via shell with cwd=home unless `cwd`, default timeout 30 s; exit 0 = success (stdout parsed as JSON), exit 2 = blocking error, other non-zero = warning [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/platform/chat/node/hookExecutor.ts#L17, L136-L157].
  **Context injection:** `SessionStart` reads only `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` (top-level key ignored; errors ignored); `UserPromptSubmit` reads `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` or top-level `additionalContext`, plus `decision:"block"`; `SubagentStart` same as SessionStart; `PreToolUse`/`PostToolUse` `additionalContext` is appended to the tool result inside `<*-context>` tags [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/prompts/node/panel/toolCalling.tsx#L611-L660] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/reference/hooks-reference]; `Stop`/`SubagentStop` can block with `hookSpecificOutput.decision:"block"`+`reason`, which is sent back as an extra user message [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/prompts/node/panel/toolCalling.tsx#L196-L197]; `PreCompact` is notification-only. Collected strings are joined with newlines and rendered as "Additional instructions from hooks: ..." in the `<context>` block of the user message [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/intents/node/toolCallingLoop.ts#L1018-L1046] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/prompt/node/defaultIntentRequestHandler.ts#L371-L397] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/extension/prompts/node/agent/agentPrompt.tsx#L534, L627-L632]. Agent Host Copilot/Claude/Codex sessions ignore these and run the provider's own hooks (Copilot = CLI hooks, see section 2 of the CLI part) [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agent-customization/hooks].
- **Plugins / extensions.** Agent Plugins (open standard agent-plugins.org: `plugin.json` + `skills/` + `mcp.json`, client extras `agents/`, `commands/`, `hooks/hooks.json`), enabled by `chat.plugins.enabled` (default false) [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agent-customization/agent-plugins] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/reference/ai-settings]. Extension manifest points `mcpServerDefinitionProviders` (+ `vscode.lm.registerMcpServerDefinitionProvider`), `languageModelTools` (+ `vscode.lm.registerTool`), `chatParticipants` (+ `vscode.chat.createChatParticipant`), `chatInstructions`/`chatAgents`/`chatPromptFiles`/`chatSkills` contribution points; dynamic provider APIs are in a proposed d.ts [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vscode-dts/vscode.d.ts#L20124, L20778, L20842] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/promptSyntax/chatPromptFilesContribution.ts#L32-L35]. No public API to read stored chat history was found.
- **Slash commands.** Prompt files `*.prompt.md` in `.github/prompts` and `<User>/prompts` invoked as `/name` [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/workbench/contrib/chat/common/promptSyntax/config/promptFileLocations.ts#L15, L97].
- **Headless.** None for chat: `code chat [-m ask/edit/agent/<custom>] [-a file] [-r] [-n] [--maximize] "prompt"` only opens the UI; `code agent host` starts a standalone Agent Host (JSON-RPC over WebSocket) [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/configure/command-line] [doc: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/agents/concepts/agent-host]. Use Copilot CLI for scripted runs.

**Best recall injection point:** `SessionStart` hook (Local harness) in `~/.copilot/hooks/recall.json` emitting `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"SessionStart","additionalContext":"..."},"additionalContext":"..."}` (dual output form, and a script that accepts both camelCase and snake_case stdin, lets the same file also serve Copilot CLI and Agent Host Copilot sessions [inferred]); static fallback `~/.copilot/instructions/recall.instructions.md` (`applyTo: "**"`) or `AGENTS.md`. Least fragile MCP registration: `~/.copilot/mcp-config.json` (`mcpServers`), with `code --add-mcp` for the Local-only profile.
**Confidence / unknowns:** High for the Local on-disk format and hooks (read from source at 351b261, nothing to test locally); unverified: whether the Local harness consumes `~/.copilot/mcp-config.json`/`.mcp.json` (docs list them as user/workspace locations), remote-window (SSH/WSL/container) storage location, Agent Host `agent-host.db`/`session.db` schemas, whether trimmed sessions' files are deleted, Insiders bundle id, and the `GitHub.copilot-chat` dir-name casing.

---

## GitHub Copilot CLI (GitHub / github/copilot-cli, npm @github/copilot)


**Version verified:** Copilot CLI v1.0.92 (npm latest, 2026-10-05; prerelease 1.0.93-1) [doc: https://github.com/github/copilot-cli/releases/tag/v1.0.92]; docs github/docs@45a0f05 (2026-10-05); github/copilot-sdk@6f67591 (2026-10-06) whose generated types describe the same runtime's events. The `github/copilot-cli` repo holds only README, changelog, install.sh (closed-source binary), so formats below come from official docs, the SDK and VS Code's embedded runtime [doc: https://github.com/github/copilot-cli/tree/6783c47] [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L1].

### 1. Detection
- Binary `copilot`: install script (`curl -fsSL https://gh.io/copilot-install | bash`) puts it in `$HOME/.local/bin` (non-root) or `/usr/local/bin` (root); `PREFIX`/`VERSION` env override; Homebrew cask `copilot-cli`; WinGet `GitHub.Copilot`; npm `@github/copilot` (bin `copilot` -> `npm-loader.js`, which spawns the native platform package `@github/copilot-<os>-<arch>`) [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:install.sh#L7-L10, L128-L132] [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:README.md#L39-L91] [doc: https://unpkg.com/@github/copilot@1.0.92/package.json]. `copilot --version` is safe to run. Auto-update package dir `~/.copilot/pkg` [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L2376].
- Data dir `~/.copilot` (`%USERPROFILE%\.copilot` on Windows), relocated by `COPILOT_HOME` (legacy `--config-dir` deprecated; old XDG locations auto-migrated); user settings are `~/.copilot/settings.json` (JSONC; `config.json` now holds only internal state incl. auth); cache dir `~/Library/Caches/copilot` (macOS), `$XDG_CACHE_HOME/copilot` or `~/.cache/copilot`, `%LOCALAPPDATA%/copilot`, override `COPILOT_CACHE_HOME` [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-config-dir-reference] [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/hooks-reference]. Other env: `COPILOT_PROVIDERS_CONFIG`, `COPILOT_CUSTOM_INSTRUCTIONS_DIRS`, `COPILOT_SKILLS_DIRS`, `COPILOT_MODEL`, `COPILOT_ALLOW_ALL`; tokens `COPILOT_GITHUB_TOKEN`/`GH_TOKEN`/`GITHUB_TOKEN` (never read) [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-command-reference].
- The same `~/.copilot/session-state` is shared by the GitHub Copilot desktop app and by VS Code's Agent Host Copilot harness [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/concepts/security-governance-and-network-settings/session-data] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:src/vs/platform/agentHost/node/copilot/copilotAgent.ts#L988]. The older `gh copilot` extension is archived (deprecated 2025-10-25) and is not covered [doc: https://github.com/github/gh-copilot#readme].

### 2. History location
- `~/.copilot/session-state/<sessionId>/` (UUID by default; `--session-id`/SDK may use other ids) with `events.jsonl`, `workspace.yaml`, `plan.md`, `checkpoints/` (`index.md` + files), `files/` [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/how-tos/copilot-cli/cli-best-practices] [src: github/copilot-sdk@6f67591:nodejs/test/e2e/session_fs.e2e.test.ts#L248-L275].
- Legacy (before 0.0.342, 2025-10-15): `~/.copilot/history-session-state/<id>.json`, migrated into the new layout on `--resume` [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L3220-L3223]; an even older flat `session-state/<id>.jsonl` is handled by [3p: agent-sessions@6fa9a73:AgentSessions/Services/CopilotSessionParser.swift#L4-L8].
- Also: `~/.copilot/session-store.db` (SQLite subset for search/Chronicle, rebuildable with `/chronicle reindex`), `~/.copilot/command-history-state/`, `~/.copilot/logs/process-<ts>-<pid>.log` [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-config-dir-reference]. Sessions are synced to the user's GitHub account by default (settings `remote`, `remoteExport`); the local copy is the complete record [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/concepts/security-governance-and-network-settings/session-data].

### 3. Storage format
- **`events.jsonl`**: one JSON object per line, envelope `{id (uuid), parentId (previous event id, null for first), timestamp (ISO 8601), type, data, agentId? (sub-agent), ephemeral?}`; events with `ephemeral:true` are never written (e.g. `session.title_changed`, `assistant.message_delta`, `assistant.usage`, `tool.execution_partial_result`, `session.idle`) [src: github/copilot-sdk@6f67591:nodejs/src/generated/session-events.ts#L1653-L1681, L2033, L2073, L6134, L6268, L7070]. Persisted (non-ephemeral) types of interest: `session.start`, `session.resume`, `session.model_change`, `session.context_changed`, `session.compaction_start/complete`, `session.truncation`, `session.shutdown`, `user.message`, `assistant.turn_start`, `assistant.message`, `assistant.reasoning`, `assistant.turn_end`, `tool.execution_start`, `tool.execution_complete`, `skill.invoked`, `subagent.started/completed/failed`, `hook.start/end`, `system.message`, `permission.requested/completed` [src: github/copilot-sdk@6f67591:nodejs/src/generated/session-events.ts#L1678-L8570].
- **`workspace.yaml`** (snake_case; fixture keys `id`, `cwd`, `summary_count`, `created_at`, `updated_at`; RPC `WorkspaceSummary` adds `git_root`, `repository`, `host_type`, `branch`, `name`, `user_named`) [src: github/copilot-sdk@6f67591:nodejs/test/e2e/transcript_recovery.e2e.test.ts#L28-L56] [src: github/copilot-sdk@6f67591:nodejs/src/generated/rpc.ts#L4781-L4800]; `name` read as the session name by [3p: agent-sessions@6fa9a73:AgentSessions/Services/CopilotSessionParser.swift#L440-L448].

| Concept | Field |
|---|---|
| session id | dir name; `session.start.data.sessionId`; `workspace.yaml: id` |
| title | `workspace.yaml: name` (+`user_named`); `session-store.db sessions.summary`; fallback first `user.message`; `session.title_changed` is ephemeral (not on disk) |
| project / cwd | `session.start.data.context{cwd,gitRoot,repository,branch,hostType}`; `session.resume.data.context`; `workspace.yaml: cwd,git_root,repository,branch` |
| created / updated | `session.start.data.startTime`; envelope `timestamp`; `workspace.yaml: created_at,updated_at` |
| user text | `user.message.data.content` (raw); `transformedContent` (model-facing); `attachments[]`; `agentMode` |
| assistant text | `assistant.message.data.content`, `reasoningText`, `messageId`, `turnId`, `model`, `outputTokens` |
| tool calls | `assistant.message.data.toolRequests[]{toolCallId,name,arguments,mcpServerName,mcpToolName}`; `tool.execution_start.data{toolCallId,toolName,arguments}` |
| tool results | `tool.execution_complete.data{toolCallId,success,result{content,detailedContent},error,fileEdits[]}` |
| model / tokens | `session.start.data.selectedModel`, `session.model_change.data.newModel`, `session.shutdown.data{modelMetrics,totalPremiumRequests,codeChanges,shutdownType}`; per-call usage is ephemeral |

  Field names from the `*Data` interfaces [src: github/copilot-sdk@6f67591:nodejs/src/generated/session-events.ts#L1683, L1742, L1840, L2441, L3102, L4484, L5689, L6021, L6928, L7216, L7316].
- **Format changes.** v1 `history-session-state/<id>.json` -> v2 `session-state/<id>/events.jsonl` at 0.0.342; `session.start.data.version` numbers the log schema (fixtures use 1, `producer:"copilot-agent"`) [src: github/copilot-sdk@6f67591:nodejs/test/e2e/transcript_recovery.e2e.test.ts#L33-L44]; session usage metrics added to the log at session end; 1.0.85 added session/memory import for an undocumented "semantic JSONL interchange format" [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L185, L2359, L3220-L3223].
- **Incremental import.** Append-only via `appendFile`, but treat as rewritable: the runtime backs up and repairs torn or corrupt tails (`events.jsonl.backup-before-recovery-*`; "resume sessions even when transcript files contain recoverable corruption") and an SDK test notes the file is rewritten at compaction; so key on (size, mtime, line count), tolerate a bad last line, and re-parse on shrink [src: github/copilot-sdk@6f67591:nodejs/test/e2e/transcript_recovery.e2e.test.ts#L84] [src: github/copilot-sdk@6f67591:nodejs/test/e2e/session_fs.e2e.test.ts#L296-L303] [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L174]. Resumed sessions append `session.resume` to the same file; cheap change detection is `workspace.yaml updated_at` or the dir mtime. Open `session-store.db` with `mode=ro` (its VS Code twin runs WAL; `immutable=1` would ignore the WAL [inferred]); its CLI schema is undocumented, the VS Code extension's equivalent (tables `sessions`, `turns`, `checkpoints`, `session_files`, `session_refs`, FTS5 `search_index`) is the nearest reference [inferred] [src: microsoft/vscode@351b261:extensions/copilot/src/platform/chronicle/node/sessionStore.ts#L178-L262].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Plaintext JSONL/YAML/SQLite, no encryption documented. Do not read `config.json` (auth state), `mcp-oauth-config/`, `mcp-secrets/`, `permissions-config.json` [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-config-dir-reference].
- Export: `/share [link/file/html/gist]` (alias `/export`), `--share[=PATH]` Markdown or `--share-gist` after `-p`; `/session [info/checkpoints/files/plan/rename/delete]`; remote sync to GitHub (Mission Control) by default [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-command-reference] [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/how-tos/copilot-cli/use-copilot-cli/chronicle].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP.** User `~/.copilot/mcp-config.json` (`$COPILOT_HOME`), project `.mcp.json` (any dir from cwd up to repo root) and `.github/mcp.json` (top-level `mcpServers` or bare server map); project files load only after folder trust (in `-p` mode need `GITHUB_COPILOT_PROMPT_MODE_WORKSPACE_MCP=true`); `.vscode/mcp.json` (`servers`) is not read. Snippet `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"type":"local","command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{},"tools":["*"]}}}`; `env` values are literals, prefix `$` to reference an env var. Commands: `copilot mcp add NAME -- CMD ARGS` (writes the user file), `list/get/remove/enable/disable`, per-session `--additional-mcp-config JSON|@file`; MCP servers and shell commands receive env `COPILOT_AGENT_SESSION_ID` (the current session id) [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L1821] [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/how-tos/copilot-cli/customize-copilot/add-mcp-servers] [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L3245-L3250].
- **Global instructions.** `~/.copilot/copilot-instructions.md`, `~/.copilot/instructions/**/*.instructions.md`; project `.github/copilot-instructions.md`, `.github/instructions/**/*.instructions.md` (`applyTo`), `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md` (+ `.claude/CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/rules`), `GEMINI.md`; extra dirs via `COPILOT_CUSTOM_INSTRUCTIONS_DIRS`; off with `--no-custom-instructions` [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/how-tos/copilot-cli/customize-copilot/add-custom-instructions] [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L77].
- **Skills** (`SKILL.md`, frontmatter `name`, `description`, `allowed-tools`, `user-invocable`): `.github/skills`, `.agents/skills`, `.claude/skills`, parent `.github/skills`, `~/.copilot/skills`, `~/.agents/skills`, `COPILOT_SKILLS_DIRS`, plugin dirs; `copilot skill add/list/remove/enable/disable`. Custom agents `*.agent.md` in `~/.copilot/agents`, `.github/agents`, `.claude/agents`; agents/skills/commands under `~/.claude/` are no longer loaded [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-command-reference] [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-config-dir-reference] [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L1708].
- **Hooks.** Sources merged in order policy (`/etc/github-copilot/policy.d/*.json`), repo `.github/hooks/*.json`, user `~/.copilot/hooks/*.json`, inline `hooks` in `.github/copilot/settings(.local).json`, repo-level `.claude/settings(.local).json` (user-level `~/.claude` is not documented as read) and `~/.copilot/settings.json`, plugin `hooks.json`. Format `{"version":1,"hooks":{"sessionStart":[{"type":"command","bash":"...","powershell":"...","cwd":"","env":{},"timeoutSec":30}]}}` (also `exec`+`args` and `http` entry types, and `{"type":"prompt","prompt":"text or /slash"}`, which on `sessionStart` only auto-submits that text for new interactive sessions (not on resume or `-p`); optional `matcher` regex only on `preToolUse`, `postToolUse`, `permissionRequest`, `preCompact`, `subagentStart`, `notification`). PascalCase event names select a VS-Code-compatible snake_case payload. Events: `sessionStart` (stdin `sessionId`,`timestamp`,`cwd`,`source` startup/resume/new,`initialPrompt`), `sessionEnd` (`reason`), `userPromptSubmitted` (`prompt`), `userPromptTransformed`, `preToolUse`, `postToolUse`, `postToolUseFailure`, `agentStop`, `subagentStart`, `subagentStop`, `preCompact`, `permissionRequest`, `notification`, `errorOccurred`. Exit 0 = JSON parsed from stdout, 2 = warning (deny for `preToolUse`/`permissionRequest`), others fail-open except `preToolUse` (fail-closed); timeout default 30 s, output cap 10 MiB [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/hooks-reference].
  **Context injection:** `sessionStart` stdout `{"additionalContext":"..."}` is injected into the conversation (multiple hooks joined by blank line); `subagentStart` `additionalContext` is prepended to the subagent's first message; `postToolUse` `additionalContext` is appended to the tool result; `notification` `additionalContext` becomes a prepended user message; `userPromptSubmitted` `modifiedPrompt` is honoured only by SDK programmatic hooks and command/HTTP output is documented as dropped, yet changelog 1.0.65 says its `additionalContext` is now included in the prompt (conflict, unresolved); `userPromptTransformed` (`modifiedTransformedPrompt`) rewrites model-facing content, command-hook support unconfirmed; `sessionEnd`, `preCompact`, `errorOccurred` have no output effect [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/hooks-reference] [src: github/copilot-cli@6783c47:changelog.md#L984, L2092].
- **Plugins / extensions.** `copilot plugin install/uninstall/list/update/enable/disable` and `plugin marketplace add/list/browse/update/remove`; plugin = dir with `plugin.json` (Agent Plugins 1.0, also reads `.claude-plugin/` and `.plugin/` manifests) bundling `skills/`, `agents/`, `commands/`, `hooks/hooks.json`, MCP/LSP config; installed under `~/.copilot/installed-plugins/`; `--plugin-dir` for local dev. Experimental CLI extensions: Node modules `extension.mjs` in `~/.copilot/extensions/<name>/` or `.github/extensions/<name>/` using the bundled SDK to add tools and slash commands [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-plugin-reference] [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/concepts/agents/copilot-cli/about-cli-extensions].
- **Slash commands.** Built-in set via `/help`; user-defined via skills (`/skill-name`), single-file `.claude/commands/*.md`, plugin `commands/`, extension commands [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-command-reference].
- **Headless.** `copilot -p "PROMPT"` (alias `--prompt`; exits after completion) with `--allow-all-tools`/`--yolo` (or `COPILOT_ALLOW_ALL`), `-s/--silent`, `--output-format json` (JSONL, one record per line; per-record schema undocumented, the dynamic-workflow final record is `{type:"workflow.result",data:{run...}}`, suggesting the same `{type,data}` event envelope [inferred]), `--resume[=id]`, `--continue`, `--session-id`, `-n NAME`, `-C DIR`, `--share[=PATH]`, `--model`, `--no-custom-instructions`; `copilot --acp [--stdio or --port N]` runs an Agent Client Protocol server (NDJSON) [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-command-reference] [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-programmatic-reference] [doc: https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/acp-server].

**Best recall injection point:** user-level hook `~/.copilot/hooks/recall.json` with `sessionStart` (fires for new and resumed sessions, `source` tells which) printing `{"additionalContext":"<memory>"}` (VS Code's Local harness also reads `~/.copilot/hooks`, but wants `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` and snake_case stdin, so the script should accept both spellings [inferred]); static fallback `~/.copilot/copilot-instructions.md`. Least fragile MCP registration: write the `recall` entry into `~/.copilot/mcp-config.json` (what `copilot mcp add` does), honouring `COPILOT_HOME`.
**Confidence / unknowns:** Formats are from GitHub docs plus the SDK's generated event types (binary is closed, so nothing is verified locally); unresolved: exact `workspace.yaml` key set, CLI `session-store.db` schema, `-p --output-format json` record shape, whether compaction rewrites `events.jsonl`, the `userPromptSubmitted` `additionalContext` conflict, and the 1.0.85 interchange format.

---

## Cline (Cline Bot Inc. / cline/cline)


**Version verified:** cline/cline main@cd80a20 (2026-10-06); VS Code ext v4.1.22 (2026-09-30); CLI cli-v3.0.68, Desktop desktop-v0.0.43, SDK v0.0.90 (all 2026-10-02) [doc: https://github.com/cline/cline/releases]

### 1. Detection
- One SDK engine, four surfaces: CLI (`npm i -g cline`, binary `cline`), Desktop app (macOS/Windows), VS Code ext `saoudrizwan.claude-dev`, JetBrains plugin (Marketplace 28247); library `@cline/sdk` [src: cline/cline@cd80a20:README.md#L44-L110].
- Desktop = Tauri app `Cline.app`, CFBundleIdentifier `bot.cline.app` (side-by-side channels `bot.cline.app.beta`, `.nightly`); asset `Cline_<ver>_universal.dmg` [src: cline/cline@cd80a20:apps/examples/desktop-app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json#L3-L5; .cline/skills/publish-desktop/SKILL.md#L12-L19].
- Data root `~/.cline` (env `CLINE_DIR`); data dir `~/.cline/data` (`CLINE_DATA_DIR`; CLI `--data-dir`); finer overrides `CLINE_SESSION_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_DB_DATA_DIR`, `CLINE_MCP_SETTINGS_PATH`, `CLINE_HOOKS_DIR` [src: cline/cline@cd80a20:sdk/packages/shared/src/storage/paths.ts#L151-L193,L240-L246,L440-L446; docs/cli/cli-reference.mdx#L236-L248]. Home resolution uses HOME then USERPROFILE, so Windows is `%USERPROFILE%\.cline\data` [src: paths.ts#L84-L103].
- Probe: `~/.cline/data/db/sessions.db` or `~/.cline/data/sessions/*/*.json` (SDK); `<globalStorage>/saoudrizwan.claude-dev/{tasks,state/taskHistory.json}` (legacy) [src: paths.ts; apps/vscode/src/sdk/sdk-task-history.ts#L35-L39].

### 2. History location
| Store | macOS | Linux / Windows |
|---|---|---|
| SDK sessions (CLI, Desktop, VS Code SDK bundle with `backendMode:"local"`; JetBrains [inferred: same engine]) | `~/.cline/data/sessions/<sessionId>/{<id>.json, <id>.messages.json, <id>.compaction.json}`; index `~/.cline/data/db/sessions.db` | `~/.cline/data/...`; `%USERPROFILE%\.cline\data\...` |
| VS Code legacy bundle, pre-SDK (<=3.89.x; still shipped inside the 4.1.x A/B VSIX) | `~/Library/Application Support/Code/User/globalStorage/saoudrizwan.claude-dev/{tasks/<taskId>/, state/taskHistory.json, settings/}` | `~/.config/Code/User/globalStorage/...`; `%APPDATA%\Code\User\globalStorage\...` |
| Classic CLI (pre-SDK) | `~/.cline/data/{tasks/<taskId>/, state/taskHistory.json}` | same under `~` |
- SDK paths [src: sdk/packages/core/src/services/session-artifacts.ts#L60-L94; services/storage/sqlite-session-store.ts#L22-L45]; VS Code SDK bundle uses `ClineCore.create({backendMode:"local"})` [src: apps/vscode/src/sdk/vscode-session-host.ts#L184-L185]; legacy paths [src: apps/vscode/src/sdk/legacy-state-reader.ts#L22-L75; apps/vscode/src/core/storage/disk.ts#L51-L52,L128-L129; core/controller/file/openDiskConversationHistory.ts#L14-L15].
- SDK sessions are editor-independent (one store for Code, Cursor, Windsurf, JetBrains, CLI). The SDK bundle also lists legacy tasks from BOTH `~/.cline/data` and VS Code globalStorage [src: apps/vscode/src/sdk/sdk-task-history.ts#L215-L222].
- Fork user-data dirs (same `<ext-id>` subdir): macOS `~/Library/Application Support/<App>/User/globalStorage/`, Linux `~/.config/<App>/User/globalStorage/`, Windows `%APPDATA%\<App>\User\globalStorage\`, App = Code, Code - Insiders, VSCodium, Cursor, Windsurf, Antigravity, Kiro. Code/Cursor/Windsurf/Antigravity `User/globalStorage` exist here [observed]; Code/Insiders/VSCodium/Cursor on 3 OSes [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/cline.rs#L37-L52]; Kiro [inferred: same VS Code fork layout].

### 3. Storage format
SDK per session: manifest `<id>.json` `{version:1, session_id, source, pid, started_at, ended_at?, exit_code?, status, interactive, provider, model, cwd, workspace_root, prompt?, metadata?, messages_path?, compaction_path?}`, `<id>.messages.json` envelope `{version:1, updated_at, agent:"lead"|"subagent"|"teammate", sessionId, taskType?, origin{source,mode,sessionId,parentThreadId?,subagent?,version?,trigger?}, messages[], system_prompt?}`, optional `<id>.compaction.json` [src: services/session-data.ts#L334-L359; session/models/session-manifest.ts#L6-L28; session/stores/session-manifest-store.ts#L112-L119]. `sessions.db` table `sessions` (WAL, busy_timeout 5000) [src: sdk/packages/shared/src/db/sqlite-db.ts#L188-L217,L351-L356].
| Concept | SDK store | Legacy task dir (`tasks/<id>/`) |
|---|---|---|
| session id | `sessions.session_id` = dir name = manifest `session_id` = messages `sessionId` | dir name = `HistoryItem.id` (`state/taskHistory.json`) |
| title | manifest/`metadata_json` `$.title` (<=120 chars, first prompt line); `sessions.prompt` | `HistoryItem.task` |
| project/cwd | `sessions.cwd`, `workspace_root` (no-folder chats use `~/.cline/data/workspaces/chat`) | `HistoryItem.cwdOnTaskInitialization` |
| created/updated | `started_at`, `ended_at`, `updated_at` (ISO), `status`; message `ts` (ms) | `HistoryItem.ts` (ms); `ClineMessage.ts` |
| user text | `messages[]` role `user`, `content` string or `{type:"text",text}`; may carry `<user_input mode=...>`/`<mode_notice>` wrappers; `metadata.displayRole:"system"` = injected hook context (skip) | `api_conversation_history.json` (Anthropic MessageParam[]); or `ui_messages.json` `say:"task"`, `say:"user_feedback"` |
| assistant text | role `assistant`, `{type:"text"}`; reasoning `{type:"thinking",thinking}` | `say:"text"`, `"reasoning"`, `"completion_result"` |
| tool calls | `{type:"tool_use",id,name,input}` | `tool_use` blocks; UI `ask/say:"tool"` (text = JSON), `"command"`, `"use_mcp_server"` |
| tool results | `{type:"tool_result",tool_use_id,name,content,is_error}` in user-role messages | `tool_result` blocks; UI `"command_output"`, `"mcp_server_response"` |
| model/tokens | message `modelInfo{id,provider,family}`, `metrics{inputTokens,outputTokens,cacheReadTokens,cacheWriteTokens,cost}`; `sessions.provider/model` | `HistoryItem.modelId,tokensIn,tokensOut,cacheReads,cacheWrites,totalCost`; `task_metadata.json{files_in_context,model_usage,environment_history}` |
| other | `source` (vscode/cli/desktop/jetbrains/...), `parent_session_id`, `is_subagent`, `agent_id`, `metadata.importedFrom` | `conversationHistoryDeletedRange`, `context_history.json` |
- Field sources [src: sdk/packages/shared/src/llms/messages.ts#L16-L165; core/services/session-data.ts#L232-L279; core/session/history-origin.ts#L24-L60; apps/vscode/src/shared/{HistoryItem.ts#L1-L26,ExtensionMessage.ts#L178-L230}; core/context/context-tracking/ContextTrackerTypes.ts#L28-L32]. Layout independently matches [3p: jazzyalex/agent-sessions@6fa9a73:AgentSessions/Services/ClineSessionDiscovery.swift#L3-L9; ClineSessionParser.swift#L7-L25].
- Format changes: classic task dirs (<=3.89.2) -> SDK sessions. 4.0.0 shipped the SDK runtime, 4.0.1 rolled back to 3.89.2 code, 4.1.0 shipped one VSIX with both bundles (staged % of windows on SDK) [src: CHANGELOG.md#L406,L516,L534-L543]. Whether a resumed legacy task keeps its id as the new SDK session id: unknown (migration seeds a new session) [src: apps/vscode/src/sdk/legacy-task-handling.ts#L95-L170].
- Incremental import: both `<id>.messages.json` and the manifest are fully rewritten with plain `writeFileSync` (non-atomic, so retry on JSON parse error); only `<id>.compaction.json` uses atomic temp+rename [src: session-manifest-store.ts#L112-L119,L220-L238,L305-L313]; detect change via manifest/messages mtime or messages `updated_at`; `status:"running"` sessions are still mutable. SQLite is WAL, so open `mode=ro` (not `immutable=1`) while Cline runs; prefer manifests as discovery unit (no SQLite needed) [3p: agent-sessions ClineSessionDiscovery.swift#L95-L115]. Subagent transcripts: `<agentId>.messages.json` inside the root session dir [src: session-artifacts.ts#L132-L144]. Skip `metadata.importedFrom` sessions (Cline imports Claude Code/Codex/OpenCode sessions into its store; duplicates) [src: services/session-import/types.ts#L3-L16; service.ts#L20-L28]. Hub daemon keeps separate `db/hub-events*.db`, `hub-runs*.db` (not transcripts) [src: sdk/packages/core/src/hub/server/hub-event-log.ts#L51-L52].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- No encryption seen; transcripts are plain JSON/SQLite. Credentials are separate: `~/.cline/data/secrets.json` (mode 0600) and `settings/providers.json` - never read [src: apps/vscode/src/shared/storage/storage-context.ts#L28-L29,L140-L143; paths.ts#L424-L430].
- Export: `cline history --json` (list), `cline history export` (self-contained HTML), `cline history delete` [src: apps/cli/src/commands/history-command.ts; apps/cli/src/session/export.ts#L11-L34].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP:** `~/.cline/data/settings/cline_mcp_settings.json` (`CLINE_MCP_SETTINGS_PATH`), shared by CLI/Desktop/VS Code SDK bundle; legacy bundle `<globalStorage>/saoudrizwan.claude-dev/settings/cline_mcp_settings.json`; no project-scope file found. `cline mcp` is an interactive wizard (add/edit/enable/delete), `cline config mcp --json` lists [src: docs/mcp/mcp-overview.mdx#L24-L64,L142-L147; core/storage/disk.ts#L22,L128-L138; CHANGELOG.md#L539].
  `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{},"disabled":false,"autoApprove":[]}}}` (remote: `"type":"streamableHttp"|"sse"`, `url`, `headers`; omitting `type` means legacy SSE) [src: mcp-overview.mdx#L70-L104].
- **Global instructions:** `~/.agents/AGENTS.md`, `~/.cline/rules/`, `~/Cline/Rules/`, `~/Documents/Cline/Rules/`; project `AGENTS.md`, `.clinerules` (file or dir), `.cline/rules/` [src: paths.ts#L517-L593]. (CLI reference tree showing `data/settings/{rules,skills}` conflicts with the resolvers; trust the resolvers [src: cli-reference.mdx#L284-L305].)
- **Skills:** yes, SKILL.md frontmatter; `~/.cline/skills`, `~/.agents/skills`, `<ws>/.clinerules/skills`, `<ws>/.cline/skills`, `<ws>/.agents/skills` [src: paths.ts#L461-L515; docs/customization/skills.mdx#L89,L154-L159]; `cline skill` wraps `npx skills` [src: apps/cli/src/commands/skill.ts#L8-L12].
- **Hooks (file):** scripts named after the event, in `~/Documents/Cline/Hooks/`, `~/.cline/hooks/`, `<ws>/.clinerules/hooks/`, `<ws>/.cline/hooks/` (+ CLI `--hooks-dir`); extensions none/.sh/.bash/.zsh/.js/.mjs/.cjs/.ts/.py/.ps1; no matcher syntax found (the script must filter on the payload itself) [src: paths.ts#L487-L501; core/hooks/hook-file-config.ts#L17-L62]. Events (file name -> payload `hookName`): TaskStart(agent_start), TaskResume(agent_resume), UserPromptSubmit(prompt_submit), PreToolUse(tool_call), PostToolUse(tool_result), TaskComplete(agent_end), TaskCancel(agent_abort), TaskError(agent_error), SessionShutdown(session_shutdown); PreCompact is not wired. Mapping to the generic notions: session-start = TaskStart/TaskResume, prompt-submit = UserPromptSubmit, session-end = TaskComplete/TaskCancel/TaskError/SessionShutdown [src: hook-file-config.ts#L30-L43; sdk/examples/hooks/README.md#L17-L28]. stdin JSON: `{clineVersion, hookName, timestamp, taskId, sessionContext, workspaceRoots[], workspaceInfo, userId, agent_id, parent_agent_id, tool_call{id,name,input} | tool_result{...} | userPromptSubmit{prompt}}` [src: sdk/packages/shared/src/hooks/events.ts#L168-L257]. stdout JSON `{cancel, review, context|contextModification, errorMessage, overrideInput}` [src: hook-file-hooks.ts#L191-L223].
  **Context injection:** SDK engine (CLI/Desktop): PreToolUse/PostToolUse `context` is injected (blocking, 120 s timeout); TaskStart/TaskResume run detached with stdio ignored and UserPromptSubmit is detached, so no injection (a host flag `blockingRunStartHooks` exists; code search finds no host that sets it) [src: hook-file-hooks.ts#L577-L606,L760-L840; sdk/CHANGELOG.md#L69]. VS Code extension: its own adapter maps TaskStart + UserPromptSubmit `contextModification` to a `<hook_context>` block on the run's first request (fixed in 4.1.20), plus Pre/PostToolUse; hooks default enabled (`userSetting ?? true`); 30 s timeout; hook scripts live in `~/Documents/Cline/Hooks/` and `<ws>/.clinerules/hooks/` and have NO file extension on Unix (`.ps1` on Windows) [src: apps/vscode/src/sdk/hooks-adapter.ts#L58-L75,L131-L145,L320-L412; core/hooks/hooks-utils.ts#L6-L8; core/hooks/hook-factory.ts#L25-L26; core/hooks/utils.ts#L48-L70,L95-L96; CHANGELOG.md#L54]. Whether TaskStart fires once per task or per turn: unverified.
- **Plugins:** TS/JS `AgentPlugin` modules in `~/.cline/plugins/`, `<ws>/.cline/plugins/`, `~/Documents/Cline/Plugins/`; `cline plugin install <npm|git|path>`; discovery via `package.json` `cline.plugins[].paths` or `index.ts`; runtime hooks `beforeRun/afterRun/beforeModel/afterModel/beforeTool/afterTool/onEvent`; `beforeRun` may return `{appendContext}` injected as `<hook_context source="RunStart">` [src: paths.ts#L610-L618,L701-L740; docs/sdk/plugins.mdx#L66,L99-L101; sdk/CHANGELOG.md#L69]. Agent Plugins (`plugin.json`) from `~/.agents/plugins` [src: paths.ts#L630-L632].
- **Slash commands:** workflows `<ws>/.clinerules/workflows/`, `~/Documents/Cline/Workflows/`, `~/.cline/workflows/`, `<ws>/.cline/workflows/` [src: paths.ts#L595-L608]; plugins can register commands [src: docs/sdk/plugins.mdx#L21-L28].
- **Headless:** `cline "<prompt>"` (headless when `--json`, piped stdin, or redirected stdout), `--json` = NDJSON `{type:"ask"|"say",say,text,ts,partial}`, `--auto-approve <bool>` (default true), `-t`, `-m`, `-P`, `-c/--cwd`, `--id <session>` resume, `--acp`, `-z` background hub; note `-p` means plan mode, not print; `cline hook` reads a hook payload on stdin [src: docs/cli/cli-reference.mdx#L27-L48,L164-L170,L264-L277; docs/usage/cli-overview.mdx#L25-L47].

**Best recall injection point:** MCP entry in `~/.cline/data/settings/cline_mcp_settings.json` (single file shared by CLI, Desktop and the VS Code SDK bundle) plus a managed global rule `~/.cline/rules/recall.md`; for dynamic load at session start use a VS Code `UserPromptSubmit`/`TaskStart` file hook (`contextModification`) and, for CLI/Desktop, a `~/.cline/plugins/` plugin whose `beforeRun` returns `appendContext` (file run-start hooks cannot inject there).
**Confidence / unknowns:** high on SDK layout (own source + agent-sessions agree); unknown: legacy-to-SDK id mapping, which windows run the SDK vs legacy bundle (staged rollout), JetBrains store location (inferred same `~/.cline/data`), cass's Cline connector omits the `tasks/` path segment and `~/.cline/data` so it likely misses real data [inferred from 3p: franken_agent_detection cline.rs#L220-L245 reading task dirs directly under the extension dir].

## Roo Code (Roo Code Inc. / RooCodeInc/Roo-Code) - DISCONTINUED 2026-05-15


The project is shut down and the repo archived (no 2026 maintenance), so only legacy data on user machines matters.
**Version verified:** RooCodeInc/Roo-Code main@b867ec9 (2026-05-15, archived); last release v3.54.0 (2026-05-15) [doc: https://github.com/RooCodeInc/Roo-Code/releases]. README: "The Roo Code Extension was shut down on May 15th"; points to Zoo Code (community fork) and Cline [src: RooCodeInc/Roo-Code@b867ec9:README.md#L66-L69]. Zoo Code: Zoo-Code-Org/Zoo-Code@d7963fc, ext `ZooCodeOrganization.zoo-code`, v3.86.0 (2026-10-03), same file names/layout and `~/.roo` dir [src: Zoo-Code-Org/Zoo-Code@d7963fc:src/package.json#L2-L6; src/shared/globalFileNames.ts#L1-L8; src/services/roo-config/index.ts#L28].

### 1. Detection
- VS Code ext `RooVeterinaryInc.roo-cline` (on-disk dir lower-case `rooveterinaryinc.roo-cline`, config namespace `roo-cline`) [src: src/package.json#L2-L5; src/utils/storage.ts#L18-L21]. No desktop app found. Roo CLI `roo` (`@roo-code/cli` 0.1.17): curl installer to `~/.roo/cli` + `~/.local/bin/roo` (macOS arm64/Linux x64; `ROO_INSTALL_DIR`, `ROO_BIN_DIR`) [src: apps/cli/README.md#L9-L54; apps/cli/package.json#L2-L3].
- Relocation: VS Code setting `roo-cline.customStoragePath` replaces the globalStorage base [src: src/utils/storage.ts#L14-L47]; CLI default base is `~/.vscode-mock/global-storage` (+ `workspace-storage/<hash>`), `--ephemeral` uses temp storage [src: packages/vscode-shim/src/context/ExtensionContext.ts#L83-L91; apps/cli/README.md#L138].

### 2. History location
- `<globalStorage>/rooveterinaryinc.roo-cline/tasks/<taskId>/`, `tasks/_index.json`, `settings/`, `cache/` [src: src/utils/storage.ts#L53-L78; src/core/task-persistence/TaskHistoryStore.ts#L11-L31]. macOS `~/Library/Application Support/Code/User/globalStorage/`; Linux `~/.config/Code/User/globalStorage/`; Windows `%APPDATA%\Code\User\globalStorage\`; same ext dir under Code - Insiders, VSCodium, Cursor, Windsurf, Antigravity, Kiro [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/cline.rs#L23,L37-L59 covers `rooveterinaryinc.roo-cline` for Code/Insiders/VSCodium/Cursor]; [observed: Code/Cursor/Windsurf/Antigravity `User/globalStorage` exist]; Kiro [inferred].
- Roo CLI sessions: `~/.vscode-mock/global-storage/tasks/<id>/` (same layout) [src: vscode-shim ExtensionContext.ts#L83-L87].

### 3. Storage format
| Concept | Field / file |
|---|---|
| session id | `tasks/<taskId>/` dir name (uuid) = `HistoryItem.id` |
| title | `HistoryItem.task` (text of first ui message) in `history_item.json` / `_index.json` |
| project/cwd | `HistoryItem.workspace` |
| created/updated | `HistoryItem.ts` (ms, last relevant message), `number` (seq); `ClineMessage.ts` |
| user text | `api_conversation_history.json` role `user` blocks (first wraps `<task>`; large `<environment_details>` noise); UI `ui_messages.json` first `say:"text"`, `say:"user_feedback"` |
| assistant text | api role `assistant` text blocks; UI `say:"text"`, `"reasoning"`, `"completion_result"` |
| tool calls / results | api `tool_use` / `tool_result` blocks (extras `ts`, `id`, `isSummary`, `condenseId/condenseParent`, `reasoning_content`); UI `ask/say:"tool"`, `ask:"command"`, `say:"command_output"`, `"mcp_server_response"` |
| model/tokens | `HistoryItem.tokensIn,tokensOut,cacheWrites,cacheReads,totalCost`, `mode`, `apiConfigName` |
| other | `rootTaskId,parentTaskId,childIds,status,delegatedToId` (delegated subtasks) |
- Sources [src: src/shared/globalFileNames.ts#L1-L9; src/core/task-persistence/apiMessages.ts#L12-L38; taskMessages.ts#L17-L44; packages/types/src/history.ts#L7-L29; packages/types/src/message.ts#L27-L39,L144-L173,L249-L273].
- Format changes: `claude_messages.json` (very old) -> `api_conversation_history.json` [src: apiMessages.ts#L72-L99]; history index moved from VS Code globalState key `taskHistory` (inside `state.vscdb`) to per-task `history_item.json` + `tasks/_index.json` (flag `taskHistoryMigratedToFiles`), introduced 2026-02-19 in b598efb (first release containing it not pinned) [src: src/core/webview/ClineProvider.ts#L293-L308; commit b598efb]. Kilo's new extension can import Roo tasks (`scanTaskStore` "discover" mode) [src: Kilo-Org/kilocode@0bdebc8:packages/kilo-vscode/src/legacy-migration/task-store.ts#L44-L55].
- Incremental: whole-file rewrites through `safeWriteJson` (atomic, `proper-lockfile`); `_index.json` debounced 2 s and re-reconciled every 5 min; discover by listing `tasks/*/history_item.json` mtimes, never depend on `_index.json` alone [src: TaskHistoryStore.ts#L61-L65; apiMessages.ts#L1].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Task files are plain JSON. Provider keys live in VS Code SecretStorage (`context.secrets`, in `state.vscdb`), not in task files [src: src/core/config/ContextProxy.ts#L68-L79,L261]. Export: only in-UI "export task" (not verified); no export CLI found.

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP:** global `<globalStorage>/rooveterinaryinc.roo-cline/settings/mcp_settings.json`, project `<ws>/.roo/mcp.json`; stdio `{command,args,env,type?}`, remote `{type:"sse"|"streamable-http",url,headers}`, common `timeout` (1-3600 s), `alwaysAllow[]`, `disabled`, `watchPaths`, `disabledTools[]` [src: src/services/mcp/McpHub.ts#L67-L137,L379,L487-L497,L594-L597]. No `roo mcp add` CLI found.
  `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"alwaysAllow":[],"disabled":false}}}`
- **Global instructions:** `~/.roo/rules/` + `rules-<mode>/`; project `.roo/rules/`, `.roo/rules-<mode>/`, legacy `.roorules`/`.clinerules`; `AGENTS.md`, `AGENT.md`, `AGENTS.local.md` at project root (default on); custom modes `.roomodes` and global `settings/custom_modes.yaml` [src: src/core/prompts/sections/custom-instructions.ts#L200-L232,L279-L336,L409-L436; src/services/roo-config/index.ts#L26-L28; src/core/config/CustomModesManager.ts#L19,L249].
- **Skills:** yes (agentskills.io name rules, `modeSlugs` frontmatter); `~/.agents/skills`, `.agents/skills`, `~/.roo/skills`, `.roo/skills` + `skills-<mode>` [src: src/services/skills/SkillsManager.ts#L98-L160,L567-L611].
- **Hooks:** none found (no hook engine in `src/`; tree search for hooks returns only React hooks) [src: tree of RooCodeInc/Roo-Code@b867ec9]. Plugins: none (MCP, modes, skills, commands only).
- **Slash commands:** `~/.roo/commands/*.md`, `<ws>/.roo/commands/*.md` (project overrides global overrides built-ins), gray-matter frontmatter [src: src/services/command/commands.ts#L124-L144].
- **Headless:** `roo --print "<prompt>" -w <dir>` with `--output-format text|json|stream-json`, `--stdin-prompt-stream` (NDJSON commands), `--create-with-session-id <uuid>`, `--require-approval`, `--ephemeral` [src: apps/cli/README.md#L94-L140].

**Best recall injection point:** project/global MCP file above plus a managed rule file `~/.roo/rules/recall.md` (loaded at every task start); no hook route. Given the shutdown, treat as import-only legacy and consider Zoo Code (same layout, ext id `ZooCodeOrganization.zoo-code`) instead.
**Confidence / unknowns:** high on final v3.54 layout (own source); unknown: exact release that introduced `history_item.json`, `state.vscdb` key spelling for the old globalState index (inferred from VS Code behavior), export UI, whether Zoo Code diverges later.

## Kilo Code (Kilo Code Inc. / Kilo-Org/kilocode)


**Version verified:** Kilo-Org/kilocode main@0bdebc8 (2026-10-06); v7.8.3 (2026-10-01, CLI `@kilocode/cli` latest and VS Code ext `kilocode.kilo-code` share the version) [src: packages/kilo-vscode/package.json#L2-L11; doc: https://github.com/Kilo-Org/kilocode/releases]. Old Roo-based extension archived at Kilo-Org/kilocode-legacy@ae046ac (last v5.16.2, 2026-06-04) [src: kilocode-legacy@ae046ac:src/package.json#L6].

### 1. Detection
- It is now an OpenCode fork: the CLI engine lives in `packages/opencode`, the VS Code extension is "a ground-up rebuild ... using Kilo CLI as the backend", JetBrains plugin and Agent Manager run the same bundled `kilo serve` [src: packages/kilo-vscode/docs/opencode-migration-plan.md#L1-L5; packages/kilo-docs/pages/contributing/architecture/cli-runtime.md#L39-L47]. Kilo merges upstream OpenCode (v1.18.x) continuously [src: packages/kilo-vscode/CHANGELOG.md#L71].
- Binaries: `kilo` and `kilocode` (npm `@kilocode/cli`, `brew install Kilo-Org/tap/kilo`, AUR `kilo-bin`, curl installer to `~/.kilo/bin/kilo`) [src: README.md#L44-L64; install#L70; npm registry bin map]. VS Code ext `kilocode.Kilo-Code` (on-disk dir `kilocode.kilo-code`); JetBrains plugin 28350 [src: README.md#L12,L73].
- Paths come from xdg-basedir with app name `kilo`: data `$XDG_DATA_HOME|~/.local/share` + `/kilo`, config `~/.config/kilo` (`KILO_CONFIG_DIR` overrides), cache `~/.cache/kilo`, state `~/.local/state/kilo` (or `<data>/state` when it already exists and `XDG_STATE_HOME` is unset) [src: packages/core/src/global.ts#L12-L42,L80; packages/core/src/kilocode/global.ts#L38-L51]; DB override `KILO_DB` (absolute, or file name under data dir), `kilo db path` prints the live path [src: packages/opencode/src/storage/db.ts#L33-L47; kilo-docs/pages/code-with-ai/agents/session-history.md#L137-L154]. Config dirs also include `.kilo` and legacy `.kilocode` found upward from cwd and in `~` [src: packages/opencode/src/config/paths.ts#L23-L41].
- The extension spawns `kilo serve` with `KILO_DISABLE_CHANNEL_DB=true` (so plain `kilo.db`) and does not move XDG dirs: CLI, VS Code, JetBrains share one DB [src: packages/kilo-vscode/src/services/cli-backend/server-manager.ts#L35,L149-L158].

### 2. History location
| Store | macOS | Linux / Windows |
|---|---|---|
| New (7.x, CLI+VS Code+JetBrains) | `~/.local/share/kilo/kilo.db` (+ `-wal`, `-shm`) | `~/.local/share/kilo/kilo.db`; `%USERPROFILE%\.local\share\kilo\kilo.db`; VS Code Remote: on the remote host [doc/src: session-history.md#L147-L154] |
| Pre-SQLite JSON (opencode lineage) | `~/.local/share/kilo/storage/{project/*.json, session/<projectId>/<id>.json, message/<sessionId>/<id>.json, part/<messageId>/<id>.json}` | same; one-time migration into `kilo.db` on first DB creation [src: packages/opencode/src/kilocode/storage/json-migration.ts#L78,L210-L219; cli-runtime.md#L211] |
| Legacy extension (<=5.16.2, Roo layout) | `~/Library/Application Support/Code/User/globalStorage/kilocode.kilo-code/tasks/<taskId>/{api_conversation_history.json,ui_messages.json}` + globalState key `taskHistory` | `~/.config/Code/...`; `%APPDATA%\Code\...`; same ext dir under Cursor/Windsurf/VSCodium/Antigravity/Kiro (Kiro [inferred]) |
| Old CLI 0.x (2025-10 to 2026-01) | config `~/.kilocode/cli/config.json`; tasks most likely `~/.vscode-mock/global-storage/tasks/` | same |
- Legacy sources [src: kilo-vscode/src/legacy-migration/sessions/migrate.ts#L37-L40; task-store.ts#L6-L7; Kilo-Org/kilocode-legacy@0068983:cli/src/config/persistence.ts#L18-L19; kilocode-legacy@ae046ac:src/shared/globalFileNames.ts]; old-CLI task location [inferred from its shared vscode-shim default; not verified]. No cass/SpecStory/agent-sessions coverage of Kilo (cass handles only Cline+Roo ext ids; SpecStory providers list has none) [3p: franken_agent_detection cline.rs#L23; specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a:specstory-cli/pkg/providers/].

### 3. Storage format
SQLite (drizzle) with WAL, 5 s busy timeout; canonical tables `session`, `message`, `part`, `todo`; v2 event-sourced tables `session_message`, `session_input`, `session_context_epoch` are experimental and disposable, v1 rows are canonical [src: packages/core/src/session/sql.ts#L24-L192; cli-runtime.md#L200-L213; specs/v2/schema-changelog.md#L15].
| Concept | Column / JSON path |
|---|---|
| session id | `session.id` (`ses_...`; `msg_...`, `prt_...` ids sort by time) |
| title | `session.title` |
| project/cwd | `session.directory` (+ `session.path`, `project_id`, `workspace_id`); assistant `message.data.path{cwd,root}` |
| created/updated | `session.time_created`, `time_updated`, `time_archived` (ms epoch); `message.time_created`; `message.data.time{created,completed}` |
| user text | `part.data` `{type:"text",text,synthetic?,ignored?}` joined via `part.message_id` to `message` with `data.role="user"`; skip `synthetic`/`ignored` |
| assistant text | same, `message.data.role="assistant"`; reasoning `{type:"reasoning",text}` |
| tool calls + results | ONE part: `{type:"tool",callID,tool,state:{status:"pending"\|"running"\|"completed"\|"error",input,output,title,metadata,time{start,end},attachments?,error?}}` |
| model/tokens | `session.model` JSON `{id,providerID,variant}`, `session.agent`, `cost`, `tokens_input/output/reasoning/cache_read/cache_write`; assistant `message.data{providerID,modelID,mode,agent,cost,tokens{input,output,reasoning,cache{read,write}},finish}` |
| other | `session.parent_id` (subagent/child sessions), `summary_*`, `permission`; part types `step-start`, `step-finish`, `patch`, `snapshot`, `file`, `agent`, `subtask`, `compaction`, `retry` |
- Field sources [src: packages/schema/src/v1/session.ts#L102-L127,L171-L180,L234-L357,L374-L415,L499-L531]. Official examples query `session`/`message`/`part` with `json_extract(data,'$.type')` [src: session-history.md#L164-L200].
- Format changes: (a) Roo-style JSON tasks (<=5.16.2) -> (b) rebuilt extension on the Kilo CLI backend (7.x line; legacy migration imports old tasks into the new store via `kilocode.sessionImport.*`) -> (c) OpenCode JSON `storage/` -> SQLite one-time migration [src: legacy-migration/sessions/migrate.ts#L79-L106; json-migration.ts]. After upgrade the same ext id still holds the old `tasks/` dir, so a machine can have all three layouts and duplicates.
- Incremental: watermark on `session.time_updated`; messages/parts by ascending id or `time_created`; parts mutate while streaming. WAL: `kilo.db-wal`/`-shm` exist while running, so open read-only WITHOUT `immutable=1` (immutable ignores the WAL) and never copy the .db alone [doc: session-history.md#L159; cli-runtime.md#L208]. Exclude child sessions (`parent_id`) or link them.

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Plain SQLite and JSON; no encryption. Credentials in `${data}/auth.json` (mode 0600) and `${data}/state` files - never read [doc: cli-runtime.md#L133,L188]. Official export/import: `kilo export [sessionID] [--sanitize]` (JSON to stdout), `kilo import <file|share URL>`, `kilo session list [--all --search --format json --max-count]`, `kilo db "<sql>" --format json` (accepts writes; use read-only) [doc: kilo-docs/pages/code-with-ai/platforms/cli-reference.md#L722-L747; session-history.md#L111-L135,L159-L177]. Cloud sessions also exist (`--cloud-fork`), not local.
- Native memory: opt-in per project Markdown under `~/.local/share/kilo/memory/<project-slug>-<sha1-12>/` (project.md, environment.md, corrections.md, sessions/) - do not collide [doc: kilo-docs/pages/customize/context/memory.md#L20-L33].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP:** global `~/.config/kilo/kilo.json` (also `kilo.jsonc`, `config.json`), project `./kilo.json` or `./.kilo/kilo.json` (project wins); key `mcp`; CLI `kilo mcp add|list|auth|logout|debug`. Legacy-shaped files are also converted in memory on every config load (lowest precedence): global `<globalStorage>/kilocode.kilo-code/settings/mcp_settings.json`, project `.kilocode/mcp.json` then `.kilo/mcp.json`, both `{"mcpServers":{...}}` [doc: kilo-docs/pages/automate/mcp/using-in-cli.md#L14-L113; cli-reference.md#L26-L120; src: packages/opencode/src/kilocode/mcp-migrator.ts#L88-L117; cli-runtime.md#L254-L256].
  `{"mcp":{"recall":{"type":"local","command":["recall","serve"],"enabled":true,"environment":{}}}}` (remote: `"type":"remote","url","headers"`; `timeout` ms, default 5000)
- **Global instructions:** `~/.config/kilo/AGENTS.md` (or `$KILO_CONFIG_DIR/AGENTS.md`), fallback `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`; project `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `CONTEXT.md` (first match upward); `instructions: [globs]` in kilo.jsonc; `.kilo/rules/`, legacy `.kilocode/rules/`; per-directory AGENTS.md is injected lazily as `<system-reminder>` when files in that dir are read [src: packages/opencode/src/session/instruction.ts#L62-L80,L152; kilo-docs/pages/customize/custom-rules.md#L36-L64; agents-md.md#L75,L148].
- **Skills:** yes; `~/.kilo/skills`, `<ws>/.kilo/skills`, `~/.agents/skills`, `.agents/skills`, `~/.claude/skills` + `.claude/skills` (Claude compat), config `skills.paths`/`skills.urls` [doc: kilo-docs/pages/customize/skills.md#L46-L103].
- **Hooks:** no declarative (settings.json-style) hooks found in docs or source; the only mechanism is TS/JS plugins (below) [src: tree + docs search of Kilo-Org/kilocode@0bdebc8; the Claude migration copies MCP+skills only: packages/opencode/src/kilocode/config/claude-migration.ts#L6-L59].
- **Plugins:** modules exporting `{id, server}` in `~/.config/kilo/plugin/`, `<ws>/.kilo/plugin/` (legacy `.kilocode/plugin/`), config `plugin: [...]`, `kilo plugin <pkg>`; run in CLI and VS Code ext. Hooks: `event` (e.g. `session.created`, `message.updated`), `config`, `tool`, `chat.message` (mutate `output.parts`), `chat.params`, `chat.headers`, `permission.ask`, `command.execute.before`, `tool.execute.before/after`, `shell.env`, `experimental.chat.system.transform` (push strings into `output.system` on every LLM call), `experimental.chat.messages.transform`, `experimental.session.compacting` (add `context[]`), `tool.definition` [src: packages/plugin/src/index.ts#L224-L337; packages/schema/src/v1/session.ts#L619-L645; kilo-docs/pages/automate/extending/plugins.md#L63-L120,L196-L211].
- **Slash commands:** `~/.config/kilo/commands/*.md`, `<ws>/.kilo/commands/*.md` (`/name`); legacy `.kilocode/workflows/` auto-migrated [doc: kilo-docs/pages/customize/workflows.md#L13-L40,L73-L75].
- **Headless:** `kilo run [message] --format json` (raw JSON events) `--auto` `-m provider/model` `--agent` `-s <session>` `-c` `--fork` `--dir` `--title` `-f <file>` `--attach <url>` `--variant`; `kilo serve` (HTTP+SSE, OpenAPI), `kilo daemon start`; auth via `KILO_SERVER_PASSWORD` [doc: cli-reference.md#L174-L205; cli-runtime.md#L39-L47,L116-L129].

**Best recall injection point:** MCP entry in `~/.config/kilo/kilo.json` (shared by CLI, VS Code and JetBrains) plus a plugin `~/.config/kilo/plugin/recall.ts` using `event` (`session.created`) and `experimental.chat.system.transform` to auto-load memory every turn; fallback is a managed `~/.config/kilo/AGENTS.md` block.
**Confidence / unknowns:** high (own docs + source, v7.8.3). Unknown: old CLI 0.x task location, whether v2 `session_message` rows are populated by default (docs treat v1 as canonical), JetBrains-specific paths (assumed same backend), exact date the rebuilt 7.x extension replaced 5.x for each user.
