## Continue (Continue Dev, Inc. / continuedev/continue) - repo read-only, final releases 2026-06


**Version verified:** continuedev/continue main@5522c6f (last commit 2026-07-21, docs only); README: "no longer actively maintained and is read-only", final 2.0.0 release (telemetry and auth removed); tags v2.1.0-vscode and v2.0.0-vscode (2026-06-19); CLI `@continuedev/cli` npm latest 1.5.47 (2026-06-18) [src: README.md#L19-L31; doc: https://github.com/continuedev/continue/releases; https://registry.npmjs.org/@continuedev/cli].

### 1. Detection
- VS Code ext `Continue.continue` (Marketplace + OpenVSX), JetBrains plugin `com.github.continuedev.continueintellijextension` (runs the same core binary), CLI `cn` (npm `@continuedev/cli`, bin `cn`) [src: README.md#L33-L45; extensions/vscode/package.json#L2-L24; extensions/intellij/src/main/kotlin/com/github/continuedev/continueintellijextension/constants/PluginConstants.kt#L9; npm registry bin map].
- Single global dir `~/.continue` (`%USERPROFILE%\.continue` on Windows) for all surfaces; env `CONTINUE_GLOBAL_DIR` (relative paths resolved against cwd) [src: core/util/paths.ts#L27-L36,L69-L76]. Because it is not under editor globalStorage, history is shared across VS Code, Cursor, Windsurf, JetBrains and the CLI [inferred from the path code; no per-editor dir exists].

### 2. History location
- `~/.continue/sessions/<sessionId>.json` + `~/.continue/sessions/sessions.json` (array index) on macOS/Linux/Windows [src: core/util/paths.ts#L78-L112]. CLI writes the same files through the shared `historyManager` (session ids are uuid v4; `CONTINUE_GLOBAL_DIR` honored) [src: extensions/cli/src/session.ts#L12-L13,L48-L67,L279-L296].
- Config: `~/.continue/config.yaml` (primary), legacy `config.json`; also `index/globalContext.json`, `sharedConfig.json`, `dev_data/` [src: core/util/paths.ts#L94-L138].

### 3. Storage format
Session file (key order written deliberately): `{sessionId, title, workspaceDirectory, history[], mode?, chatModelTitle?, usage?}`; index entry `{sessionId, title, dateCreated, workspaceDirectory, messageCount}` [src: core/util/history.ts#L111-L176; core/index.d.ts#L279-L298].
| Concept | Field |
|---|---|
| session id | `sessionId` (= file name) |
| title | `title` (index and file) |
| project/cwd | `workspaceDirectory`: VS Code stores `file:///...` URI strings, CLI stores raw `process.cwd()` - normalize both [src: extensions/vscode/src/ContinueGUIWebviewViewProvider.ts#L166-L170; gui/src/redux/thunks/session.ts#L253; extensions/cli/src/session.ts#L103] |
| created/updated | only `sessions.json[].dateCreated` (ms epoch as a string); NO per-message timestamps; updated = file mtime |
| user text | `history[].message` `{role:"user", content: string or text/image parts}`; attached context in `history[].contextItems[]` |
| assistant text | `message{role:"assistant", content, toolCalls?, usage?}`; reasoning as separate `role:"thinking"` messages |
| tool calls | `message.toolCalls[]` `{id,type:"function",function:{name,arguments(JSON string)}}`; `history[].toolCallStates[]{toolCallId,toolCall,status,parsedArgs,output[]}` (status generating/generated/calling/errored/done/canceled) |
| tool results | `message{role:"tool", toolCallId, content}`; `toolCallStates[].output` |
| model/tokens | `chatModelTitle`, `usage{totalCost,promptTokens,completionTokens,...}`, per-assistant `usage` |
- Field sources [src: core/index.d.ts#L335-L445,L497-L545].
- Format changes: `sessions.json` entries with snake_case `session_id` are the old format and are filtered out when listing; `config.json` -> `config.yaml`; Hub-based remote sessions removed [src: core/util/history.ts#L33-L38; extensions/cli/src/session.ts#L442-L444].
- Incremental: files are fully rewritten (`writeFileSync`, non-atomic) on every save; `sessions.json` is rewritten too (read-modify-write, race-prone) and has no updated-at, so use per-file mtime plus `messageCount` (count of assistant messages) as the cheap change signal [src: core/util/history.ts#L131-L181].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Plain JSON, no encryption; `config.yaml` is chmod 0600 because it can hold API keys - never read it [src: core/util/paths.ts#L17-L25,L119-L130]. No official export path found; `cn ls --json` lists sessions [src: extensions/cli/src/index.ts#L301-L303]. Optional local dev-data event logs under `.continue/dev_data` (not transcripts, not verified) [src: docs/customize/deep-dives/development-data.mdx#L8].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP:** IDE extensions load block files from `~/.continue/mcpServers/` and `<ws>/.continue/mcpServers/` (YAML with `name, version, schema: v1, mcpServers[]`, or Claude-Desktop-style JSON dropped in the same dir) and `mcpServers:` inside `config.yaml`; `type: stdio|sse|streamable-http`; CLI evidently reads only the loaded config's `mcpServers` (block-dir loading by `cn` not found) [src: core/config/yaml/loadYaml.ts#L57-L71,L369-L380; core/config/loadLocalAssistants.ts#L99-L131; docs/customize/deep-dives/mcp.mdx#L32-L68,L93-L100; extensions/cli/src/services/MCPService.ts#L73].
```yaml
# ~/.continue/mcpServers/recall.yaml
name: recall
version: 0.0.1
schema: v1
mcpServers:
  - name: recall
    type: stdio
    command: recall
    args: [serve]
```
- **Global instructions:** `~/.continue/rules/*.md` (frontmatter `name, globs, regex, description, alwaysApply`; no globs = always on), project `.continue/rules/`, root `AGENTS.md`/`AGENT.md`/`CLAUDE.md`, legacy `.continuerules`, `rules:` in config.yaml [src: core/config/markdown/loadMarkdownRules.ts#L10-L13; getWorkspaceContinueRuleDotFiles.ts#L4; docs/customize/deep-dives/rules.mdx#L57-L74; core/config/workspace/workspaceBlocks.ts#L127-L193].
- **Skills:** yes in the IDE core loader: SKILL.md (frontmatter `name`, `description`) under `~/.continue/skills`, `<ws>/.continue/skills`, `<ws>/.claude/skills`; CLI support not verified [src: core/config/markdown/loadMarkdownSkills.ts#L14-L63].
- **Hooks:** the CLI contains a Claude-Code-compatible engine (added 2026-03-04, abb1fc0): 17 events PreToolUse, PostToolUse, PostToolUseFailure, PermissionRequest, UserPromptSubmit, SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, Notification, SubagentStart, SubagentStop, PreCompact, ConfigChange, TeammateIdle, TaskCompleted, WorktreeCreate, WorktreeRemove; handlers command/http/prompt/agent; regex `matcher`; reads `~/.claude/settings.json`, `~/.continue/settings.json`, `.claude/settings(.local).json`, `.continue/settings(.local).json`; `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` for SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit/PostToolUse; exit 2 or `decision:"block"` blocks [src: extensions/cli/src/hooks/types.ts#L12-L30,L116-L129,L268-L348; hookConfig.ts#L1-L15,L73-L91]. BUT `fireSessionStart`, `fireUserPromptSubmit`, `firePreToolUse`, `firePostToolUse`, `fireStop`, `fireSessionEnd` are defined and never called anywhere in the repo (code search), and the introducing commit touched no chat-loop file, so hooks never fire [src: extensions/cli/src/hooks/fireHook.ts#L68-L231; gh code search of continuedev/continue; commit abb1fc0]. No hook engine found in core or the IDE extensions (tree search) [src: tree of continuedev/continue@5522c6f].
- **Plugins:** none (config blocks, context providers, MCP; Hub integration removed) [src: extensions/cli/src/session.ts#L442-L444].
- **Slash commands:** markdown prompts with `invokable: true` in `~/.continue/prompts/` and `<ws>/.continue/prompts/` (`/name` in IDE and CLI); CLI `--prompt <slug>` [src: docs/customize/deep-dives/prompts.mdx#L10-L24,L75-L97; core/config/markdown/loadMarkdownRules.ts#L12-L13].
- **Headless:** `cn -p "<prompt>"` (stdin piping ok), `--format json`, `--silent`, `--resume`, `--fork <sessionId>`, `--allow/--ask/--exclude <Tool>` (permission overrides), `--auto`/`--readonly`, `--config <file|slug>`; default config `~/.continue/config.yaml` [src: extensions/cli/src/index.ts#L183-L198; docs/cli/headless-mode.mdx#L5-L56; docs/cli/tool-permissions.mdx#L19-L26,L72-L73; docs/cli/configuration.mdx#L7-L33].

**Best recall injection point:** MCP via `~/.continue/mcpServers/recall.yaml` (IDE) and an `mcpServers:` entry in `~/.continue/config.yaml` (CLI), plus an always-on global rule `~/.continue/rules/recall.md`; hooks are unusable (dormant). Low priority since upstream is frozen.
**Confidence / unknowns:** high for storage and hooks status (own source); unknown: whether `cn` loads `.continue/mcpServers/` and skills, JetBrains-specific quirks (assumed same core), `dev_data` content, how long the frozen IDE builds keep working.

## Zed agent panel (Zed; zed-industries/zed)


**Version verified:** Zed v1.22.0 (GitHub release 2026-09-30) [doc: https://github.com/zed-industries/zed/releases/tag/v1.22.0]; source zed-industries/zed@2239fa7 (main, 2026-10-06); ACP spec agentclientprotocol/agent-client-protocol@92c0551.

### 1. Detection
- Bundle ids: Stable `dev.zed.Zed` (`Zed.app`), Preview `dev.zed.Zed-Preview`, Nightly `dev.zed.Zed-Nightly`, Dev `dev.zed.Zed-Dev` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/release_channel/src/lib.rs#L206-L235]. CLI binary `zed` (crates/cli) [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/cli/src/main.rs#L59,L71-L155].
- Data dir: macOS `~/Library/Application Support/Zed`; Linux `$XDG_DATA_HOME/zed` (default `~/.local/share/zed`; Flatpak `FLATPAK_XDG_DATA_HOME`); Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Zed`; shared by all release channels. Config dir: macOS `~/.config/zed` (not Application Support), Linux `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/zed`, Windows `%APPDATA%\Zed` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/paths/src/paths.rs#L49-L66,L121-L165].
- Relocation: `zed --user-data-dir DIR` makes data = DIR and config = DIR/config [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/paths/src/paths.rs#L103-L141; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/cli/src/main.rs#L96-L97]. `ZED_STATELESS=1` keeps threads in an in-memory DB, so nothing persists [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L431-L432; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/zed_env_vars/src/zed_env_vars.rs#L6]. ACP agent binaries live under `<data_dir>/external_agents` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/paths/src/paths.rs#L454-L460].

### 2. History location
- Zed Agent (native) threads: `<data_dir>/threads/threads.db` (macOS `~/Library/Application Support/Zed/threads/threads.db`) [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L445-L447].
- Thread metadata for all thread kinds (native, External Agents, Terminal Threads; `archived` flag): table `sidebar_threads` in `<data_dir>/db/0-<channel>/db.sqlite` (channel stable/preview/nightly/dev; WAL) holding `session_id`, `agent_id`, `title`, `updated_at`, `created_at`, `folder_paths`, `archived`, `remote_connection`, no message content [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent_ui/src/thread_metadata_store.rs#L311-L325,L1370-L1385; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/db/src/db.rs#L126-L138,L164-L167].
- External Agents (ACP: Claude, Codex, Gemini CLI, OpenCode, Copilot, Cursor, Pi, ...) own their transcripts; Zed only hosts the thread UI, calling ACP `session/list` and `session/load` (agent replays history) and "Import Threads" adds metadata rows [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/external-agents.md; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent_servers/src/acp.rs#L439-L446,L1458-L1463] [doc: https://github.com/agentclientprotocol/agent-client-protocol/blob/92c0551/docs/protocol/v1/session-list.mdx]. So Claude via ACP is read from Claude's own `~/.claude/projects/<cwd>/*.jsonl` [inferred: adapter wraps the Claude Agent SDK, which persists there per https://code.claude.com/docs/en/agent-sdk/sessions].
- Text threads (`conversations/*.zed`) have been removed from Zed; leftover files on old installs not verified [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/text-threads.md]. Legacy Rules Library: LMDB `prompts/prompts-library-db.0.mdb` (macOS under `~/.config/zed/prompts`, else `<data_dir>/prompts`), superseded by Skills/Instructions [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/prompt_store/src/prompt_store.rs#L28; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/paths/src/paths.rs#L386-L395].

### 3. Storage format (Zed Agent)
`threads(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, summary TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL, data_type TEXT NOT NULL, data BLOB NOT NULL)` plus columns added by ALTER: `parent_id`, `folder_paths`, `folder_paths_order`, `created_at` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L451-L486]. `data_type='zstd'`: `data` = zstd(level 3) of JSON `{...DbThread, "version":"0.3.0"}`; `'json'` = uncompressed (older rows) [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L190,L504-L538].

| Concept | Field |
|---|---|
| session id | `threads.id` (ACP SessionId string); sub-agent rows set `parent_id` = `subagent_context.parent_thread_id` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L515-L518] |
| title | `threads.summary` = JSON `title` |
| project/cwd | `threads.folder_paths` (+ `folder_paths_order`; serialized PathList, may be NULL); JSON `initial_project_snapshot` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L38,L62] |
| created/updated | `threads.created_at`, `threads.updated_at` (RFC3339 text); JSON `updated_at` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L514,L540-L543] |
| user text | `messages[]` item `{"User":{"id","content":[{"Text":str}\|{"Mention":{"uri","content"}}\|{"Image":..}]}}` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/thread.rs#L288-L302] |
| assistant text | `{"Agent":{"content":[{"Text":str}\|{"Thinking":{"text","signature"}}\|{"RedactedThinking":str}\|{"ToolUse":..}],"tool_results":{..},"reasoning_details"}}` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/thread.rs#L744-L760] |
| tool calls | `Agent.content[].ToolUse` = `{id, name, raw_input, input, is_input_complete, thought_signature}` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/language_model_core/src/language_model_core.rs#L593-L602] |
| tool results | `Agent.tool_results` map keyed by tool_use_id: `{tool_use_id, tool_name, is_error, content:[{"Text"}\|{"Image"}], output}` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/language_model_core/src/request.rs#L68-L76,L149-L152] |
| other | `model`, `profile`, `cumulative_token_usage`, `request_token_usage`, `detailed_summary`, `speed`, `thinking_*`, `draft_prompt`, `sandbox_grants`; message variants `"Resume"`, `{"Compaction":{"Summary"\|"ProviderNative"}}` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L54-L90; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/thread.rs#L203-L218] |

- Format changes: JSON `version` "0.3.0"; older agent-1 JSON (no matching version) is upgraded on load via `upgrade_from_agent_1`; columns added incrementally; text threads removed [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L196-L211; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/legacy_thread.rs].
- Incremental: each save upserts the whole row (full blob rewrite, `ON CONFLICT(id)`), never append-only; detect change by `updated_at` (+ length(data)); one row per sub-agent thread. WAL/journal mode of threads.db is not set in db.rs (unknown); read an immutable copy. [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L545-L570]

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Not encrypted: zstd+JSON, no key [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L536]; provider API keys live in the OS keychain, not the DB [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/external-agents.md] [inferred: no secret fields exist in DbThread].
- Export: right-click a response in the Agent Panel > "Open Thread as Markdown" (`DbThread::to_markdown`); no CLI or bulk export [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/agent-panel.md#L86; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent/src/db.rs#L192-L194].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP**: `context_servers` in `~/.config/zed/settings.json` (macOS/Linux default; Windows `%APPDATA%\Zed\settings.json`; syntax is JSON with `//` comments, so edit with a comment-preserving writer) and in project `.zed/settings.json` (`ProjectSettingsContent.context_servers` exists in source, while the docs call project settings limited; verify). Forwarded to External Agents over ACP. No `zed mcp add` CLI [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/mcp.md; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/configuring-zed.md#L37; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/settings_content/src/project.rs#L44-L70,L506-L557].
  ```json
  {"context_servers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{}}}}
  ```
  (remote form `{"url":"https://...","headers":{}}`). Extension route: `[context_servers.<id>]` in extension.toml + `context_server_command`; slated for deprecation in favour of the MCP registry [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/extensions/mcp-extensions.md].
- **Global instructions**: personal `~/.config/zed/AGENTS.md` (Windows `%APPDATA%\Zed\AGENTS.md`), Zed Agent only; project: first match of `.rules`, `.cursorrules`, `.windsurfrules`, `.clinerules`, `.github/copilot-instructions.md`, `AGENT.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `GEMINI.md`. Rules replaced by Skills and Instructions; External Agents read their own files [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/instructions.md; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/paths/src/paths.rs#L323-L325].
- **Skills**: yes, Zed Agent only: global `~/.agents/skills/<name>/SKILL.md`, project `<worktree>/.agents/skills/` (trusted worktrees only, flat layout), frontmatter `name`, `description`, `disable-model-invocation`; SKILL.md at most 100 KB, catalog at most 50 KB; live reload; `/name` or `@skill`; the `skill` tool asks permission unless allowed in tool permissions [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/skills.md; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/agent_skills/agent_skills.rs#L13-L53].
- **Hooks**: none for agents (no session-start or prompt-submit event; nothing injects context). The only hook is the Task hook `create_worktree` in tasks.json [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/tasks.md#L233-L241].
- **Plugins/extensions**: WASM extensions (languages, themes, MCP, debuggers, snippets); slash-command extensions removed; agent-server extensions deprecated in favour of the ACP Registry; custom ACP agents via `{"agent_servers":{"name":{"type":"custom","command":"..","args":[".."],"env":{}}}}` [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/extensions/slash-commands.md; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/external-agents.md].
- **Slash commands**: built-ins (`/compact`, `/create-skill`) and skills only; no custom command dir [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/skills.md; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/agent-panel.md].
- **Headless**: none; `zed` is an editor launcher (`--wait`, `--new`, `--add`, `--diff`, `--user-data-dir`, `--foreground`). Terminal Threads can host any agent CLI that has its own config [src: zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:crates/cli/src/main.rs#L71-L155; zed-industries/zed@2239fa7:docs/src/ai/terminal-threads.md].

**Best recall injection point:** no hooks, so register MCP under `context_servers` in `~/.config/zed/settings.json` (also forwarded to ACP agents), put a static memory digest in `~/.config/zed/AGENTS.md` (always-on, Zed Agent only) and add a skill at `~/.agents/skills/recall/`. For External Agents (Claude, Codex, ...) use that agent's own hooks instead.
**Confidence / unknowns:** storage and settings paths are source-pinned (high); not installed locally, threads.db journal mode, legacy `conversations/*.zed` leftovers and live ACP-agent behaviour are unverified.

---

## Goose (aaif-goose/goose, formerly block/goose)


Goose reads `~/.agents/skills` as a skill directory [observed].
**Version verified:** v1.53.0 released 2026-10-02; `main` @540df77 (2026-10-06); session schema version 16 [src: aaif-goose/goose@540df77:ui/desktop/package.json#L4; crates/goose/src/session/session_manager.rs#L28]

### 1. Detection
- Canonical repo is `aaif-goose/goose` (Apache-2.0); `github.com/block/goose` 301-redirects; "part of the Agentic AI Foundation (AAIF) at the Linux Foundation" [src: aaif-goose/goose@540df77:README.md#L29]. Docs: https://goose-docs.ai.
- CLI `goose`: installer `download_cli.sh` puts it in `$GOOSE_BIN_DIR` (default `~/.local/bin`) [src: aaif-goose/goose@540df77:download_cli.sh#L17,L66-L69]; Homebrew formula `block-goose-cli`. CAUTION: Homebrew core formula `goose` is an unrelated DB-migration tool, so verify with `goose --version` / `goose info` [3p: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/formula/goose.json].
- Desktop: Electron `Goose.app` (`productName: Goose`) [src: aaif-goose/goose@540df77:ui/desktop/package.json#L2-L4]; Homebrew cask `block-goose` installs `/Applications/Goose.app` [3p: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/block-goose.json]. CFBundleIdentifier is not set in forge config; the repo's own script lists `com.electron.goose` and `com.block.goose` as candidates [src: ui/desktop/scripts/unregister-deeplink-protocols.js#L50-L53 (@540df77)]. Electron state and `logs/main.log` live in `~/Library/Application Support/Goose/` [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/logs].
- Dirs (no override): etcetera `choose_app_strategy` = XDG on macOS/Linux, Windows strategy on Windows: config `~/.config/goose`, data `~/.local/share/goose`, state `~/.local/state/goose` (honours `XDG_*_HOME`); Windows `%APPDATA%\Block\goose\{config,data}` [src: aaif-goose/goose@540df77:crates/goose/src/config/paths.rs#L8-L38; 3p: lunacookies/etcetera src/lib.rs#L57 (default app strategy is XDG on Linux and macOS); doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/logs].
- Env: `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT` (must be absolute) relocates everything to `<root>/{config,data,state}` and `<root>/.agents/{plugins,agents}` [src: crates/goose/src/config/paths.rs#L8-L46 (@540df77)]. Docs conflict: the env-var page says the macOS default is `~/Library/Application Support/Block/goose/`, which the source and the logs page contradict (that is the Windows-style path) [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/environment-variables vs .../logs]. Others: `CONTEXT_FILE_NAMES`, `GOOSE_MOIM_MESSAGE_FILE`, `GOOSE_DISABLE_SESSION_NAMING`.

### 2. History location
- `~/.local/share/goose/sessions/sessions.db` (+ `-wal`/`-shm`) on macOS and Linux; `%APPDATA%\Block\goose\data\sessions\sessions.db` on Windows; CLI and Desktop share it; with `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT`: `<root>/data/sessions/sessions.db` [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/logs; src: aaif-goose/goose@540df77:crates/goose/src/session/session_manager.rs#L29-L30,L960-L967].
- Legacy (before v1.10.0): `sessions/<name>.jsonl`, auto-imported into the DB on first run, files left in place [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/logs; src: crates/goose/src/session/legacy.rs#L1-L40 (@540df77)]. cass says SQLite began at "v1.20+" and also probes `~/.goose/sessions` [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/goose.rs#L1-L8]; trust the vendor's v1.10.0.
- Related: CLI prompt history `~/.config/goose/history.txt` (user inputs only); logs `~/.local/state/goose/logs/` [doc: logs page].

### 3. Storage format
- SQLite via sqlx; WAL, `busy_timeout` 30 s, foreign keys on, dir mode 0700 [src: aaif-goose/goose@540df77:crates/goose/src/session/session_manager.rs#L940-L956]. `schema_version` table, version 16, migrations applied on open (a write) [src: session_manager.rs#L1263-L1275].
- Tables: `sessions` [src: #L1028-L1062]; `messages(id INTEGER PK AUTOINCREMENT, message_id, session_id FK, role, content_json, created_timestamp INTEGER, timestamp, tokens, metadata_json)` [src: #L1066-L1079]; `usage_ledger` (per-request model/tokens/cost) [src: #L1084-L1099]; `threads` + `thread_messages` are created by migration 10 but nothing at this SHA writes them [src: #L1436-L1495]. All @540df77.

| Concept | Column / path |
|---|---|
| session id | `sessions.id`, `YYYYMMDD_<n>` (UTC date + per-day counter), e.g. `20260217_5` [src: session_manager.rs#L1620-L1650; doc: environment-variables AGENT_SESSION_ID] |
| title | `sessions.name` (AI-named unless `user_set_name`; defaults "New Chat"/"CLI Session"); `description` kept, old docs query it [src: #L1028-L1062; doc: session-management] |
| project/cwd | `sessions.working_dir`; optional `project_id` |
| created / updated | `sessions.created_at` / `updated_at` (SQLite UTC text, `datetime('now')` on every message insert) [src: #L1958]; per message `messages.created_timestamp` = Unix seconds (values above 1e10 are treated as ms) [src: #L31,L737-L741] |
| user text | `messages` row `role='user'`; in `content_json` (JSON array) element `{"type":"text","text":...}` |
| assistant text | `role='assistant'`; elements `text`, plus `thinking` {thinking, signature} and `redactedThinking` [src: crates/goose-provider-types/src/conversation/message.rs#L317-L329] |
| tool calls | element `toolRequest` {id, toolCall:{status:"success", value:{name, arguments}}}; names are `<ext>__<tool>` or bare (`shell`, `edit`, `write`) [src: message.rs#L132-L140; tool_result_serde.rs#L1-L30; doc: hooks "Tool Input Keys"] |
| tool results | element `toolResponse` {id (= toolRequest.id), toolResult:{status:"success"/"error", value:{content[], isError}}} [src: message.rs#L176-L184] |
| other | `messages.metadata_json` {userVisible, agentVisible, usage, inference} (skip `userVisible=false`) [src: message.rs#L826-L856; session_manager.rs#L743-L745]; `sessions.provider_name`, `model_config_json`, `goose_mode`, token columns, `accumulated_cost`; `session_type` in user/scheduled/sub_agent/hidden/terminal/gateway/acp [src: #L48-L56] |

- Format changes across versions: JSONL to SQLite at v1.10.0 [doc: logs page]; `description` renamed `name` (serde alias) [src: session_manager.rs#L62-L68]; schema 1 to 16 adds columns by `ALTER TABLE` (name, session_type, provider/model, goose_mode, archived_at, project_id, parent_session_id, token columns) [src: #L1325-L1610]. Goose itself imports Claude Code, Codex and Pi JSONL [src: crates/goose/src/session/import_formats/mod.rs#L1-L13].
- Incremental-import notes: NOT append-only; editing/forking deletes later messages (`DELETE FROM messages WHERE session_id=? AND created_timestamp >= ?`) and compaction rewrites [src: session_manager.rs#L2622-L2650]. Detect changes per session via `sessions.updated_at` plus `max(messages.id)`/count; re-import a session when either moves. Open with `sqlite3 -readonly "file:<db>?mode=ro"`; do NOT use `immutable=1` on a live DB (ignores the WAL, drops recent turns); or copy db+wal+shm together. Never let the importer open it read-write (Goose migrates on open). Default UI set is `session_type IN ('user','scheduled')`; `archived_at` marks archived [src: session_manager.rs#L2284].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
Plaintext local SQLite, never uploaded [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/logs]. Export: `goose session export [-n NAME / --session-id ID] --format json/yaml/markdown -o FILE` (JSON = serialized `Session` incl. `conversation`); `goose session list --format json [-w DIR] [--limit N]` [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/goose-cli-commands; src: crates/goose/src/session/import_formats/mod.rs#L1-L6]. API keys live in the keyring or `~/.config/goose/secrets.yaml` (never read) [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/config-files]. Goose already ships a "Chatrecall" extension that searches past sessions [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/sessions/session-management].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- MCP ("extensions"): `~/.config/goose/config.yaml` (`<root>/config/` with `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT`), `extensions:` map; types `stdio`, `streamable_http`, `builtin`, `platform` (SSE dropped); no project-scope file. No `goose mcp add`; use YAML edit, `goose configure`, per-session `--with-extension "<cmd>"` / `--with-streamable-http-extension`, or a `goose://extension?cmd=...&arg=...` deeplink; restart needed after edits [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/config-files; https://goose-docs.ai/docs/getting-started/using-extensions; src: aaif-goose/goose@540df77:crates/goose/src/agents/extension.rs#L155-L220; crates/goose/src/config/extensions.rs#L13].
  ```yaml
  extensions:
    recall:                # placeholder command
      type: stdio
      name: recall
      enabled: true
      cmd: recall
      args: ["serve"]
      envs: {}
      timeout: 300
  ```
  MCP server `instructions` are placed in the system prompt [src: crates/goose/src/agents/prompt_manager.rs#L109-L127 (@540df77)].
- Global instructions: `~/.config/goose/.goosehints` (global hints) and `~/.config/goose/AGENTS.md`; project `.goosehints` and `AGENTS.md` from cwd up to the git root, nested ones loaded when files there are touched; `CONTEXT_FILE_NAMES` (JSON array, default `[".goosehints","AGENTS.md"]`); `@file` includes; needs the `developer` extension on [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/context-engineering/using-goosehints; .../environment-variables]. Per-turn injection: `GOOSE_MOIM_MESSAGE_FILE` / `GOOSE_MOIM_MESSAGE_TEXT` env (re-read every turn, 64 KB cap) [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/context-engineering/using-persistent-instructions].
- Skills: yes (Agent Skills): `~/.agents/skills/<name>/SKILL.md`, `<project>/.agents/skills/`, plugin-supplied; compat `.goose/skills`, `.claude/skills`, `~/.claude/skills`; `goose skills list`, `/skills` in CLI [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/context-engineering/using-skills].
- Hooks: Open Plugins style. File `hooks/hooks.json` inside a plugin dir `~/.agents/plugins/<plugin>/` (user) or `<project>/.agents/plugins/<plugin>/` (project) plus `plugin.json`; `goose plugin install <git-url>`; new plugins auto-enable and are recorded in `config.yaml` `plugins:` [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/context-engineering/hooks; src: crates/goose/src/plugins/discovery.rs#L9-L16,L120-L151]. Events: `SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PreToolUseResult, PostToolUse, PostToolUseFailure, BeforeReadFile, AfterFileEdit, BeforeShellExecution, AfterShellExecution` [src: crates/goose/src/hooks/mod.rs#L55-L67]. Rule = `{matcher (unanchored regex), hooks:[{type:"command", command, timeout (30 s), on_failure}]}`, run with `sh -c`; stdin JSON `event, session_id, matcher_context, tool_name, tool_input, message (UserPromptSubmit), last_assistant_message (Stop), working_dir, tool_call_id`. NO context injection: decisions are Allow/Deny only, only `PreToolUse` and `Stop` can block (exit 2 or stdout `{"decision":"block"}`); `SessionStart` stdout `{"banner":"..."}` is printed to the user by the interactive CLI, not sent to the model [src: hooks/mod.rs#L348-L352,L618-L690,L892-L904; crates/goose-cli/src/session/mod.rs#L561-L568 (all @540df77)]. Useful for triggering imports (`SessionEnd`/`Stop`), not memory loading.
- Plugins: `plugin.json` + `skills/` + `hooks/` (Open Plugins) via `goose plugin install|update` [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/context-engineering/plugins]. Slash commands: recipes bound by `slash_commands: [{command, recipe_path}]` in config.yaml [doc: .../slash-commands]; custom agents `~/.agents/agents/*.md` [doc: .../custom-agents].
- Headless: `goose run -t "<text>"` / `-i <file>` / `--recipe <file>` (`--params k=v`, `--no-session`, `-q`, `--max-turns`, `--with-extension`, `--provider/--model`), `--output-format text/json/stream-json`; `goose acp` (ACP over stdio) [doc: https://goose-docs.ai/docs/guides/goose-cli-commands; src: aaif-goose/goose@540df77:crates/goose-cli/src/cli.rs#L235-L395,L826-L895].

**Best recall injection point:** MCP via a `extensions:` entry in `~/.config/goose/config.yaml`; memory auto-load via global `~/.config/goose/AGENTS.md` / `.goosehints` (system prompt, loaded at session start) and/or `GOOSE_MOIM_MESSAGE_FILE` (every turn); one skill in `~/.agents/skills/recall/` also reaches Amp and Droid. Hooks cannot inject.
**Confidence / unknowns:** High (docs plus source at 540df77). Unknown: Desktop CFBundleIdentifier; whether `threads`/`thread_messages` become the Desktop store later.

---

## Warp (Warp terminal: Warp Agent / Oz; warpdotdev/warp)


The database lives under `~/Library/Group Containers/2BBY89MBSN.dev.warp`; on macOS, listing it can fail with EPERM unless the reading process has Full Disk Access (TCC), so it was not read [observed]. `~/.warp/settings.toml` is TOML; its names include `[privacy]` crash_reporting_enabled, telemetry_enabled and custom_secret_regex_list, `[agents]` cloud_conversation_storage_enabled, and `[agents.warp_agent.input]` nld_in_terminal_enabled [observed].
**Version verified:** Warp Stable 0.2026.09.30.08.29.stable_01 [doc: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/warp.json]; client source warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3 (2026-10-06, AGPL-3.0, open source) [doc: https://github.com/warpdotdev/warp]; docs read from warpdotdev/docs@87a03ca (served at https://docs.warp.dev); proto repo warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis@5a34987.

### 1. Detection
- App: `/Applications/Warp.app` (bundle id `dev.warp.Warp-Stable`), `/Applications/WarpPreview.app` (`dev.warp.Warp-Preview`) [doc: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/warp.json; https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/warp@preview.json]; channels Stable/Preview/Dev/Oss/Local/Integration [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/warp_core/src/paths.rs#L35-L45].
- Two CLIs. `warp` = Warp Agent CLI, a standalone TUI of the same agent (`curl -fsSL https://app.warp.dev/download/agent-cli | bash` or `brew install --cask warp-agent-cli`) [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli/quickstart/]. `oz` / `oz-preview` = older agent and cloud CLI (formerly `warp-cli`; bundled with the app or `brew install --cask oz`), now called a supported legacy interface [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli/oz-cli/].
- Config (macOS): `~/.warp/` for Stable (settings.toml, keybindings.yaml, themes, workflows); Preview `~/.warp-preview/`. Home carve-out shared by both: `~/.warp/.mcp.json`, `~/.warp/skills/`, plus `~/.agents/` [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/terminal/settings/file-locations/] [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/warp_core/src/paths.rs#L66-L76,L99-L113].
- State dir (SQLite, codebase index, MCP logs): macOS `~/Library/Group Containers/2BBY89MBSN.dev.warp/Library/Application Support/dev.warp.Warp-Stable/` (Preview: `.../dev.warp.Warp-Preview/`), falling back to `~/Library/Application Support/<bundle id>/` when the container is unusable [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/warp_core/src/paths.rs#L260-L295,L400-L425]. Linux `${XDG_STATE_HOME:-~/.local/state}/warp-terminal[-preview]/`; Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%\warp\Warp[Preview]\data\` [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/terminal/settings/file-locations/] [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/warp_core/src/paths.rs#L364-L389].
- `warp` TUI has separate config (`~/.warp_cli/`; Linux `~/.config/warp-terminal/cli/`; Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%\warp\Warp\config\cli\`) and a separate DB at `<state dir>/tui/warp.sqlite` [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli/configuration/] [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/warp_core/src/paths.rs#L226-L245,L306-L308].
- Env: no data-relocation variable in release builds (`WARP_DATA_PROFILE` only when `debug_assertions`) [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/warp_core/src/channel/state.rs#L136-L147]; Linux honours XDG_*; also `WARP_API_KEY`, `WARP_TUI_DISABLE_AUTOUPDATE`, `WARP_SKILL_DIRS` (cloud runs) [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli/reference/].

### 2. History location
- Local (default): `warp.sqlite` (+ `-wal`, `-shm`) in the state dir above, so inside the TCC-protected group container on macOS [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:app/src/persistence/sqlite.rs#L126,L473-L481]. The docs' uninstall page also lists this container path as "database" [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/support-and-community/troubleshooting-and-support/logging-out-and-uninstalling/].
- Cloud: conversations are local by default; opt-in "Settings > Privacy > Store AI conversations in the cloud" syncs snapshots to the Warp account (settings.toml key `agents.cloud_conversation_storage_enabled` exists here [observed]; mapping setting-to-key is [inferred] from the name); cloud-agent runs are always cloud; with sync off, data is lost at logout [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/local-agents/cloud-conversations/]. The `warp` TUI keeps conversation state in its own DB and says conversations sync to the account [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:app/src/persistence/mod.rs#L106-L117] [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli/agent-conversations/].
- Third-party CLI agents run inside Warp (Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Gemini CLI, ...) keep their own history; Warp only detects them and adds toolbelt/notifications [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli-agents/overview/]; schema.rs has no table for their transcripts [inferred from the table list in crates/persistence/src/schema.rs]. Whether `oz agent run` writes the app DB: unknown (launch mode `CommandLine` appears to share `PersistenceScope::App` [inferred from the doc comment at persistence/mod.rs#L71]).

### 3. Storage format
SQLite via diesel, `PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL`, `wal_autocheckpoint=500`, `busy_timeout=1000` [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:app/src/persistence/sqlite.rs#L218-L235]. Chat = `agent_conversations` (JSON metadata) + `agent_tasks` (one prost-encoded `warp.multi_agent.v1.Task` per row) [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/src/schema.rs#L10-L28; warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:app/src/persistence/agent.rs#L57-L98]. The .proto is public (edition 2023) [src: warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis@5a34987:apis/multi_agent/v1/task.proto].

| Concept | Field / column / path |
|---|---|
| session id | `agent_conversations.conversation_id` (UNIQUE); tasks keyed `agent_tasks.task_id` + `conversation_id`; server id in `conversation_data` JSON `server_conversation_token` [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/src/model.rs#L1172-L1173] |
| title | `agent_conversations.summary` JSON `title` (root task description, fallback `initial_query`) [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/src/model.rs#L1047-L1073] |
| project/cwd | summary `initial_working_directory`; per message `user_query.context.directory.pwd` [src: warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis@5a34987:apis/multi_agent/v1/input_context.proto#L41-L43]; `ai_queries.working_directory` is prompt-history only [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/src/schema.rs#L48-L61] |
| created/updated | `agent_conversations.last_modified_at`, `agent_tasks.last_modified_at` (trigger-maintained; migration 2025-06-09); per message `Message.timestamp` [src: warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis@5a34987:apis/multi_agent/v1/task.proto#L299-L303] |
| user text | `Task.messages[].user_query.query` (system prompts: `system_query`) [src: warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis@5a34987:apis/multi_agent/v1/task.proto#L421-L423,L461-L477] |
| assistant text | `Message.agent_output.text`; reasoning `agent_reasoning.reasoning` [src: warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis@5a34987:apis/multi_agent/v1/task.proto#L527-L544] |
| tool calls | `Message.tool_call{tool_call_id, oneof run_shell_command.command, read_files, apply_file_diffs, grep, call_mcp_tool, ...}` [src: warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis@5a34987:apis/multi_agent/v1/task.proto#L572-L617] |
| tool results | `Message.tool_call_result{tool_call_id, oneof result}` [src: warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis@5a34987:apis/multi_agent/v1/task.proto#L1217-L1262] |
| other | `Message.model_used`, `request_metadata` (timing, charges, tokens), `Task.dependencies.parent_task_id`; JSON `parent_conversation_id`, `agent_name`, `run_id` [src: warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis@5a34987:apis/multi_agent/v1/task.proto#L27-L58,L317-L339; warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/src/model.rs#L1184-L1218] |

Not chat: `blocks` (terminal blocks; `stylized_command`/`stylized_output` BLOBs, `pwd`, `exit_code`, `start_ts`, `ai_metadata`) and `commands` (shell history incl. `is_agent_executed`) [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/src/schema.rs#L79-L107,L142-L159].
- Format changes: 2024-08 `ai_exchanges` (input/output TEXT) split into `ai_queries` + `ai_blocks` (2024-08-20); 2025-06-09 `agent_conversations` + `agent_tasks` (protobuf); `ai_blocks` dropped 2025-11-19; `summary` column added 2026-07-07 [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/migrations/2024-08-20-201322_split_ai_exchanges_table; warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/migrations/2025-06-09-013710_create_agent_conversations_table; warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/migrations/2025-11-19-224140_remove_persisted_ai_blocks; warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/persistence/migrations/2026-07-07-000000_add_summary_to_agent_conversations].
- Incremental: not append-only; every write replaces the conversation's full task snapshot and deletes tasks missing from it (rewind) [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:app/src/persistence/agent.rs#L59-L64,L110-L119]. Diff on `agent_conversations.last_modified_at`, decode `agent_tasks.task` only for changed ids; `summary` lists sessions without decoding blobs. Only the newest 200 conversation rows are kept (tree-aware eviction), so import often [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:app/src/persistence/agent.rs#L41-L45]. Read a consistent copy of db + -wal + -shm rather than the live files [inferred from WAL mode].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- No encryption in the schema or pragmas (plain SQLite + plain protobuf) [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:app/src/persistence/sqlite.rs#L218-L235] [inferred: absence of SQLCipher]; the only barrier on macOS is the TCC-protected App Group container [observed]. A remote-daemon scope forces owner-only perms; the GUI scope does not [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:app/src/persistence/sqlite.rs#L376-L378].
- Official export: `/export-to-file [name]` and `/export-to-clipboard` (Markdown) in app and TUI [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/capabilities/slash-commands/]. Cloud: `oz run conversation get <CONVERSATION_ID>`; REST base `https://app.warp.dev/api/v1` (`GET /agent/runs`, `GET /agent/runs/{runId}`, `GET /agent/conversations/{id}/redirect`); API key via `WARP_API_KEY` [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli/oz-cli/] [src: warpdotdev/oz-sdk-python@ba8310c:api.md]. Share links/web view for cloud-synced conversations [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/local-agents/cloud-conversations/].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP** (UI-managed or file-based): global `~/.warp/.mcp.json` (auto-spawns by default), project `.warp/.mcp.json` (never auto-spawns; enable per session). Also reads `~/.claude.json` + `.mcp.json`, `~/.codex/config.toml` + `.codex/config.toml`, `~/.agents/.mcp.json` + `.agents/.mcp.json`; third-party global servers spawn only if "Auto-spawn servers from third-party agents" is on; agent or automation edits to MCP config files require explicit approval [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/capabilities/mcp/]. Keys: `command`, `args`, `env`, `working_directory`; remote `url`, `headers`. No `warp mcp add`; `oz agent run --mcp <uuid|file|json>`; the TUI reads only `~/.warp_cli/.mcp.json` (no project files) [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli/configuration/].
  ```json
  {"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{},"working_directory":"~"}}}
  ```
- **Global instructions**: file-based global rule is only `~/.agents/AGENTS.md` (watched live); GUI "Global Rules" are Warp Drive objects (cloud-synced) [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/ai/src/project_context/global_rules.rs#L17-L45] [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/capabilities/rules/]. Project: `AGENTS.md` (or `WARP.md`, which wins) at repo root and cwd; `/init` can link CLAUDE.md, .cursorrules, AGENT.md, GEMINI.md, .clinerules, .windsurfrules, copilot-instructions.md [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/capabilities/rules/].
- **Skills**: yes (SKILL.md, `$ARGUMENTS`/`$N`; invoked as `/skill-name`). Global and project roots (cwd up to repo root): `.agents/skills` (recommended), `.warp/skills`, `.claude/skills`, `.codex/skills`, `.cursor/skills`, `.gemini/skills`, `.copilot/skills`, `.factory/skills`, `.github/skills`, `.opencode/skills`, and (source only) `.kiro/skills` [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/capabilities/skills/] [src: warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3:crates/ai/src/skills/skill_provider.rs#L105-L154].
- **Hooks**: none for the Warp Agent; no session-start or prompt-submit event, nothing injects context (only internal shell-integration hooks exist) [inferred: no hooks page in warpdotdev/docs@87a03ca and no hook handler in warpdotdev/warp@9ccb4e3 other than crates/warp_terminal/src/model/ansi/dcs_hooks.rs]. For third-party agents run inside Warp, notifications come through the agent's own plugin: Claude Code `warp@claude-code-warp` (`claude plugin marketplace add warpdotdev/claude-code-warp`), Codex via a config change, OpenCode via plugin; these only signal status [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli-agents/claude-code/]. A shipped example of a Claude Code plugin with start/stop/end hook scripts: [src: warpdotdev/claude-code-warp@f8c6287:plugins/oz-harness-support/scripts].
- **Plugins/extensions**: none for the Warp Agent beyond MCP, skills, rules, Warp Drive prompts/workflows. Warp Agent Memory is server-side (research preview) [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/agent-memory/]. SDKs: oz-sdk-python/typescript.
- **Slash commands**: built-ins (list in slash-commands page) + skills; saved Warp Drive prompts via `/prompts`; no user command dir [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/capabilities/slash-commands/].
- **Headless**: `oz agent run --prompt "..." [-C dir] [--mcp ..] [--skill ..] [--profile ..] [--model ..] [-f cfg.yaml]` (local) and `oz agent run-cloud`; `--output-format json|text` is documented for `oz whoami` only. The `warp` TUI has no one-shot prompt flag (flags: `--api-key`, `--auto-approve`, `--resume <token>`, `--set/--clear-provider-api-key`) [doc: https://docs.warp.dev/agents/cli/reference/].

**Best recall injection point:** no hook exists, so use `~/.agents/AGENTS.md` (global rule, picked up by app and TUI) plus a skill at `~/.agents/skills/recall/SKILL.md`; register MCP in `~/.warp/.mcp.json` (global Warp servers auto-spawn; `~/.agents/.mcp.json` and `~/.claude.json` need the third-party auto-spawn toggle).
**Confidence / unknowns:** schema, paths, protobuf and MCP/skill dirs are source/doc-pinned (high); unread locally (TCC), so no real-row verification, and `oz agent run` persistence plus the exact Markdown export shape are unknown.

---

## Kiro (AWS Kiro IDE + Kiro CLI, formerly Amazon Q Developer CLI; kiro.dev)


**Version verified:** Kiro IDE 1.2.37 and Kiro CLI 2.27.1 [doc: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/kiro.json; https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/kiro-cli.json]; docs read via https://kiro.dev/llms.txt `.md` twins and CLI changelog (2026-10-06). Kiro IDE/CLI have no public source: the CLI is licensed under the AWS Intellectual Property License (Q CLI was Apache-2.0) [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/upgrade-guides/migrating-from-q.md]; kirodotdev/Kiro@bfe7ff3 holds only issue-triage scripts and docs [inferred from its file tree]. Legacy Q CLI source: aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3 (last release v1.19.7, 2025-11-17).

### 1. Detection
- IDE: `/Applications/Kiro.app` (bundle id `dev.kiro.desktop`; Code OSS fork; launcher `kiro`; extensions from open-vsx.org by default) [doc: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/kiro.json; https://kiro.dev/docs/ide/editor/extension-registry.md]. CLI: `/Applications/Kiro CLI.app` (`dev.kiro.cli`), binary `kiro-cli` (+ `~/.local/bin/kiro-cli`, `~/.local/bin/kiro-cli-chat`); `q` / `q chat` still work [doc: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/kiro-cli.json; https://kiro.dev/docs/upgrade-guides/migrating-from-q.md]. Kiro CLI replaced Q Developer CLI from 2025-11-17 [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/upgrade-guides/migrating-from-q.md].
- Dirs: global `~/.kiro/`; `KIRO_HOME` relocates agents, prompts, skills, steering, settings and sessions (CLI 2.3.0, 2026-05-12) [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/configuration.md; https://kiro.dev/changelog/cli/page/3/]. Per-app data: `~/Library/Application Support/Kiro` (IDE), `~/Library/Application Support/kiro-cli` (CLI) [doc: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/kiro.json; https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/kiro-cli.json]; Linux/Windows variants [3p: github.com/getagentseal/codeburn@ad242b3:docs/providers/kiro.md; Paresh-Maheshwari/kiro-cli-history@ed29302:README.md]. Legacy Q: `~/Library/Application Support/amazon-q/data.sqlite3`, `~/.aws/amazonq/` [src: aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3:crates/chat-cli/src/util/paths.rs#L59-L66,L327-L331]. CLI logs `$TMPDIR/kiro-log` [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/upgrade-guides/migrating-from-q.md].
- Env: `KIRO_HOME`, `KIRO_API_KEY` (headless), `KIRO_CHAT_UI`, `KIRO_LOG_LEVEL`, `KIRO_LOG_NO_COLOR` [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/headless.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/reference/cli-commands.md].

### 2. History location (five generations; vendor docs never specify on-disk layouts)
| Surface / generation | Location (macOS) | Format | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| CLI TUI V2/V3 (TUI default from CLI 2.0; changelog entry 2.0.1 dated 2026-04-16) | `~/.kiro/sessions/cli/<uuid>.jsonl` + `<uuid>.json` sidecar (+ `.lock`) | JSONL | vendor says only "Session data is stored in ~/.kiro/sessions/" [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/v3/new-features.md]; layout [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/kiro.rs; getagentseal/codeburn@ad242b3:docs/providers/kiro.md; Paresh-Maheshwari/kiro-cli-history@ed29302:README.md] |
| CLI classic (`--classic`, deprecated 2.26.0) | `~/Library/Application Support/kiro-cli/data.sqlite3`: `conversations_v2`, older `conversations` | SQLite | [3p: Paresh-Maheshwari/kiro-cli-history@ed29302:internal/session/sqlite.go#L70-L162] |
| Amazon Q CLI (up to v1.19.7) | `~/Library/Application Support/amazon-q/data.sqlite3`, table `conversations(key=cwd, value=JSON)` | SQLite | [src: aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3:crates/chat-cli/src/database/sqlite_migrations/007_conversations_table.sql; aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3:crates/chat-cli/src/database/mod.rs#L385-L411] |
| IDE 0.x | `~/Library/Application Support/Kiro/User/globalStorage/kiro.kiroagent/<workspace-hash>/*.chat`, extensionless session/execution files (after 2026-02), `workspace-sessions/<base64url(path)>/<uuid>.json` | JSON | [3p: getagentseal/codeburn@ad242b3:docs/providers/kiro.md] |
| IDE 1.x | `~/.kiro/sessions/<workspace-hash>/sess_<id>/{session.json,messages.jsonl,publish.cursor,snapshots/}` | JSONL event log | vendor: "IDE 1.0 uses a new session storage format", 0.x sessions need migration [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/ide/whats-new-v1.md]; layout [3p: getagentseal/codeburn@ad242b3:docs/providers/kiro.md] |
- The vendor session-management page says "Local database (`~/.kiro/`)" and per-directory UUID sessions, which conflicts with the JSONL layout; treat as stale or ambiguous [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/chat/session-management.md]. Another 3p issue sample (`{"role","content"}` lines) conflicts with `{version,kind,data}` and was unverified by its author; not used [3p: https://github.com/vshulcz/deja-vu/issues/3103].
- Cloud sessions (Kiro Web/Mobile/CLI cloud) are server-side [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cloud-sessions.md]. `--no-interactive` runs reportedly land only in `data.sqlite3` [3p: https://github.com/vshulcz/deja-vu/issues/4300 (title only; unverified)].

### 3. Storage format
| Concept | CLI JSONL + sidecar [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/kiro.rs; Paresh-Maheshwari/kiro-cli-history@ed29302:internal/session/jsonl.go] | Classic SQLite / Q CLI [src: aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3:crates/chat-cli/src/cli/chat/conversation.rs] [3p: Paresh-Maheshwari/kiro-cli-history@ed29302:internal/session/sqlite.go] |
|---|---|---|
| session id | filename `<uuid>`; sidecar `session_id` | `conversations_v2.conversation_id`; Q JSON `conversation_id` [src: aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3:crates/chat-cli/src/cli/chat/conversation.rs#L107-L109] |
| title | sidecar `title` (may be null; first prompt used) | none (basename of cwd) |
| project/cwd | sidecar `cwd` | `key` column (cwd path) |
| created/updated | sidecar `created_at`, `updated_at`; Prompt `data.meta.timestamp` (epoch seconds) | `conversations_v2.created_at`, `updated_at` (ms) [3p]; `history[].user.timestamp` [src: aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3:crates/chat-cli/src/cli/chat/message.rs#L54-L59] |
| user text | line `kind:"Prompt"`, `data.content[]` blocks `{kind:"text",data}` | `history[].user.content.Prompt.prompt` |
| assistant text | `kind:"AssistantMessage"` text blocks (skip `thinking`) | `history[].assistant.Response.content` / `ToolUse.content` [src: aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3:crates/chat-cli/src/cli/chat/message.rs#L436-L448] |
| tool calls | blocks `kind:"toolUse"` | `assistant.ToolUse.tool_uses[]` |
| tool results | line `kind:"ToolResults"`, blocks `toolResult` | next `user.content.ToolUseResults.tool_use_results[]` `{tool_use_id, content[], status}` [src: aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3:crates/chat-cli/src/cli/chat/message.rs#L333-L339] |
| other | sidecar `session_state.model_info.model_id`, `session_state` metering (multi-MB) | JSON `model_info`, `request_metadata`, `latest_summary`, `transcript` [src: aws/amazon-q-developer-cli@15cc8f3:crates/chat-cli/src/cli/chat/conversation.rs#L107-L152] |
Each JSONL line is `{"version":"v1","kind":...,"data":...}`; unknown kinds should be skipped [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/kiro.rs]. IDE 1.x `messages.jsonl` lines are `{id,timestamp,payload:{type,...}}`; vendor export zip has `session.json`, `messages.jsonl`, `sub-executions/*.jsonl` [3p: getagentseal/codeburn@ad242b3:docs/providers/kiro.md] [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/ide/chat/chat-export.md].
- Format changes: Q `conversations` -> Kiro classic `conversations_v2` [3p] -> TUI JSONL. Vendor changelog: experimental TUI in CLI 1.28.1 (2026-03-20), default from CLI 2.0 (changelog entry 2.0.1, 2026-04-16); classic sessions still load in the TUI; V3 sessions cannot resume in V2 [doc: https://kiro.dev/changelog/cli/page/3/; https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/terminal-ui.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/v3/new-features.md]. The exact version that began writing JSONL is not stated by the vendor [inferred: tied to the TUI/V2 engine]. IDE: .chat -> extensionless (2026-02) -> `sess_` dirs (IDE 1.0) [3p: getagentseal/codeburn@ad242b3:docs/providers/kiro.md].
- Incremental: JSONL is append-only; keep a byte offset per file, skip 0-byte files and `.lock`; the sidecar is rewritten and its large `session_state` follows the metadata fields (readers parse only the first 16 KB) [3p: Paresh-Maheshwari/kiro-cli-history@ed29302:internal/session/jsonl.go#L42-L65]. SQLite values are whole-row rewrites per turn ("every conversation turn saved") [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/chat/session-management.md]; use `updated_at`. Children (tangent, rewind, sub-agent) are separate sessions [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/chat/session-management.md].

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- No encryption documented; home state and session data are owner-only permissions [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/chat/session-management.md]. Local sessions stay local; service content for Free/individual users is stored in US East (N. Virginia) [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/privacy-and-security/data-protection.md].
- Export: CLI `/chat save <path>` and `/chat load <path>` (session JSON; `save-via-script`/`load-via-script` hooks) [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/chat/session-management.md]; IDE "Export Chat" zip, read-only, not re-importable [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/ide/chat/chat-export.md]; V3 dashboard `kiro-cli chat --sessions`, `--list-sessions`, `--resume-id` [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/chat/session-management.md].

### 5. Integration surfaces (IDE and CLI share one harness and one `~/.kiro` tree)
- **MCP**: `~/.kiro/settings/mcp.json` (user) and `.kiro/settings/mcp.json` (workspace; merged, workspace wins); Q-era `~/.aws/amazonq/mcp.json` is copied once at install and project `.amazonq/mcp.json` still read. Keys: `command`, `args`, `env`, `disabled`, `autoApprove`, `disabledTools`; remote `url`, `headers`, `oauth`. CLI: `kiro-cli mcp add --name recall --command "recall serve" --scope global|workspace [--env K=V,..] [--timeout ms] [--force]`; also `mcp list|remove|import --file|status`. Custom agents use `mcpServers` / `includeMcpJson` [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/mcp/configuration.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/reference/cli-commands.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/upgrade-guides/migrating-from-q.md].
  ```json
  {"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{},"disabled":false}}}
  ```
- **Global instructions**: steering `~/.kiro/steering/*.md` (global) and `.kiro/steering/*.md` (workspace wins), front matter `inclusion: always|fileMatch|manual|auto` with `fileMatchPattern`; `AGENTS.md` is always-on from the workspace root, subdirectories, or `~/.kiro/steering/`; Q `.amazonq/rules` still read; custom agents must list steering in `resources` [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/steering.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/v3/new-features.md].
- **Skills**: yes (Agent Skills): `~/.kiro/skills/<name>/SKILL.md`, `.kiro/skills/`; `/name` slash with `$ARGUMENTS`; custom agents need `resources: ["skill://..."]`; docs list only `~/.kiro/skills` and `.kiro/skills` (no `~/.agents/skills`) [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/skills.md].
- **Hooks (injecting)**: v1 JSON in `~/.kiro/hooks/<id>.json` and `.kiro/hooks/<id>.json`: `{"version":"v1","hooks":[{"name","trigger","matcher","action":{"type":"command|agent","command"|"prompt"},"timeout","enabled"}]}`. Triggers (PascalCase): `SessionStart` (IDE, CLI V3, Web), `UserPromptSubmit`, `Stop`, `SessionEnd` (CLI V3), `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `PostFileCreate|Save|Delete`, `PreTaskExec`, `PostTaskExec`, `Manual`. Legacy names: IDE 0.x `.kiro.hook` `when.type` `agentSpawn|promptSubmit|agentStop|fileEdited|fileCreated|fileDeleted|preToolUse|postToolUse|preTaskExecution|postTaskExecution|userTriggered`; CLI 2.x embeds camelCase `agentSpawn|userPromptSubmit|preToolUse|postToolUse|stop` in agent-config `hooks`. stdin JSON: `hook_event_name`, `cwd`, `session_id`, plus `prompt` (UserPromptSubmit), `tool_name`, `tool_input`, `tool_response`. Exit 0: STDOUT is added to the agent context for SessionStart and UserPromptSubmit (ignored for others); exit 2 blocks PreToolUse/UserPromptSubmit/PreTaskExec (STDERR to model); a Stop hook may print `{"decision":"block","reason":..}` to continue; an `agent` action on UserPromptSubmit appends its prompt to the user prompt. Timeout default 60 s in the v1 schema vs 30 s (`timeout_ms`) in the CLI 2.x text [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/hooks.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/hooks/types.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/hooks/actions.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/v3/hooks-migration.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/ide/whats-new-v1/hooks.md].
- **Plugins/extensions**: Powers (`~/.kiro/powers/`; `plugin.json` per the Agent Plugins spec, or legacy `POWER.md`; bundle `mcp.json`, skills, steering; install from GitHub URL, local dir or catalog; IDE-installed powers auto-appear in the CLI) [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/powers/create.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/powers/installation.md]. Kiro also runs as an ACP agent (`kiro-cli acp [--agent NAME]`, JSON-RPC over stdio) for ACP editors such as Zed and JetBrains [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/acp.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/how-kiro-works.md].
- **Slash commands**: saved prompts `~/.kiro/prompts/*.md` and `.kiro/prompts/` (V3 exposes them as slash commands), skills `/name`, manual steering `/filename` [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/upgrade-guides/migrating-from-q.md; https://kiro.dev/changelog/cli/].
- **Headless**: `kiro-cli chat --no-interactive [--trust-all-tools|--trust-tools=read,grep] [--agent A] [--model ID] [--effort L] "prompt"` (or piped stdin); requires `KIRO_API_KEY` (paid plans); `--output-format stream-json` (JSONL; V2/V3); `--list-models --format json`; `--v3|--v2` selects the engine [doc: https://kiro.dev/docs/cli/headless.md; https://kiro.dev/docs/reference/cli-commands.md].

**Best recall injection point:** a global command hook `~/.kiro/hooks/recall.json` on `SessionStart` and `UserPromptSubmit` (stdout becomes context; works in IDE 1.x and CLI V3, and as `agentSpawn`/`userPromptSubmit` inside a custom agent for CLI 2.x, whose default agent is in-memory [inferred from migrating-from-q.md]); static fallback `~/.kiro/steering/recall.md` (`inclusion: always`); register MCP with `kiro-cli mcp add --scope global` or by editing `~/.kiro/settings/mcp.json`.
**Confidence / unknowns:** config, hooks and MCP semantics are vendor-doc backed (high); session file layouts are third-party only, changed four times in about 12 months, and the version that started JSONL, the IDE 1.x event schema and whether V3 still writes SQLite are unconfirmed.

---

## Amp (ampcode.com; Sourcegraph / Amp Inc.)


**Name collision:** a Homebrew core formula named `amp` is an unrelated text editor [3p: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/formula/amp.json].
**Version verified:** CLI `@ampcode/cli` 0.0.1791273659-g33d612 (npm, 2026-10-06) [doc: https://registry.npmjs.org/@ampcode/cli]; docs read 2026-10-06 [doc: https://ampcode.com/llms.txt]. Closed source: sources are docs, the vendor's npm bundles and installer.

### 1. Detection
- CLI `amp`: installer `curl -fsSL https://ampcode.com/install.sh | bash` puts the binary in `$AMP_HOME/bin` (default `~/.amp/bin/amp`) and symlinks it into the first of `~/.local/bin`, `~/bin`, `~/.bin` that is on PATH [src: https://ampcode.com/install.sh#L5-L9,L74,L380-L386]. npm: `@ampcode/cli` (renamed from `@sourcegraph/amp`; old name was an alias, removal scheduled 2026-06-15) now ships a Bun-compiled single executable with per-platform optional deps like `@ampcode/cli-darwin-arm64` [doc: https://ampcode.com/news/npm-package-changes; https://registry.npmjs.org/@ampcode/cli]. `amp version`.
- Editor extensions (VS Code `sourcegraph.amp`, Cursor) were shut off on 2026-03-05 20:00 PT; the CLI connects to VS Code, Cursor, Windsurf, Zed, Neovim through `ide connect` [doc: https://ampcode.com/news/the-coding-agent-is-dead; https://ampcode.com/docs/cli]. Leftover globalStorage dirs might hold old data [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/amp.rs#L48-L70; unverified].
- macOS app "Amp" (drag to Applications, macOS 26+, beta) is a remote-control client and optional runner; bundle id unknown [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/macos-and-ios].
- Dirs/env: config `~/.config/amp/` (`settings.json[c]`, `AGENTS.md`, `plugins/`, `skills/`, legacy `tools/`; `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` honoured for plugins); data `$XDG_DATA_HOME/amp` or `~/.local/share/amp` [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/cli/settings; https://ampcode.com/docs/customize/plugins; src: npm @sourcegraph/amp@0.0.1774959077-ga71644:package/dist/main.js (data dir = `XDG_DATA_HOME || ~/.local/share` + `amp`)]. Env: `AMP_HOME`, `AMP_SETTINGS_FILE` (or `--settings-file`), `AMP_URL`, `AMP_API_KEY` (CI token; never touch), `AMP_TOOLBOX`, `AMP_IGNORE_GUIDANCE_FILES`, `AMP_SKIP_UPDATE_CHECK`, `AMP_LOG_FILE` [doc: settings, agents-md pages; src: bundle `AMP_*` strings, 0.0.1774963753-g9f23e6]. `AMP_DATA_DIR` is ccusage's variable, not Amp's [3p: ryoppippi/ccusage@f5a3007:rust/adapters/amp/src/paths.rs#L5-L25].

### 2. History location
- Current threads are server-side only: URL `https://ampcode.com/threads/T-<uuid>`, usable from web, CLI, apps [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/threads]. Local thread store was dropped on 2026-03-31: the string `threads-development` (the file-store call) is present in `@sourcegraph/amp@0.0.1774959077-ga71644` `dist/main.js` and gone in `0.0.1774963753-g9f23e6` [src: npm bundles, string count 1 to 0]; a third party reports the data dir now holds only `bin`, `logs`, `pids` [3p: https://github.com/vshulcz/deja-vu/issues/4355]. May 2026 "Neo" CLI rebuild is remote-controllable and plugin-powered [doc: https://ampcode.com/news/neo].
- Legacy local mirror (builds before 2026-03-31, may exist from old installs): `~/.local/share/amp/threads/T-<uuid>.json` [src: bundle `threads` dir under the data dir; 3p: ryoppippi/ccusage@f5a3007 docs/guide/amp/index.md].
- Retrieval for current threads, all need a login: `amp threads list [--include-archived]`, `amp threads search <q>`, `amp threads markdown <T-id|url>`, hidden `amp threads export <T-id|url>` ("Export a thread as JSON ... full thread payload") [src: npm @sourcegraph/amp@0.0.1774963753-g9f23e6 command definitions; doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/threads]. Web: add `.md` to the thread URL; More Actions, Export gives Markdown, and JSON for the creator [doc: threads page]. Workspace External API v2 (machine-to-machine OAuth app): `GET /api/v2/threads`, `/threads/{id}/messages`, `/threads/{id}/usage`; message fields other than `messageID`, `messageVersion`, `createdAt` are "not stable" [doc: https://ampcode.com/api/external].

### 3. Storage format
- Legacy local thread JSON, one file per thread. Top-level `v`, `id` (`T-<uuid>`), `created` (epoch ms), `title`, `env.initial.trees[].uri`, `messages[]`, `meta`, `agentMode`, relationship ids (`originThreadID`, `mainThreadID`) [src: npm @sourcegraph/amp@0.0.1774959077-ga71644:package/dist/main.js (thread summarizer, message builders); 3p: deja-vu#4356]. Older builds also had `usageLedger.events[]` {id, timestamp, model, tokens{input,output,total}, credits, toMessageId} (absent from the March 2026 bundle) [3p: ryoppippi/ccusage@f5a3007:rust/adapters/amp/src/parser.rs#L20-L75; https://github.com/getagentseal/codeburn/pull/1578].

| Concept | Field |
|---|---|
| session id | `id` = `T-<uuid>` (filename stem) |
| title | `title` (nullable) |
| project/cwd | `env.initial.trees[0].uri` (file URI) [3p: deja-vu#4356] |
| created / updated | `created` (ms); last interaction = max(`created`, user `messages[].meta.sentAt`) [src: bundle]; file mtime is NOT bumped on append [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/amp.rs#L284-L290] |
| user text | `messages[]` `role:"user"`, `content[]` blocks `type:"text"`; `meta.sentAt` (ms), `fileMentions`, `userState` [src: bundle] |
| assistant text | `role:"assistant"` blocks `text` / `thinking`; per-message `usage` (input/output/cache tokens) [src: bundle; 3p: codeburn#1578] |
| tool calls | assistant block `tool_use` {id, name, input, complete}; names Bash(cmd,cwd), Read, edit_file(path,old_str,new_str), create_file, Task, oracle, `mcp__<srv>__<tool>` [3p: deja-vu#4356] |
| tool results | user block `tool_result` {toolUseID, run:{status, result{output/diff, exitCode}}} [src: bundle `toolUseID`/`run`] |
| other | `messageId` (int), `meta.traces`, ledger credits |

- Documented headless schema (stable, Claude-Code-compatible): `{"type":"system","subtype":"init","cwd","session_id":"T-...","tools":[...],"mcp_servers":[...]}`, `{"type":"user"/"assistant","message":{...},"parent_tool_use_id","session_id"}`, final `{"type":"result","subtype":"success"/"error_during_execution"/"error_max_turns","duration_ms","num_turns","result","session_id","usage"}` [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/cli/streaming-json]. Shape of `amp threads export` output is unverified (login needed); presumably the same thread object [inferred].
- Format changes across versions: `usageLedger` era (2025) to no ledger (Mar 2026 bundle) [src: bundle string count 0; 3p: ccusage]; local store removed 2026-03-31 [src: npm bundles]; Neo rebuild 2026-05-06, `--take-me-back` removed 2026-05-27 [doc: https://ampcode.com/news/neo; .../drop-the-neo].
- Incremental-import notes: legacy files never bump mtime on append, so hash/size-check instead of mtime [3p: amp.rs#L284-L290]. Server side: list threads, compare `updatedAt` (External API) or re-export on demand; thread ids are stable.

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
Server-held on ampcode.com; private by default, workspace/group/unlisted sharing; public discoverable threads removed 2026-06-02 [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/threads; https://ampcode.com/news/end-of-public-threads]. `amp login` keeps account state locally and `AMP_API_KEY` (`sgamp_...`) works headless; neither is to be read [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/cli; .../cli/execute-mode]. Export paths in section 2. Minimal Data Retention only governs model providers [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/enterprise/minimal-data-retention].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- MCP: `~/.config/amp/settings.json` (or `.jsonc`) key `amp.mcpServers`; workspace `.amp/settings.json` (nearest upward) overrides; CLI `amp mcp add <name> -- <cmd> <args>` or `amp mcp add <name> <url>`; per-run `--mcp-config '<json>'`; server-stored `amp mcp remote ...`; `${VAR}` expansion; allow/reject with `amp.mcpPermissions`. Docs prefer bundling MCP in a skill (`mcp.json` or `mcpServers` frontmatter, lazy-loaded) [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/customize/mcp; https://ampcode.com/docs/cli/settings].
  ```jsonc
  { "amp.mcpServers": { "recall": { "command": "recall", "args": ["serve"] } } }  // placeholder command
  ```
- Global instructions: `~/.config/amp/AGENTS.md` and `~/.config/AGENTS.md` (always loaded); project `AGENTS.md` in cwd and parents up to `$HOME`, subtree files when a file there is read; `AGENT.md` or `CLAUDE.md` used if no AGENTS.md in that dir; system-wide `/Library/Application Support/ampcode/AGENTS.md`, `/etc/ampcode/AGENTS.md`; `@file` mentions with `globs:` frontmatter; skip via `AMP_IGNORE_GUIDANCE_FILES` [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/customize/agents-md].
- Skills: yes; first match wins in this order: `~/.config/agents/skills/`, `~/.agents/skills/`, `~/.config/amp/skills/`, `.agents/skills/` (project + parents), `.claude/skills/`, `~/.claude/skills/`, `~/.claude/plugins/cache/`, `amp.skills.path`, built-in, hosted personal/workspace skill repos; searched 5 levels deep; `amp skills list`, `amp skill add <src> [--global]` [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/customize/skills; .../cli/settings].
- Hooks = plugins (TS/JS run by Bun, package `@ampcode/plugin`): `.amp/plugins/<name>.ts` or `<name>/index.ts` (project), `~/.config/amp/plugins/` (system; `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/amp/plugins`), plus hosted personal/workspace repos; `amp plugins add <url>`, `amp plugins list` [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/customize/plugins]. Events via `amp.on(...)`: `session.start` (payload `{thread:{id}}`, fire-and-forget, cannot inject), `agent.start` (user prompt submitted; payload `{thread, message, id, changesWorkflow?}`; may return `{message:{content, display?}}` = text appended after the user's content in the user message, i.e. INJECTED into model context), `agent.end` (`status`, `messages`; may return `{action:'continue', userMessage}`), `tool.call` (return allow / reject-and-continue / modify / synthesize), `tool.result` (may replace output), `changes.prompt`. No `session.end` [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/plugin-api (types `AgentStartResult`, `PluginRequestResultMap`)]. In `amp -x`, `agent.start/agent.end` only fire when `--plugin-ready-timeout [s]` is passed [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/cli/execute-mode].
- Slash commands: custom commands (`.agents/commands`, `~/.config/amp/commands`) were removed in Jan 2026 in favour of skills; plugins add palette commands via `amp.registerCommand` [doc: https://ampcode.com/news/slashing-custom-commands; plugins page].
- Headless: `amp -x "<prompt>"` / `--execute` (implied when stdout is redirected; stdin piping works), `--stream-json`, `--stream-json-input`, `--stream-json-thinking`, `--mcp-config`, `--settings-file`, `amp threads continue <T-id> -x`, `-ox` runs in an orb; auth via `AMP_API_KEY`; SDKs `@ampcode/sdk` (TypeScript) and Python [doc: https://ampcode.com/docs/cli/execute-mode; .../cli/streaming-json; .../sdk].

**Best recall injection point:** a plugin whose `agent.start` handler returns `{message:{content:<recall digest>}}` (only per-prompt injection; ship in `~/.config/amp/plugins/` or via `amp plugins add`); static fallback `~/.config/amp/AGENTS.md` with an `@~/path/to/memory.md` mention; MCP via `amp mcp add recall -- <cmd>` or `amp.mcpServers`; shared skill in `~/.agents/skills/recall/`.
**Confidence / unknowns:** Integration high (vendor docs). History: the "no local threads since 2026-03-31" call rests on the vendor npm bundle diff plus one third-party issue; the exact `amp threads export` payload, any remaining local cache contents, and the macOS app bundle id are unverified.
