## Factory Droid (Factory AI; `droid` CLI + Factory App)


Droid reads `~/.agents/skills` as a personal compat skills dir [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/skills].
**Version verified:** docs changelog CLI v0.233.0 / Desktop v0.190.0 (2026-10-03) [doc: https://docs.factory.com/changelog/release-notes]; npm `droid` 0.234.0 (2026-10-06) [doc: https://registry.npmjs.org/droid]. Canonical docs host is now docs.factory.com (the docs.factory.ai llms.txt index links there).

### 1. Detection
- CLI `droid`: `curl -fsSL https://app.factory.ai/cli | sh` installs `~/.local/bin/droid` [src: https://app.factory.ai/cli#L106]; Homebrew cask `droid` (binary on `$HOMEBREW_PREFIX/bin`; zap lists `~/.factory`) [3p: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/droid.json]; npm `droid` / `@factory/cli`; Windows `irm https://app.factory.ai/cli/windows | iex` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/droid-cli/cli-reference]. Standalone builds auto-update unless `FACTORY_DROID_AUTO_UPDATE_ENABLED=false` [doc: same page].
- Desktop "Factory App" (Electron): `/Applications/Factory.app` via cask `factory`; support dirs `~/Library/Application Support/Factory` and `ai.factory.desktop`, prefs `ai.factory.desktop.plist` (so CFBundleIdentifier is probably `ai.factory.desktop`) [3p: https://formulae.brew.sh/api/cask/factory.json]. Whether App sessions also land in `~/.factory/sessions`: unknown.
- Data dir `~/.factory/` holds `settings.json`, `settings.local.json`, `mcp.json`, `hooks.json`, `skills/`, `droids/`, `commands/`, `output-styles/`, `sessions/`, `logs/`, `worktrees/`, `specs/`, `automations/` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/droid-cli/settings; .../harness/mcp; .../harness/hooks; .../harness/skills]. No documented env var relocates it; the vendor SDK hard-codes `Path.home()/.factory/sessions` [src: Factory-AI/droid-sdk-python@aa6fd94:src/droid_sdk/_high_level/discovery.py#L16-L17]. Env seen in docs: `FACTORY_API_KEY` (never touch), `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR`, `DROID_PLUGIN_ROOT`, `FACTORY_LOG_FILE`, `FACTORY_DISABLE_KEYRING` [doc: llms-full.txt].

### 2. History location
- `~/.factory/sessions/<slug>/<session-uuid>.jsonl` with sibling `<session-uuid>.settings.json`; `<slug>` = `-` + cwd with path separators replaced by `-`; legacy flat `~/.factory/sessions/<uuid>.jsonl` (cwd then comes from the first line); `~/.factory/sessions/.favorites` = JSON array of ids [src: Factory-AI/droid-sdk-python@aa6fd94:src/droid_sdk/_high_level/discovery.py#L16-L175]. Windows `%USERPROFILE%\.factory\` is documented for settings; sessions path there is [inferred].
- Slug decoding is lossy (SDK replaces only separators, SpecStory replaces every non-alphanumeric); read `cwd` from `session_start` instead [src: discovery.py#L20-L29; 3p: specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a:specstory-cli/pkg/providers/droidcli/path_utils.go#L15-L25].
- `cloudSessionSync` (default true) mirrors sessions to Factory web; `sessionRetentionDays` is an org setting for the synced copy [doc: https://docs.factory.com/droid-cli/settings (Cloud session sync)]. Stream-json/exported logs may sit in `~/.factory/projects/**/*.jsonl` [3p: jazzyalex/agent-sessions@6fa9a73:AgentSessions/Services/DroidSessionDiscovery.swift#L1-L10]; the hooks doc example `transcript_path` also points under `.factory/projects/.../session.jsonl`, contradicting the sessions dir, so trust `transcript_path` from a live hook payload [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/hooks].

### 3. Storage format
- JSONL, one event per line. Line 1 is `{"type":"session_start", id, title, owner, cwd, ...}` (SDK requires string `title`; `cwd` and `owner` optional) [src: Factory-AI/droid-sdk-python@aa6fd94:src/droid_sdk/_high_level/discovery.py#L123-L160]. Remaining line types come from third-party parsers only (CLI is closed source): `message` {id, timestamp, message{role, model?, content: string or blocks}}, `todo_state` {todos[{description,status}]}, `compaction_state` {summary{title,body}}; blocks `text`, `thinking`/`reasoning`, `tool_use` {id,name,input,riskLevel,riskLevelReason}, `tool_result` {tool_use_id, content, is_error} [3p: specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a:specstory-cli/pkg/providers/droidcli/parser.go#L19-L77,L116-L162; jazzyalex/agent-sessions@6fa9a73:AgentSessions/Services/DroidSessionParser.swift#L4-L10; Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/factory.rs#L1-L12].
- Companion `<uuid>.settings.json`: `model`, `tokenUsage{inputTokens, outputTokens, cacheCreationTokens, cacheReadTokens, thinkingTokens}`, `archivedAt` (archived sessions are hidden by the SDK) [src: discovery.py#L150-L158; 3p: getspecstory@c80e93a:.../droidcli/types.go#L90-L100].

| Concept | Field |
|---|---|
| session id | `session_start.id` = filename stem `<uuid>` [src: discovery.py#L123-L170] |
| title | `session_start.title` (`sessionTitle` also seen) [src: same; 3p: parser.go] |
| project/cwd | `session_start.cwd`; parent dir slug [src: same] |
| created / updated | SDK uses file birth time / mtime (no created field) [src: discovery.py#L159-L171]; per message `timestamp` ISO-8601, sometimes epoch ms [3p: factory.rs tests] |
| user text | `message.role=="user"` text blocks (leading `<system-reminder>` blocks should be stripped) [3p: DroidSessionParser.swift#L877-L880] |
| assistant text | `message.role=="assistant"` `text` blocks; reasoning in `thinking` |
| tool calls | `tool_use`; tool names `Execute, Read, Edit, Create, ApplyPatch, LS, Glob, Grep, Task, FetchUrl, WebSearch`, `mcp__<srv>__<tool>` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/hooks matcher list] |
| tool results | `tool_result` {tool_use_id, content, is_error} in the next user message [3p: parser.go] |
| other | `settings.json` model + `tokenUsage`; `riskLevel`; `todo_state`; `compaction_state`; `.favorites` |

- Format changes across versions: flat sessions dir to per-project subdirs (SDK reads both) [src: discovery.py#L52-L108]; `.droid.yaml` and `config.json` superseded by `settings.json` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/droid-cli/settings]; exec `stream-json` input deprecated for `stream-jsonrpc` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/droid-exec/overview].
- Incremental-import notes: per-event appends (SpecStory watches create/write events on `.jsonl`) [3p: getspecstory@c80e93a:.../droidcli/watcher.go#L120-L170]. Detect new sessions by directory listing, changed ones by `(size, mtime)`; read `.settings.json` after the JSONL because it is rewritten for token usage; skip `archivedAt`; message count is simply non-empty lines after line 1 [src: discovery.py#L123-L160]. Whether rewind/compaction rewrites earlier lines is unverified.

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
Plaintext local files plus an on-by-default cloud mirror (`cloudSessionSync`) [doc: settings page]. No export command; official read paths: `droid search "<q>" [--kind message_text/document/tool_use/tool_result/all] [--limit-sessions N] --json [--reindex]` over a local index, SDK `list_sessions` / `listSessions` (metadata only) and `resume`/`load_session` for full content [doc: https://docs.factory.com/droid-cli/cli-reference; src: Factory-AI/droid-sdk-python@aa6fd94:examples/list_saved_sessions.py#L1-L10]. OAuth tokens sit in the keyring or a fallback file (never read) [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/mcp].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- MCP: `~/.factory/mcp.json` (user), `.factory/mcp.json` (project root), and `.factory/mcp.json` in ancestor folders; `droid mcp add <name> <urlOrCommand...> --type stdio/http/sse [--env K=V] [--header "K: V"] [--no-oauth]` always writes the user file; `droid mcp list/remove/permissions`; live reload; `${NAME}` expansion only in `env`, `headers`, `oauth.clientId/clientSecret` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/mcp].
  ```json
  { "mcpServers": { "recall": { "type": "stdio", "command": "recall", "args": ["serve"], "disabled": false } } }
  ```
  (placeholder command) or `droid mcp add recall "recall serve" --type stdio`.
- Global instructions: `AGENTS.md` (also `agents.md`, `Agents.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `Claude.md`) searched cwd up to the git root, in each dir and its `.factory/`, `.agents/`, `.agent/`; personal dirs `~/.factory/`, `~/.agents/`, `~/.agent/`; nested files loaded on file reads; caps 80,000 chars initial / 40,000 dynamic [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/agents-md].
- Skills: yes: `<repo>/.factory/skills/<n>/SKILL.md`, folder-level `.factory/skills/`, personal `~/.factory/skills/<n>/SKILL.md`, compat `<repo>/.agents/skills/**`, `.agent/skills/**`, `~/.agents/skills/**`, `~/.agent/skills/**`, plugin skills; frontmatter `name`, `description`, optional `allowed-tools`, `disable-model-invocation` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/skills].
- Hooks (Claude-Code-style): `~/.factory/hooks.json` (user), `.factory/hooks.json` (project), `hooks` key in settings.json as fallback, legacy `.factory/hooks/hooks.json`, plugin `hooks/hooks.json`, org-managed. Shape `{ "<Event>": [ { "matcher": "...", "commandRegex": "...", "hooks": [ { "type": "command", "command": "...", "timeout": 60 } ] } ] }`; matcher empty/`*`/exact/regex (case-sensitive) on tool name. Events: `PreToolUse, PostToolUse, UserPromptSubmit, Notification, Stop, SubagentStop, PreCompact, SessionStart, SessionEnd`. Stdin JSON: `session_id, transcript_path, cwd, permission_mode, hook_event_name, message_id?` plus `tool_name/tool_input/tool_response`, `prompt/has_images`, `source` (startup/resume/clear/compact) for `SessionStart`, `reason/session_duration_ms/message_count` for `SessionEnd`; env `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR`, `DROID_PLUGIN_ROOT`. INJECTION: exit-0 stdout becomes context for `UserPromptSubmit` and `SessionStart`; JSON `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` is appended for `SessionStart` and `PostToolUse` (and `additionalContext` for `UserPromptSubmit`, nesting not stated, assume `hookSpecificOutput` [inferred]); other events' stdout is only shown in the transcript (`showHookOutput`); exit 2 blocks `PreToolUse`/`UserPromptSubmit` and feeds stderr back on `PostToolUse`/`Stop` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/hooks; https://docs.factory.com/droid-cli/settings].
- Plugins: dir with `.factory-plugin/plugin.json` plus `skills/ commands/ droids/ output-styles/ hooks/hooks.json mcp.json`; Claude layouts (`.claude-plugin/`, `agents/`, `.mcp.json`) are translated; `droid plugin marketplace add/list/update/remove`, `droid plugin install <plugin@marketplace> --scope user/project` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/plugins]. A third-party tool can ship hooks + skill + `mcp.json` as one marketplace plugin.
- Slash commands: `~/.factory/commands/*.md` (or shebang scripts) and `<repo>/.factory/commands`; `$ARGUMENTS` only; skills preferred [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/custom-slash-commands]. Custom droids (subagents): `~/.factory/droids/*.md`, `.factory/droids/*.md` [doc: https://docs.factory.com/harness/subagents].
- Headless: `droid exec "<prompt>"` (`-f file`, stdin), `-o text/json/stream-json/stream-jsonrpc`, `--input-format stream-jsonrpc`, `--auto low/medium/high` (default read-only), `-s <session-id>` resume, `--fork`, `--cwd`, `--settings <file>`, `--append-system-prompt[-file]`, `--tag`; `json` result `{type:"result", subtype, is_error, duration_ms, num_turns, result, session_id}`; JSON-RPC methods `droid.initialize_session` / `droid.load_session`; SDKs `@factory/droid-sdk` (TS) and `droid-sdk` (Python) [doc: https://docs.factory.com/droid-exec/overview; https://docs.factory.com/droid-cli/cli-reference].

**Best recall injection point:** `SessionStart` and `UserPromptSubmit` hooks in `~/.factory/hooks.json` that print the recall digest (stdout, or `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext`): true session-start plus per-prompt injection; MCP through `~/.factory/mcp.json` or `droid mcp add`; package hooks + skill + MCP as a plugin for scoped installs.
**Confidence / unknowns:** Integration high (vendor docs). Session location and line 1 are vendor-source confirmed (SDK); the rest of the JSONL schema is third-party-only; unknown: Factory App session store, real `transcript_path`, any relocation env var.

## Pi coding agent (Earendil / earendil-works/pi, formerly badlogic/pi-mono)


**Version verified:** `@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent` v1.0.4 (2026-10-05), `earendil-works/pi@428a12b` (main, 2026-10-06); `gh api repos/badlogic/pi-mono` resolves to `earendil-works/pi`; product site pi.dev. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/package.json#L2-L10]

### 1. Detection
- Binary `pi`: `curl -fsSL https://pi.dev/install.sh | sh` or `npm i -g --ignore-scripts @earendil-works/pi-coding-agent` (Node >= 22.19). [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/README.md#L28-L43] Agent dir `~/.pi/agent` (`piConfig.configDir=".pi"`).
- Env: `PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR` (agent dir), `PI_CODING_AGENT_SESSION_DIR` (sessions; `--session-dir` wins; `sessionDir` setting also works), `PI_PACKAGE_DIR`, `PI_OFFLINE`. Pi exports `PI_CODING_AGENT=true`, `PI_SESSION_ID`, `PI_SESSION_FILE`, `PI_PROVIDER`, `PI_MODEL` to child shell commands. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/environment-variables.md#L16-L32] [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/environment-variables.md#L81-L83]

### 2. History location
- `~/.pi/agent/sessions/--<path>--/<timestamp>_<session-id>.jsonl`; `<path>` = cwd with the leading separator removed and `/`, `\`, `:` replaced by `-` (lossy; header `cwd` is authoritative); file timestamp = ISO time with `:` and `.` replaced by `-`. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L587-L593] [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L1062-L1080] Same layout on Linux and Windows (under the agent dir). [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/session-format.md#L7-L11]

### 3. Storage format
One JSON object per line; first line is the header, later lines are entries linked by `id`/`parentId` (8-hex ids), `timestamp` ISO-8601; nested `message.timestamp` is epoch ms. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/session-format.md]

| Concept | Field |
|---|---|
| session id | header `{"type":"session","version":3,"id":<uuid>,"timestamp","cwd","parentSession?"}` |
| title / name | latest `session_info` entry `name` (`/name`, `--name`); otherwise first user message |
| project / cwd | header `cwd`; fork/clone: header `parentSession` (path of parent file) |
| created / updated | header `timestamp`; last entry `timestamp` or file mtime |
| user text | `type:"message"`, `message.role:"user"`, `content` = string or `[{type:text,text}\|{type:image,data,mimeType}]` |
| assistant text | `message.role:"assistant"`, `content[]` items `{type:"text",text}`, `{type:"thinking",thinking,thinkingSignature,redacted}` |
| tool calls | assistant `content[]` item `{type:"toolCall",id,name,arguments}` |
| tool results | separate entry `message.role:"toolResult"` `{toolCallId,toolName,content[],details,isError}` |
| other roles | `bashExecution` `{command,output,exitCode,cancelled,truncated}`, `custom` (extension-injected `{customType,content,display}`), `branchSummary`, `compactionSummary`, `system` (prompt sections + tool loadout) |
| model / usage | assistant `{api,provider,model,usage{input,output,cacheRead,cacheWrite,totalTokens,cost},stopReason,responseId}`; entries `model_change{provider,modelId}`, `thinking_level_change{thinkingLevel}`, `usage{kind,...}` |
| other entry types | `compaction{summary,firstKeptEntryId,tokensBefore}`, `context_edit{targetId,replacement}`, `branch_summary{fromId,summary}`, `custom{customType,data}` (not in LLM context), `custom_message` (in context), `label{targetId,label}` |

[src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L41-L176] [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/message-types.md] The active conversation is the path from the last entry (leaf) back to the root; abandoned branches stay in the file, and several roots can exist. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/session-format.md]
**Format changes:** v1 linear (no `version`, auto-migrated on load), v2 tree, v3 renamed `hookMessage` role to `custom`; recent builds persist system prompt/tool loadout as `system` messages and add `usage`/`context_edit` entries (older files lack them). [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/session-format.md] A 3rd-party connector documents provider/modelId/thinkingLevel on the header; v3 source has only `id,timestamp,cwd,parentSession`, with the rest in `model_change`/`thinking_level_change`. [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/pi_agent.rs#L1-L15]
**Incremental-import notes:** append-only per entry (`appendFileSync`); the file is created lazily once the session has a user or assistant message; a full `_rewriteFile` happens on version migration and some branch operations, so keep per-file (size, mtime, byte offset) and re-parse from line 1 if the file shrank or the prefix changed; dedupe by entry `id`. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L1124-L1132] [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L1172-L1195]

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- Plain JSONL, no encryption; credentials are in `<agent-dir>/auth.json` (never read). [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/configuration.md#L13-L20] Official export: `pi --export <session.jsonl> [out.html]`; the raw file is the portable form; deleting a session = deleting its file. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/cli.md#L49]

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP (built in since 1.0).** User `~/.pi/agent/mcp.json`, project `.pi/mcp.json` (read only after project trust; a same-name project entry replaces the user entry). Snippet: `{"mcpServers":{"recall":{"command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{}}}}`; HTTP: `url`, `headers`, `oauth`; SSE is rejected; tools surface as `mcp__<server>__<tool>`. CLI: `pi mcp add [-l] <name> [--env K=V] [--cwd d] -- <cmd> [args]` or `--url ... --bearer-token-env-var X` (user-level by default); `/reload` after external edits. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/mcp.md#L1-L75]
- **Global instructions.** `<agent-dir>/AGENTS.override.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `AGENTS.MD`, `CLAUDE.md` or `CLAUDE.MD`, plus AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md from the cwd and its parents (no trust needed); `SYSTEM.md` replaces and `APPEND_SYSTEM.md` appends to the system prompt (user and `.pi/`). [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/configuration.md#L7-L62]
- **Skills.** Agent Skills spec; `~/.pi/agent/skills/`, `.pi/skills/`, `~/.agents/skills/`, `.agents/skills/` (walks up to the repo root); `/skill:name` forces one. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/skills.md#L7-L63]
- **Hooks / extensions.** TypeScript extensions (loaded with jiti) subscribe via `pi.on(event, handler)`: `project_trust`, `resources_discover`, `mcp_servers_change`, `session_start` (`reason`: startup/reload/new/resume/fork), `session_before_switch/fork/compact/tree`, `session_compact`, `session_shutdown`, `context`, `context_with_system`, `before_provider_request/headers`, `after_provider_response`, `before_agent_start`, `agent_start/end/settled`, `turn_start/end`, `message_start/update/end`, `tool_execution_*`, `model_select`, `input`, `user_bash`, `tool_call`, `tool_result`. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/src/core/extensions/types.ts#L679-L1290]
- **Context injection.** Yes, several: `pi.sendMessage({customType,content,display,details},{triggerTurn,deliverAs:"steer"|"followUp"|"nextTurn"})` appends a `custom_message` entry that enters LLM context; `before_agent_start` (after prompt submit, every prompt) may return `{message, systemPrompt}`; `context`/`context_with_system` rewrite messages before each LLM call; `input` can transform or handle user text; `session_start` fires at start/resume/fork. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/src/core/extensions/types.ts#L443-L446] [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/src/core/extensions/types.ts#L1132-L1148] [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/src/core/extensions/types.ts#L1455-L1462]
- **Plugins.** Extension files in `~/.pi/agent/extensions/*.ts` or `*/index.ts`, `.pi/extensions/` (trusted projects), `-e/--extension <path|npm:|git:>`; Pi Packages: `pi install npm:<pkg>` / `git:` / path (written to `settings.json` `packages`; `-l` for project), package.json `"pi":{"extensions":[],"skills":[],"prompts":[]}` + keyword `pi-package`. A third party ships via an npm Pi Package or a dropped `.ts` file. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/packages.md#L12-L74]
- **Slash commands.** Prompt templates `~/.pi/agent/prompts/*.md`, `.pi/prompts/*.md` (frontmatter `description`, `argument-hint`; `$1`, `$@`); extensions call `pi.registerCommand`. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/prompt-templates.md#L9-L55]
- **Headless.** `pi -p/--print "<prompt>"` (final text), `pi --mode json` (JSONL event stream), `pi --mode rpc` (JSONL commands over stdin/stdout), `--no-session`, `--session <path|id>`, `-c`, `-r`, `--fork`, `--session-dir`; SDK `SessionManager` API. [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/cli.md#L25-L51] [src: earendil-works/pi@428a12b:packages/coding-agent/docs/cli.md#L90-L107]

**Best recall injection point:** a small extension `~/.pi/agent/extensions/recall.ts` that on `session_start` (or `before_agent_start`) calls `pi.sendMessage(..., {deliverAs:"nextTurn"})` / returns `{message}`; zero-code fallback is `~/.pi/agent/AGENTS.md` or `APPEND_SYSTEM.md`. Register MCP in `~/.pi/agent/mcp.json` (`pi mcp add` defaults to user scope).
**Confidence / unknowns:** high (first-party docs and source at `428a12b`) but nothing observable locally; unknown: whether agent-dir AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md are both loaded (doc lists them as alternatives), `settings.json` key names (not read).

## Crush (Charm / charmbracelet/crush)


**Version verified:** v0.97.1 (2026-09-29), nightly 2026-10-05, main HEAD `65865e0` (2026-10-06) [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:README.md]. No separate docs site found (repo README + `docs/`).

### 1. Detection
- Binary `crush` (install methods are in the README; not enumerated here). Data dir is per project: `.crush/` in the project (not a global dir). [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/config/config.go#L25-L26]
- Env/flags: `CRUSH_GLOBAL_CONFIG` (dir holding crush.json), `CRUSH_GLOBAL_DATA`, `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`, `XDG_DATA_HOME`, `CRUSH_CACHE_DIR`, `CRUSH_SKILLS_DIR`; flags `--data-dir/-D`, `--cwd/-c`; option `data_directory`. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/config/load.go#L1212-L1285] [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/cmd/root.go#L55-L56] `crush dirs` prints config/data dirs; `crush projects [--json]` lists registered projects. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/cmd/projects.go#L14-L38]

### 2. History location
- `<DataDirectory>/crush.db`. DataDirectory defaults to `.crush`: closest existing `.crush` walking up within the project boundary, else `<cwd>/.crush`; relative `data_directory` resolves against cwd. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/config/load.go#L578-L596] [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/db/connect.go#L93]
- Discovery registry: `~/.local/share/crush/projects.json` = `{"projects":[{"path","data_dir","last_accessed"}]}`. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/projects/projects.go#L14-L32] Linux the same; Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%\crush`, config `%USERPROFILE%\.config\crush`. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:README.md#L286-L319]
- Same dir holds `logs/crush.log`, `commands/`, workspace `crush.json`. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:README.md#L905-L926] A 3rd-party connector claims a global `~/.crush/crush.db`; not supported by the source. [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/crush.rs#L1-L14]

### 3. Storage format
SQLite via goose migrations; WAL, `busy_timeout=30000`, `foreign_keys=ON`; optional per-data-dir lock file. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/db/connect.go#L19-L26]

| Concept | Column / path |
|---|---|
| session id | `sessions.id` (UUID; CLI also accepts a hash prefix) |
| title | `sessions.title` |
| project / cwd | not stored; derive from the `.crush` dir's parent or `projects.json` `data_dir` -> `path` |
| created / updated | `sessions.created_at`, `updated_at` = Unix SECONDS (`strftime('%s')`); trigger rewrites `updated_at` on any session UPDATE |
| other session cols | `parent_session_id`, `message_count`, `prompt_tokens`, `completion_tokens`, `cost`, `summary_message_id`, `todos` (JSON), `channel` |
| messages | `messages.id`, `session_id`, `role` (user/assistant/system/tool), `parts` (JSON), `model`, `provider`, `created_at`, `updated_at`, `finished_at` (seconds), `is_summary_message` |
| user / assistant text | `parts` = JSON array of `{"type":"text","data":{"text","hidden?"}}`; also `reasoning` (`thinking`,`signature`,`started_at`,`finished_at`) |
| tool calls | assistant `parts` item `{"type":"tool_call","data":{"id","name","input"(JSON string),"provider_executed","finished"}}` |
| tool results | `role='tool'` message, item `{"type":"tool_result","data":{"tool_call_id","name","content","data","mime_type","metadata","is_error"}}` |
| other part types | `finish` (`reason`,`time`,`message`,`details`), `image_url`, `binary` (no JSON tags: Go field names), `shell_command` (`command`,`output`,`exit_code`) |
| file history | `files(session_id,path,content,version)` full content per edit version; `read_files(session_id,path,read_at)` |

[src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/db/models.go#L11-L65] [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/message/message.go#L613-L710] [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/message/content.go#L20-L145] [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/db/migrations/20250424200609_initial.sql] Timestamps: a 3rd-party connector says `created_at` ms; the source (insert SQL `strftime('%s','now')`, comment "one-second resolution") says seconds. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/db/sql/messages.sql#L22-L36]

**Format changes:** goose migrations 2025-04-24 initial; +`summary_message_id` (2025-05), created_at indexes (06), `provider` on messages (06), `is_summary_message` (08), `todos` (08), `read_files` (2026-01-27), `prism_*` message columns (2026-09-02), roles index (09-12), `sessions.channel` (09-21), `mcp_enabled/disabled_servers` (09-28). [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/db/migrations] Config moved in 2026 from `crush.json` to a Bash-DSL `crushrc` (JSON "deprecated but supported"). [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:README.md#L236-L332]
**Incremental-import notes:** open `mode=ro` (WAL; writers hold a 30 s busy timeout); detect change via `sessions.updated_at` (second resolution, tie-break by rowid) and `messages.updated_at`; assistant rows are updated in place while streaming (`UpdateMessage` rewrites `parts`). [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/db/sql/messages.sql#L38-L48] Enumerate DBs from `projects.json`, then verify each `data_dir/crush.db` exists.

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
- No encryption found; data dir created `0700`, files plain SQLite. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/db/connect.go#L118-L119] Official export: `crush session list|show <id>|last|rename|delete [--json]` (`show --json` returns metadata + messages + parts); `crush logs [--tail N --follow]`; `crush stats`. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/cmd/session.go#L34-L93]

### 5. Integration surfaces
- **MCP.** Config precedence (low to high): `/etc/crush/crush.json`, `~/.config/crush/crush.json`, `~/.config/crush/crushrc`, `~/.local/share/crush/crush.json`, then project walk-up `crush.json`, `.crush.json`, `crushrc`, `.crushrc` (project root bounded by git root). [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/config/load.go#L938-L965] JSON: `{"$schema":"https://charm.land/crush.json","mcp":{"recall":{"type":"stdio","command":"recall","args":["serve"],"env":{}}}}`; fields `type` (stdio/sse/http), `command`, `args`, `env`, `url`, `headers`, `disabled`, `disabled_tools`, `enabled_tools`, `timeout`, `oauth*`. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/config/config.go#L209-L221] crushrc: `mcp add recall --command recall --args serve` (`--type http --url ...`). [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:README.md#L416-L432] No `crush mcp add` subcommand found.
- **Global instructions.** `~/.config/crush/CRUSH.md` and `~/.config/AGENTS.md` (+ `options.global_context_paths`); project context defaults: `.github/copilot-instructions.md`, `.cursorrules`, `.cursor/rules/`, `CLAUDE.md`, `CLAUDE.local.md`, `GEMINI.md`, `crush.md`, `CRUSH.md` (+`.local`), `AGENTS.md`; `/init` writes AGENTS.md. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/config/config.go#L29-L45] [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:README.md#L534-L548]
- **Skills.** Agent Skills standard; global `$CRUSH_SKILLS_DIR`, `~/.config/agents/skills`, `~/.config/crush/skills`, `~/.agents/skills`, `~/.claude/skills`, `options.skills_paths`; project `.agents/skills`, `.crush/skills`, `.claude/skills`, `.cursor/skills`. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:README.md#L602-L627]
- **Hooks.** Only `PreToolUse` exists (docs: "plans to support the full gamut"); Claude Code-compatible; `hooks.PreToolUse[]` in crush.json = `{matcher (regex on tool name), command, timeout (s, default 30)}`; stdin `{event, session_id, cwd, tool_name, tool_input}`; env `CRUSH_EVENT`, `CRUSH_TOOL_NAME`, `CRUSH_SESSION_ID`, `CRUSH_CWD`, `CRUSH_PROJECT_DIR`, `CRUSH_TOOL_INPUT_COMMAND`, `CRUSH_TOOL_INPUT_FILE_PATH`; exit 2 = deny, 49 = halt turn; stdout JSON `{version, decision, halt, reason, context, updated_input}`, where `context` (string or array) is appended to what the model sees for that tool call. Top-level agent only. No session-start, prompt-submit or session-end event. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:docs/hooks/README.md#L10-L24] [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/skills/builtin/crush-hooks/SKILL.md] [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/hooks/hooks.go#L13-L22]
- **Plugins / extensions.** None found beyond MCP, skills, hooks and custom commands; MCP `channel_enabled`/`channel_reply` fields and `sessions.channel` exist but were not investigated. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/config/config.go#L209-L221]
- **Slash commands.** Markdown files in `~/.config/crush/commands/`, `~/.crush/commands/` (prefix `user:`) and `<DataDirectory>/commands/` (prefix `project:`); MCP prompts and user-invocable skills also appear in the palette. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/commands/commands.go#L120-L135]
- **Headless.** `crush run [prompt...] [-q] [-v] [-m model] [--small-model m] [--reasoning-effort e] [-s <session-id>] [-C/--continue]`; `crush server` runs a workspace server. [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/cmd/run.go#L33-L76] [src: charmbracelet/crush@65865e0:internal/cmd/run.go#L168-L174]

**Best recall injection point:** no context-injecting session-start or prompt hook exists; put the memory preamble in `~/.config/crush/CRUSH.md` (always in the system prompt) or `~/.config/AGENTS.md`, and register MCP in `~/.config/crush/crush.json` (works whether or not the user adopts crushrc). A `PreToolUse` hook can only add per-tool-call `context`.
**Confidence / unknowns:** source-verified at `65865e0` but nothing observable locally; unknown: hooks declaration inside crushrc, MCP channels, DB location for `crush server` meta-workspace (`~/.local/share/crush`), `projects.json` freshness for deleted projects.

## Aider (Aider-AI/aider)


**Version verified:** aider-chat 0.86.2 (PyPI, 2026-02-12) [doc: https://pypi.org/project/aider-chat/]; GitHub `main` @5dc9490 dated 2026-05-22, newest tag v0.86.x, no later release [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:HISTORY.md#L1-L30]. Project is in slow maintenance.

### 1. Detection
- CLI only: no macOS app, no CFBundleIdentifier. Console script `aider` from dist `aider-chat` [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:pyproject.toml#L3-L27]. Installers: `aider-install` / `curl -LsSf https://aider.chat/install.sh | sh` (uv-managed env), pip, pipx, Homebrew formula `aider` [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/install.html]. Typical bin `~/.local/bin/aider` [inferred: uv tool default].
- Per-project marker files: `.aider.chat.history.md`, `.aider.input.history`, `.aider.llm.history` (only if flag set), `.aider.conf.yml`, `.aider.model.settings.yml`, `.aider.model.metadata.json`, `.aider.tags.cache.v3/` or `v4/` [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:aider/args.py#L269-L301; aider/repomap.py#L35-L43; aider/main.py#L335-L338,L390-L399].
- Global dir `~/.aider/` holds only `analytics.json`, `caches/`, `oauth-keys.env`; no history and no project list [src: aider/analytics.py#L139; aider/models.py#L169; aider/versioncheck.py#L12; aider/main.py#L370 (all @5dc9490)]. User config: `~/.aider.conf.yml`, `~/.env` [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/config/aider_conf.html].
- Env: every option has `AIDER_<OPTION>` (configargparse auto prefix), e.g. `AIDER_CHAT_HISTORY_FILE`, `AIDER_INPUT_HISTORY_FILE`, `AIDER_LLM_HISTORY_FILE`, `AIDER_RESTORE_CHAT_HISTORY`, `AIDER_READ`, `AIDER_MESSAGE` [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:aider/args.py#L35-L41; doc: https://aider.chat/docs/config/options.html]. `.env` is read from home, git root, cwd and `--env-file` [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/config/dotenv.html]. Nothing but `HOME` relocates `~/.aider` (hard-coded `Path.home()`) [src: aider/models.py#L169].

### 2. History location
- Default on every OS: `<git root>/.aider.chat.history.md` and `<git root>/.aider.input.history`; outside a git repo, the cwd [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:aider/args.py#L269-L288]. Relocate with `--chat-history-file` / `--input-history-file` / `--llm-history-file`, their `AIDER_*` env vars, or YAML keys [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/config/options.html].
- Aider offers to append `.aider*` to the repo `.gitignore`, so these files are normally untracked [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:aider/main.py#L155-L181].
- No central index exists, so discovery needs a project scan or a known-roots list. cass walks cwd and `$HOME` to depth 5 for the file name [3p: Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/aider.rs#L35-L75]. For recall: seed roots from repos already known via other agents, user-configured roots, and a bounded `$HOME` walk; also honour `AIDER_CHAT_HISTORY_FILE` / `.aider.conf.yml` overrides [inferred].

### 3. Storage format
- `.aider.chat.history.md`: UTF-8 markdown, opened in append mode per write, undecodable bytes dropped (`errors="ignore"`) [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:aider/io.py#L1117-L1137]. One file accumulates many sessions. Line grammar, confirmed by aider's own parser (`--restore-chat-history`): `# ` header lines skipped; `> ` = tool/system; `#### ` = user; everything else = assistant [src: aider/utils.py#L148-L190; aider/coders/base_coder.py#L519-L522 (both @5dc9490)].
- CAUTION: cass treats `>` lines as USER turns, the opposite of aider's own format [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/aider.rs#L1-L8,L101-L130]; follow the vendor source above.

| Concept | Field / marker |
|---|---|
| session id | none; synthesize from `<abs path>` + ordinal/byte offset of the `# aider chat started at` header [inferred] |
| title | none (derive from first `####` line) [inferred] |
| project/cwd | parent dir of the file (git root or launch cwd); banner line `> Git repo: <dir> with N files` [src: aider/args.py#L271-L276; aider/coders/base_coder.py#L255 (@5dc9490)] |
| created | header `# aider chat started at YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS`, local time, no timezone, written at every aider start [src: aider/io.py#L335-L336] |
| updated | file mtime only; no per-message timestamps [src: aider/io.py#L1117-L1137] |
| user text | `#### <line>` (multi-line prompts: each line prefixed, joined with two trailing spaces); slash commands appear too, e.g. `#### /add x` [src: aider/io.py#L775-L790] |
| assistant text | unprefixed markdown, `content.strip()` plus blank line; edit blocks (SEARCH/REPLACE or diff) stay inline text [src: aider/io.py#L793-L795] |
| tool calls | none structured; shell/lint/test output surfaces only as `> ` lines [src: aider/io.py#L995-L1000] |
| tool results | `> ` lines (applied edits, commit lines, `/run` output) |
| other | `> Aider v<ver>`, `> Main model: <name>`, `> Tokens: ... Cost: ...` lines carry version, model, usage [src: aider/coders/base_coder.py#L209,L2023,L2059] |

- `.aider.input.history`: prompt_toolkit `FileHistory`; each entry is `\n# <datetime.now()>\n` followed by `+<line>` per input line (user inputs only; the only per-prompt timestamps, joinable to `####` text) [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:aider/io.py#L355-L356,L736-L752; prompt-toolkit/python-prompt-toolkit@583b341:src/prompt_toolkit/history.py#L262-L305].
- `.aider.llm.history` (opt-in): blocks `TO LLM <iso ts>` + formatted messages and `LLM RESPONSE <iso ts>` + `ASSISTANT ...` [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:aider/io.py#L754-L765; aider/coders/base_coder.py#L1793,L1822-L1826]. `--analytics-log FILE` writes analytics events, not transcripts [src: aider/args.py#L575-L578; content unverified].
- Format changes across versions: none documented; HISTORY.md shows only fixes (auto-create parent dirs for history files, encoding) [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:HISTORY.md#L72-L79].
- Incremental-import notes: append-only, no locks, no WAL. Track `(path, size, mtime, hash of first 4 KB)`; resume from the stored offset and re-anchor on the next `# aider chat started at` header; if size shrinks or the head hash changes, re-import the file. Files can reach tens of MB over years, so cap or stream-parse (cass caps at 100 MB [3p: franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e:src/connectors/aider.rs#L80-L92]). Users delete these files freely.

### 4. Encryption / availability / export
Plaintext, local, never synced; no official export (the history file is the record). `/copy`, `/copy-context` only go to the clipboard [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/usage/commands.html]. `--restore-chat-history` re-reads the markdown into context (default off) [src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:aider/args.py#L289-L294].

### 5. Integration surfaces
- MCP: none upstream. No MCP code at main; PRs #3672 and #3937 closed unmerged, #5539 open, issue #3314 "MCP SUPPORT" open [src: https://github.com/Aider-AI/aider/issues/3314; .../pull/3937; .../pull/3672; .../pull/5539 (GitHub API merged=false)]. Fork `cecli-dev/cecli` (ex aider-ce) adds `--mcp-servers`, uses `.cecli.conf.yml` and `.cecli/chat.history` instead [3p: cecli-dev/cecli@41be4bc:cecli/args.py#L495-L508,L661-L664]; its format is NOT covered here.
- Global instructions: no AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md auto-load found. Convention: put rules in `CONVENTIONS.md` and load read-only via `aider --read CONVENTIONS.md` or `read: CONVENTIONS.md` in `.aider.conf.yml` (cache-friendly) [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/usage/conventions.html]. Config search order home, git root, cwd; later wins; `--config` loads only one file [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/config/aider_conf.html; src: Aider-AI/aider@5dc9490:aider/main.py#L464-L477].
- Skills: none. Plugins: none. Custom slash commands: none (built-ins only; `/load <file>` / `--load` replays commands at startup) [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/usage/commands.html; src: aider/args.py#L773-L777].
- Hooks: none (PRs #4485/#4488 "pre/post handlers" closed unmerged). Nearest: `--lint-cmd`, `--test-cmd` + `--auto-test` (non-zero exit output is fed back to the model), `--notifications-command` (runs when waiting for input; no payload) [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/usage/lint-test.html; https://aider.chat/docs/usage/notifications.html; src: aider/args.py#L534-L560,L825-L842]. No session-start or prompt-submit hook, no context injection.
- Headless: `--message/-m "<text>"`, `--message-file/-f <file>` (one reply, then exit), `--yes-always`, `--no-stream`; unsupported Python API `Coder.create(...).run()` [doc: https://aider.chat/docs/scripting.html; src: aider/args.py#L639-L656].

**Best recall injection point:** a recall-generated markdown file referenced by `read:` in `~/.aider.conf.yml` (loaded read-only at every launch); refresh it before launch (wrapper) since there is no hook. No MCP path exists [inferred].
**Confidence / unknowns:** High on file format and flags (read from aider's own source); unknown how to enumerate projects without a scan, and whether MCP lands upstream.

---

# Appendix: third-party registries used as cross-checks

These are community projects that already encode per-agent paths. They were used to find the right vendor sources and to cross-check, never as sole proof (any claim in an agent section that rests only on them is tagged `[3p]`). Snapshot 2026-10-06.

## A. Which third-party parsers cover which agents

Sources: franken_agent_detection (the crate that implements cass's connectors; `Dicklesworthstone/franken_agent_detection@7fb5c0e`, `src/connectors/<name>.rs`; cass itself is `Dicklesworthstone/coding_agent_session_search@76bc79b` and only wraps these), SpecStory CLI (`specstoryai/getspecstory@c80e93a`, `specstory-cli/pkg/providers/<name>`), agent-sessions (`jazzyalex/agent-sessions@6fa9a73`, `AgentSessions/Services/<Name>Session{Discovery,Indexer,Parser}.swift` and README support table).

| Agent | cass / franken_agent_detection connector | SpecStory provider | agent-sessions |
|---|---|---|---|
| Claude Code | claude_code | claudecode | Claude |
| Claude desktop (Code tab / Cowork) | none separate (their transcripts are Claude Code JSONL; see `entrypoint`) | none | none |
| Codex | codex (+ codex/reader) | codexcli | Codex |
| ChatGPT desktop / export | chatgpt | none | none |
| Cursor | cursor | cursorcli, cursoride | Cursor |
| opencode | opencode | opencode | OpenCode |
| T3 Code | none | none | none |
| Antigravity | antigravity | antigravitycli | Antigravity |
| Gemini CLI | gemini | geminicli | none in README table |
| Windsurf | none (devin = Devin CLI only) | none | Devin (CLI) |
| VS Code Copilot Chat | copilot, copilot_vscode | copilotide, vscode | none (README lists Copilot CLI only) |
| Copilot CLI | copilot_cli | none | Copilot |
| Cline / Roo Code | cline (scans `saoudrizwan.claude-dev` and `rooveterinaryinc.roo-cline`) | none | Cline (CLI and Desktop) |
| Kilo Code, Continue | none | none | none |
| Zed, Warp | none | none | none |
| Kiro | kiro (Kiro CLI JSONL; IDE out of scope) | none | none |
| Goose | goose | none | none |
| Amp | amp | none | none |
| Factory Droid | factory | droidcli | Droid (legacy sessions) |
| Pi | pi_agent, pi_wire (+ omp for Oh My Pi) | piagent | Pi |
| Crush | crush | none | none |
| Aider | aider | none | none |

## B. Where skills installers write (project dir / user dir)

Source: `vercel-labs/skills@14cf84a`, `src/agents.ts` (the registry behind `npx skills`; `.agents/skills` is the cross-client convention documented at https://agentskills.io/client-implementation/adding-skills-support). It records where an installer WRITES, which for most agents is also where they read; vendor docs in each section are authoritative.

| Agent | Project skills dir | User skills dir |
|---|---|---|
| Claude Code | `.claude/skills` | `$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/skills` (default `~/.claude/skills`) |
| Codex | `.agents/skills` | `$CODEX_HOME/skills` (default `~/.codex/skills`) |
| Cursor | `.agents/skills` | `~/.cursor/skills` |
| opencode | `.agents/skills` | `~/.config/opencode/skills` |
| Antigravity (IDE / `agy` CLI) | `.agents/skills` | `~/.gemini/antigravity/skills` / `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/skills` |
| Gemini CLI | `.agents/skills` | `~/.gemini/skills` |
| Windsurf | `.windsurf/skills` | `~/.codeium/windsurf/skills` |
| GitHub Copilot | `.agents/skills` | `~/.copilot/skills` |
| Cline | `.agents/skills` | `~/.agents/skills` |
| Roo Code | `.roo/skills` | `~/.roo/skills` |
| Kilo Code | `.agents/skills` | `~/.kilo/skills` |
| Continue | `.continue/skills` | `~/.continue/skills` |
| Zed | `.agents/skills` | `~/.agents/skills` |
| Goose | `.goose/skills` | `~/.config/goose/skills` |
| Warp | `.agents/skills` | `~/.agents/skills` |
| Kiro CLI | `.kiro/skills` | `~/.kiro/skills` |
| Amp | `.agents/skills` | `~/.config/agents/skills` |
| Factory Droid | `.agents/skills` | `~/.factory/skills` |
| Pi | `.agents/skills` | `~/.agents/skills` |
| Crush | `.crush/skills` | `~/.config/crush/skills` |
| Aider, Claude desktop, ChatGPT, T3 Code | not in the registry | not in the registry |

A common setup makes `~/.agents/skills` the canonical store (installed by the `skills` CLI, with `~/.agents/.skill-lock.json`), and `~/.claude/skills`, `~/.gemini/skills`, `~/.codex/skills`, `~/.cursor/skills`, `~/.config/opencode/skills` and `~/.codeium/windsurf/skills` are directories of symlinks into it [observed]. A single skill installed under `~/.agents/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` is therefore visible to every agent that scans that directory natively (see per-agent sections), and per-agent symlinks cover the rest.

## C. Where MCP installers write

Sources: `neon-solutions/add-mcp@ad92a01` (README table of agent project/global paths) and `supermemoryai/install-mcp` (`src/client-config.ts`). Cross-check only; the per-agent sections give vendor-documented paths and flag disagreements.

| Agent | Global MCP config path (macOS) | Project path |
|---|---|---|
| Claude Code | `~/.claude.json` | `.mcp.json` |
| Claude desktop | `~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude_desktop_config.json` | none |
| Codex | `~/.codex/config.toml` | `.codex/config.toml` |
| Cursor | `~/.cursor/mcp.json` | `.cursor/mcp.json` |
| Antigravity | `~/.gemini/config/mcp_config.json` (add-mcp says shared by IDE and CLI; an older install may have `~/.gemini/antigravity/mcp_config.json`) | none |
| Gemini CLI | `~/.gemini/settings.json` | `.gemini/settings.json` |
| Windsurf | `~/.codeium/windsurf/mcp_config.json` | none |
| VS Code (Copilot) | `~/Library/Application Support/Code/User/mcp.json` | `.vscode/mcp.json` |
| Copilot CLI | `$COPILOT_HOME/mcp-config.json` (default `~/.copilot/mcp-config.json`) | `.mcp.json` or `.github/mcp.json` |
| Cline | VS Code `globalStorage/saoudrizwan.claude-dev/settings/cline_mcp_settings.json`; CLI `~/.cline/data/settings/cline_mcp_settings.json` | none |
| Roo Code | VS Code `globalStorage/rooveterinaryinc.roo-cline/settings/mcp_settings.json` (install-mcp) | `.roo/mcp.json` |
| Kilo Code | `~/.config/kilo/kilo.json` (or `.jsonc`) | `kilo.json`, `.kilo/`, `.kilocode/` |
| Zed | `~/.config/zed/settings.json` (install-mcp) vs `~/Library/Application Support/Zed/settings.json` (add-mcp): DISAGREE, see Zed section | `.zed/settings.json` |
| Goose | `~/.config/goose/config.yaml` | `.goose/config.yaml` |
| Kiro | `~/.kiro/settings/mcp.json` (shared by IDE and CLI) | `.kiro/settings/mcp.json` |
| opencode | `~/.config/opencode/opencode.jsonc` or `.json` | `opencode.jsonc` |
| Pi | `$PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR/mcp.json` (default `~/.pi/agent/mcp.json`) | `.pi/mcp.json` |
| Factory Droid | `~/.factory/mcp.json` (install-mcp) | `.factory/mcp.json` |
| Aider | `~/.aider/mcp.yml` (install-mcp; community convention, Aider itself has no native MCP client unless the Aider section says otherwise) | `.aider.mcp.yml` |
| Amp, Warp, Continue, Crush, ChatGPT desktop, T3 Code | not in these registries | not in these registries |

## D. Method and limitations

- Sources, in order of trust: vendor documentation and changelogs fetched on 2026-10-06 (many vendors publish raw Markdown by appending `.md`); the agents' own source on GitHub, pinned to commit SHAs; third-party parsers and registries (Appendix A-C) only as pointers, always tagged `[3p]`; read-only, structure-only inspection of one macOS workstation (directory names, file types and sizes, SQLite DDL and counts, JSON key names and value types, and entropy/printable-ratio statistics for opaque files; no conversation text, titles, prompts, project paths, account ids or secrets were read into the document, and no Keychain or credential file was opened).
- Research was done as parallel per-agent passes; the lead re-verified high-impact claims against primary sources (Claude Code and Codex hooks, Cursor hooks and third-party hook import, Gemini CLI storage and hooks, VS Code and Copilot CLI hooks, Factory, Kiro, Crush, Amp, Goose, Zed and Warp paths, Windsurf/Devin and Codex/ChatGPT merger notes, Roo Code and Continue status) and censused local Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, opencode and Claude desktop stores. Counts quoted from that workstation describe one machine, not a population.
- Closed-source agents (Cursor, Claude desktop, ChatGPT/Codex desktop shell, Amp, Antigravity, Warp's server side, Kiro, Droid) were described from docs, shipped bundles read as strings, vendor SDKs and third-party parsers; where a layout is third-party-only the section says so and the format has usually changed several times in 12 months.
- Hook semantics were read from docs and source, not exercised: nothing was installed, no agent was run beyond `--version`/`--help` of binaries already present, and no hook was fired. Behaviour such as Cursor's `sessionStart` race comes from vendor docs plus community reports.
- macOS TCC prevented reading Warp's App Group container; that was recorded, not bypassed.

## E. Open questions (not established in this research)

- Claude Code: whether `~/.claude.json` relocates under `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`; the transcript schema is officially unstable. Claude desktop: format of the Cowork full-history download; whether `claude_desktop_config.json` servers reach Cowork; custom commands in the Code tab.
- Codex/ChatGPT: `thread/list`/`thread/read` app-server method names; desktop-only `codex-dev.db` semantics (closed source); the cipher, key item name and release date of the ChatGPT Classic v3 `.data` format; post-merger Windows/Linux paths.
- Exports: neither OpenAI nor Anthropic documents the `conversations.json` schema, shard naming or the extra files; all of it is community-observed.
- Cursor: reliability of `sessionStart` context injection in 3.22.x; exactly what the third-party import toggle loads (MCP import not verified); `agentKv` blob format (opaque); behaviour of the newest CLI (2026.10.01).
- opencode/Kilo: v2 table contents come from source and a third-party doc because no v2-written database exists here; Kilo's old CLI 0.x task location; Windows data dir for opencode.
- Antigravity/Windsurf/Devin: `.db` DDL differences between IDE and `agy`; which data root the 2.0 app uses on a given build; `sessions.db` timestamp unit (seconds vs milliseconds) and macOS GUI data root; which `mcp_config.json` path current builds read; the v1 `.pb` cipher key.
- Copilot: remote-window (SSH/WSL/container) storage location; whether VS Code deletes files for sessions trimmed from its 400-entry index; Copilot CLI `userPromptSubmitted` `additionalContext` (docs and changelog disagree), `session-store.db` schema and `-p --output-format json` record shape.
- Cline/Roo/Continue: legacy-to-SDK session id mapping and which VS Code windows run the SDK bundle; the Roo release that introduced `history_item.json`; whether `cn` loads `.continue/mcpServers/` and skills.
- Zed: `threads.db` journal mode and leftover `conversations/*.zed`; live ACP-agent behaviour. Warp: real-row verification (TCC), `oz agent run` persistence, Markdown export shape.
- Kiro: the CLI version that began writing JSONL, the IDE 1.x event schema, whether CLI V3 still writes SQLite. Amp: exact `amp threads export` payload and any remaining local cache. Droid: Factory App session store and the real `transcript_path`. Goose: Desktop `CFBundleIdentifier` and the conflicting macOS default-root statements in its docs. Pi: whether agent-dir `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md` are both loaded. Crush: hooks inside `crushrc`. Aider: project enumeration without a scan.
